### [1] [GEM: Geometric Entropy Mixing for Optimal LLM Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26121)
*Yue Min,Ziyun Qiao,Ruining Chen,Yujun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEM框架，通过超球面上的几何熵变分问题优化LLM预训练数据组合，解决了传统分类方法的局限性，在1.1B模型上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练效果越来越依赖数据组合而非单纯规模，但最优混合策略受限于分类缺陷：人类分类法存在本体论错位，欧氏聚类无法解决嵌入各向异性问题。

Method: 提出GEM（几何熵混合）框架，将数据管理重构为带混合平衡正则项的超球面变分问题；通过解耦生成先验并使用可证明的MM（Minorize-Maximize）算法优化目标，有效对抗簇坍缩以发现欧氏启发式方法无法识别的平衡语义结构；采用教师-学生蒸馏将几何保真度扩展到网络规模语料库，并引入几何影响分数（GIS）用于可解释的分类生成。

Result: 在1.1B参数模型上的实验表明，GEM集成到DoReMi和RegMix等混合策略中时建立了新的SOTA，平均下游准确率提升达1.2%，为可预测的数据混合提供了稳健的坐标系统。

Conclusion: GEM框架成功解决了数据组合优化中的分类缺陷问题，通过几何方法实现了平衡语义结构的发现，为LLM预训练提供了更优的数据混合策略和可解释的分类体系。

Abstract: LLM pre-training efficacy increasingly depends on data composition rather than sheer volume. Yet, optimal mixing is hindered by categorization flaws: human taxonomies suffer from ontological misalignment, and Euclidean clustering fails to address embedding anisotropy. We introduce GEM (Geometric Entropy Mixing), a framework reformulating data curation as a variational problem on the hypersphere augmented with a mixing-balance regularizer. By decoupling the generative prior and optimizing the objective via a provable MM (Minorize-Maximize) algorithm, GEM effectively counteracts the cluster collapse to discover balanced semantic structures invisible to Euclidean heuristics. We employ teacher-student distillation to scale this geometric fidelity to web-scale corpora and introduce the Geometric Influence Score (GIS) for interpretable taxonomy generation. Experiments with 1.1B-parameter models demonstrate that GEM establishes a new state-of-the-art when integrated into mixing strategies like DoReMi and RegMix, improving average downstream accuracy by up to 1.2% and offering a robust coordinate system for predictable data mixing.

</details>


### [2] [The Constraint Tax: Measuring Validity-Correctness Tradeoffs in Structured Outputs for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26128)
*Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了小型语言模型（<3B）在强制结构化输出约束时存在"约束税"现象：硬性JSON/工具调用schema约束虽能提升格式正确率至100%，但会导致任务准确率从19.7%降至11.0%，错误但格式正确的输出率从49.5%升至88.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM系统日益需要机器可读的结构化输出（JSON、类型化追踪、正则约束字段、工具调用schema）。业界普遍假设硬性输出约束仅提升可靠性而不改变底层答案质量，但这一假设针对小型模型（<3B）的安全性尚未得到验证。小型模型在隐私、延迟和通用硬件方面具有优势，但容量有限，在满足schema约束的同时解决任务的能力受限。

Method: 提出"约束税"(constraint tax)测量协议，用于在固定模型、固定任务分布和固定问题实例的条件下，分离结构化输出约束导致的答案损失和可执行准确率损失。在15,000次商品级GPU生成实验中，使用Qwen2.5-0.5B、Qwen2.5-1.5B和SmolLM2-1.7B三种小型模型，对比提示式JSON与硬性schema解码两种模式。实验包括答案级schema任务和确定性日历工具调用任务。

Result: 硬性答案级schema解码将schema有效率从61.5%提升至100.0%，但答案准确率从19.7%降至11.0%，错误但格式正确的输出率从49.5%增至88.9%。在日历工具调用任务中，Qwen2.5-1.5B在提示式JSON模式下达到91.5%可执行准确率，但在相同硬性工具调用schema下仅为48.0%，且两种模式均为100% schema有效。3B边界模型仍支付直接schema税。延迟打包策略（先自由推理后约束）展现出建设性设计模式价值。

Conclusion: 硬性输出约束不改变底层答案的假设对小型模型不安全，约束会引发语义错误而非结构性错误。生产系统应分别报告schema有效率、答案准确率、可执行准确率和错误但格式正确率四个指标。建议采用"先自由推理、后延迟约束"的设计模式以缓解约束税问题。

Abstract: Production LLM systems increasingly require machine-readable outputs: JSON objects, typed traces, regex-constrained fields, and tool-call schemas. This paper targets on-device and low-cost small language model (SLM) deployments, where sub-3B models are attractive for privacy, latency, and commodity hardware but have limited capacity to satisfy schemas while solving tasks. The usual engineering assumption is that hard output constraints improve reliability without changing the underlying answer. We show that this assumption is unsafe for small models. We introduce \emph{constraint tax}, a measurement protocol for isolating the answer and executable-accuracy loss caused by structured-output constraints at fixed model, fixed task distribution, and fixed problem instances. Across 15,000 commodity-GPU generations with Qwen2.5-0.5B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and SmolLM2-1.7B, hard answer-only schema decoding raises schema validity from 61.5\% to 100.0\%, but lowers answer accuracy from 19.7\% to 11.0\% and increases wrong-valid-schema outputs from 49.5\% to 88.9\%. The strongest industry analogue is a deterministic calendar tool-call task: Qwen2.5-1.5B achieves 91.5\% executable accuracy with prompt-only JSON but only 48.0\% under the same hard tool-call schema, while both modes are 100.0\% schema-valid. The error is semantic, not structural. We also show that the 3B boundary still pays a direct-schema tax and that delayed packaging supports a constructive design pattern: reason free, constrain late. The practical conclusion is direct: production systems should report schema validity, answer accuracy, executable accuracy, and wrong-valid-schema rate separately.

</details>


### [3] [AirCast-SR: A Foundation Model for Kilometer-Scale Atmospheric Super-Resolution via Latent Consistency Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26130)
*Somnath Luitel,Manmeet Singh,Joshua Durkee,Abdullah Al Fahad,Naveen Sudharsan,Prabhjot Singh,Cenlin He,Harsh Kamath,Zong-Liang Yang,Krishnagopal Halder,Sandeep Juneja,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Saptarishi Dhanuka,Amit Kumar Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AirCast-SR（正文中称为EarthMind-SR），一个用于大气超分辨率的基础模型，能够将全球AI天气预报从0.25度（约28公里）降尺度至1公里水平分辨率，生成8个耦合地表变量的67小时预报，并在不同季节和地区验证了其准确性和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报（NWP）模型在公里尺度上的计算成本过高，限制了能源、农业和灾害管理等需要精细时空细节的应用对预报的获取。因此需要一种能够经济高效地生成高分辨率天气预报的方法。

Method: 提出了EarthMind-SR模型，采用在潜在一致性模型（LCM）扩散框架内条件化的三维U-Net架构。模型基于美国本土（CONUS）的补丁样本进行训练，使用GraphCast预报作为输入，NOAA的AORC数据集作为目标，将0.25度分辨率的全球AI天气预报降尺度至1公里分辨率。

Result: 模型在所有变量和预报时效上实现了接近零偏差；径向功率谱密度分析显示模型在10-100公里波长范围内保留了精细尺度大气结构；在美国本土的冬、夏、春三季案例研究中得到验证，并在印度和德国展示了零样本全球迁移能力，无需重新训练或微调即可使用独立地面观测站数据进行验证。

Conclusion: 作为一个开放权重的基础模型，EarthMind-SR建立了公里尺度AI天气预报的新范式，为区域微调、知识蒸馏以及气候服务和灾害预报等下游应用提供了平台。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction at kilometer scales remains computationally prohibitive for traditional numerical weather prediction (NWP) models, limiting forecast access for applications in energy, agriculture, and disaster management that require fine-grained spatiotemporal detail. Here we introduce AirCast-SR, a foundation model for atmospheric super-resolution that downscales global AI weather forecasts from 0.25 degree (~28 km) to 1 km horizontal resolution at hourly temporal resolution, producing 67-hour forecasts of eight coupled surface variables simultaneously. EarthMind-SR employs a three-dimensional U-Net conditioned within a Latent Consistency Model (LCM) diffusion framework, trained on patch-based samples over the contiguous United States (CONUS) using GraphCast forecasts as input and NOAA's Analysis of Record for Calibration (AORC) as the target. The model achieves near-zero bias across all variables and lead times, and its radial power spectral density analysis demonstrates preservation of fine-scale atmospheric structure at wavelengths of 10 km to 100 km where coarser models lose spectral power. We validate EarthMind-SR across three CONUS case studies spanning winter, summer, and spring seasons, and demonstrate zero-shot global transferability over India and Germany using independent surface station observations without any retraining or fine-tuning. As an open-weights foundation model, EarthMind-SR establishes a new paradigm for kilometer-scale AI weather prediction and provides a platform for regional fine-tuning, distillation, and downstream applications in climate services and hazard forecasting.

</details>


### [4] [SilIF: Silhouette-Augmented Isolation Forest for Unsupervised Transaction Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26135)
*Venkatakrishnan Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SilIF方法，通过在隔离森林(Isolation Forest)中添加基于轮廓系数的评分层来增强异常检测性能，在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测数据集上相比原始IF提升+0.0080 AUC-PR，但在Sparkov合成数据集上无改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督异常检测在标签稀缺的交易欺诈检测中应用广泛，隔离森林因其可扩展性和易部署性成为最流行的经典方法之一。作者希望在此基础上进一步提升性能。

Method: 提出SilIF方法：(1)提取每棵树的路径长度向量作为"指纹"特征；(2)将这些特征聚类成结构组；(3)计算轮廓系数得分，衡量点与所属组的拟合程度；(4)通过超参数alpha将轮廓信号与基础IF分数结合。

Result: 在IEEE-CIS欺诈检测基准数据集(~59万交易，3.5%欺诈率)上，alpha=1.0时平均提升+0.0080 AUC-PR，五次实验全部胜出(配对t检验p=0.046)；在Sparkov合成信用卡数据集上轮廓增强未带来改进。

Conclusion: SilIF是一种可调节、易部署的隔离森林增强方法，诚实地报告了其有效和无效的条件，为实践者提供了改进传统异常检测方法的可行路径。

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used in transaction fraud detection where labels are scarce. Isolation Forest (IF) is among the most popular classical methods due to its scalability and ease of deployment. We propose SilIF, an augmentation of Isolation Forest that adds a silhouette-based scoring layer computed in a representation space induced by the trees of the forest. For each point, we extract a vector of per-tree path lengths, cluster these "fingerprints" into structural groups, and compute a silhouette score that measures how well the point fits its assigned group versus the nearest alternative. The silhouette signal is combined with the base IF score via a single hyperparameter alpha. On the IEEE-CIS Fraud Detection benchmark (~590K transactions, 3.5% fraud), SilIF with alpha=1.0 improves over plain Isolation Forest by +0.0080 AUC-PR on average across five seeds, with SilIF winning on all five seeds (paired t-test p=0.046). We also report results on a synthetic credit-card dataset (Sparkov) where the silhouette augmentation does not improve over plain IF, and we characterize the conditions that distinguish the two outcomes. The paper presents SilIF as a tunable, easy-to-deploy enhancement to Isolation Forest with honest reporting of when it helps and when it does not. Code at https://github.com/venkat15vk/silif-anomaly-detection.

</details>


### [5] [Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26147)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经贝叶斯序列路由（NBSR）框架，将神经推理建模为层次化有向无环图（DAG）上的主动证据累积过程，通过狄利克雷-类别共轭框架实现不确定性量化、早期退出和资源感知推理，在多个任务上实现了竞争性预测性能并提供了可解释的决策路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类决策具有序列性和不确定性感知特征，而标准神经网络依赖于静态、稠密的前向计算，缺乏对证据获取、不确定性演化以及计算何时停止的可见性。现有神经网络框架无法有效建模这种序列化的决策过程和不确定性管理机制。

Method: NBSR框架将神经推理建模为层次化有向无环图（DAG）上的主动证据累积过程。在狄利克雷-类别共轭框架下，神经专家查询持久化全局知识预言机以提取正证据向量，这些向量作为伪计数通过精确共轭加法更新狄利克雷信念状态。结合Gumbel-Softmax直通估计器，该方法实现了硬路径依赖路由，同时保留了用于端到端训练的代理梯度。狄利克雷精度和熵为不确定性量化、基于熵的早期退出、分布外弃权以及成本感知证据获取提供了机制。

Result: 理论分析表明，在严格正证据提取条件下，总狄利克雷精度沿任何有效轨迹单调递增，边际预测方差有界，形式化了序列"假设锐化"过程；在理想化容量和优化假设下，终端狄利克雷期望恢复贝叶斯最优条件分布。在视觉分类、结构化医疗诊断、语言建模、部分可观测控制和成本感知贝叶斯实验设计等多个任务上的实验表明，NBSR实现了竞争性预测性能，同时提供透明的路由轨迹、路径依赖证据归因、不确定性感知决策控制和资源理性推理。

Conclusion: NBSR为可解释、模块化和资源理性的智能体AI提供了数学上严谨的框架，成功将人类决策的序列性和不确定性感知特性融入神经网络推理过程，为构建更加透明和高效的AI系统开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Human decision-making is sequential and uncertainty-aware, yet standard neural networks often rely on static, dense forward computation with limited visibility into evidence acquisition, uncertainty evolution, or when computation should stop. We introduce \textbf{Neural Bayesian Sequential Routing (NBSR)}, a framework that models neural inference as active evidence accumulation over a hierarchical Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). Within a Dirichlet--Categorical conjugate framework, neural experts query a persistent global knowledge oracle to extract positive evidence vectors, which act as pseudo-counts and update a Dirichlet belief state by exact conjugate addition. Coupled with a Gumbel-Softmax Straight-Through estimator, this update enables hard, path-dependent routing while preserving surrogate gradients for end-to-end training. The resulting Dirichlet precision and entropy provide mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, entropy-based early exiting, OOD abstention, and cost-aware evidence acquisition. We prove that, under strictly positive evidence extraction, total Dirichlet precision increases monotonically along any valid trajectory and marginal predictive variance is bounded, formalizing sequential ``hypothesis sharpening''; under idealized capacity and optimization assumptions, the terminal Dirichlet expectation recovers the Bayes-optimal conditional distribution. Empirical evaluations across visual categorization, structured medical diagnosis, language modeling, partially observable control, and cost-aware Bayesian experimental design show that NBSR achieves competitive predictive performance while providing transparent routing traces, path-dependent evidence attribution, uncertainty-aware decision control, and resource-rational inference. Overall, NBSR offers a mathematically grounded framework for interpretable, modular, and resource-rational agentic AI.

</details>


### [6] [TSFMAudit: Data Contamination Auditing in Forecasting Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26161)
*Hongkai Li,Shifeng Xie,Lefei Shen,Zhuo Li,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaobin Zhang,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次研究时间序列基础模型预训练污染审计问题，提出TSFMAudit方法，基于探测适应动态实现高效污染检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型越来越多地在大规模语料库上预训练，评估数据集可能因包含在预训练数据中而产生过于乐观的性能估计，这种污染审计在时间序列领域具有挑战性。

Method: 提出TSFMAudit方法，基于探测适应动态，核心思想是污染表现为异常高效的适应：经过微调探测后，被污染的数据集往往展现出更快的损失下降和更小的骨干网络移动。

Result: 在6个TSFMs和187个数据集上评估TSFMAudit，使用文档化的训练来源证据作为监督，并与LLM文献中改进的10个竞争基线进行对比。

Conclusion: TSFMAudit为时间序列基础模型的预训练污染审计提供了首个系统性解决方案，通过适应动态有效识别被污染的数据集，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are increasingly pretrained on large corpora, raising concerns that evaluation datasets may have been exposed during pretraining and thus yield overly optimistic performance estimates. Auditing such contamination is challenging in time series because signals are continuous and heterogeneous, and often lack corpus documentation. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to study pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs. We formalize the problem of pretraining contamination auditing for TSFMs and propose TSFMAudit, a method based on probe adaptation dynamics. Our key intuition is that contamination manifests as unusually efficient adaptation: after a fine tuning probe, contaminated datasets tend to exhibit faster loss reduction with smaller backbone movement. We evaluate TSFMAudit on 6 TSFMs and 187 datasets using documented training source evidence as supervision, and compare against 10 competitive baselines adapted from the LLM literature.

</details>


### [7] [On the Push-Based Asynchronous Federated Learning: A Bias-Correction Aggregation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26162)
*Jiahui Bai,Hai Dong,A. K. Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架，通过质心表示空间耦合通信、聚合与正则化，解决了异步去中心化联邦学习中的通信开销大、聚合偏差和模型漂移问题，在数据异构场景下准确率提升高达6%，通信成本降低超过80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步去中心化联邦学习(ADFL)虽消除了中心协调与全局同步，适合大规模异构系统，但面临三大挑战：(1)频繁的点对点通信导致过高通信开销；(2)有向拓扑上的异步更新导致聚合偏差；(3)非独立同分布数据引发严重的模型漂移。这些因素共同制约了ADFL的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出PushCen-ADFL框架，核心创新包括：(1)在共享质心表示空间中耦合通信、聚合与局部稳定化，形成压缩与优化的闭环；(2)客户端交换质心形式消息，应用保持平均的push-sum混合来校正聚合偏差；(3)使用锚定于同一质心空间的轻量级质心正则化来缓解异构性和陈旧性下的漂移；(4)设计有界且发送方去重的缓冲区以提升不规则异步到达下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在视觉数据集上的实验表明，PushCen-ADFL在数据异构条件下准确率提升高达6%，每次推送的通信成本降低超过80%，实现了良好的准确率-通信权衡。

Conclusion: PushCen-ADFL成功解决了异步去中心化联邦学习中的关键瓶颈问题，通过质心空间的统一设计实现了通信效率与训练稳定性的协同优化，为大规模异构系统中的联邦学习应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Asynchronous decentralized federated learning (ADFL) eliminates central coordination and global synchronization, making it attractive for large-scale and heterogeneous systems. However, frequent peer-to-peer communication, asynchronous updates on directed topologies, and non-IID data jointly lead to excessive communication overhead, biased aggregation and severe model drift. We propose PushCen-ADFL, a communication-efficient ADFL framework that enables stable training under asymmetric communication and delayed client participation. PushCen-ADFL couples communication, aggregation, and local stabilization in a shared centroid representation space, forming a closed loop between compression and optimization. Clients exchange centroid-form messages, apply average-preserving push-sum mixing to correct aggregation bias, and use a lightweight centroid regularization anchored in the same centroid space to mitigate drift under heterogeneity and staleness. A bounded, sender-deduplicated buffer further improves robustness under irregular asynchronous arrivals. Experiments on vision datasets demonstrate that PushCen-ADFL improves accuracy under data heterogeneity by up to 6\% while reducing per-push communication cost by more than 80\%, achieving a favorable accuracy-communication trade-off.

</details>


### [8] [Planning Neural Dynamics with Lie Group Embedding through Supervised Projective Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26167)
*Tianwei Wang,Bryan Chen,Qian Zuo,Qiyue Xia,Xin Li,Wei Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出李群嵌入动力学神经网络(LieEDNN)，通过在李代数上引入伴随作用解决李群与神经网络加法运算不兼容的问题，实现了在SE(3)等李群上的稳定可学习动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络方法难以处理具有连续对称性的流形几何，李群(如SO(3)、SE(3))虽具有强大表示能力，但其非线性结构与神经网络加法范式不兼容，限制了在机器人、图形学和控制等领域的应用。

Method: 通过李群在李代数上的伴随作用诱导线性映射，转化为权重矩阵的分块结构，使加法运算可在李代数向量空间进行；将李代数和伴随作用参数化为线性变换，使架构与神经网络感知器对齐；在权重上施加分块流形约束，开发具有稳定性保证的时序神经网络动力学学习算法。

Result: 在SE(3)李群上完成实验验证，应用于伸缩机械臂场景，成功实现稳定可学习的动力学建模。

Conclusion: LieEDNN成功将李群内蕴表示嵌入神经网络动力学，为连续对称性流形上的工程问题提供了统一框架，证明了该方法在机器人控制等实际应用中的有效性。

Abstract: We propose Lie group embedded dynamical neural networks (LieEDNN) and the corresponding learning algorithms based on gradient descent and metric projection on smooth manifold, where we treat Lie group as an intrinsic representation for continuous symmetry of manifold geometry. Thereby we achieve learnable and stable dynamics on the underlying manifold for general Lie group, and we are able to utilize the powerful representation capability of Lie group such as SO(3) and SE(3) to solve real world engineering problems in areas such as robotics, graphics, and control. Two core challenges are: (i) General Lie groups are incompatible with addition arithmetic, which is necessary for neural network interactions. (ii) The dynamics evolve in the nonlinear representation space of special algebra rather than the normal Euclidean space, which violates the paradigm of common neural ODEs. To address these two challenges, we firstly introduce adjoint Lie group action on the Lie algebra, which induces a linear mapping and transfer to the block-wise structure of weight matrices, such that addition could operate on the Lie algebra as a vector space. Then we parameterize the Lie algebra and the adjoint action as linear transformation so that the architecture is aligned with neural network perceptrons. Explicitly, this embedding appears as block-wise manifold constraints on weights, and we develop algorithms to learn the equilibrium with stability guarantees of the temporal neural network dynamics. Experiments are implemented on a specific Lie group SE(3), with the application scenario of telescopic manipulators.

</details>


### [9] [When Rule Violations Are Rare: Chimera Training for Logical Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26171)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种神经规则评估器，用于检测违反语义约束的异常。通过将逻辑约束编译为DAG并学习特征感知的子树MLP门控，结合“chimera训练”（特征级反事实构建）解决训练数据覆盖不足的问题，在多个数据集上提升了规则级异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的异常往往不只是稀有输入，而是违反语义约束的情况（如对象共现、动作前提条件、事件时序关系等）。然而在训练时，真实的规则违反样本往往非常稀少甚至不存在，且同图像训练数据对有信息量的真值配置覆盖不足，还可能存在捷径解，这使得异常检测变得困难。

Method: 提出神经规则评估器，将每个约束编译为有向无环图(DAG)，为内部逻辑算子学习特征感知的子树MLP门控。每个门控将子特征和边级否定映射到父表示和规则满足概率，并通过真实概念标签的布尔传播获得中间监督。关键创新是引入chimera训练：在特征层面进行操作数级反事实构建，拼接来自不同样本的子树特征，继承硬真值标签，通过对继承标签应用节点的逻辑算子得到chimera目标，从而无需真实异常图像即可提供监督逻辑反例。

Result: 在CLEVRER、OpenImages和VidOR数据集上，该方法在规则级异常AUROC指标上优于独立事件和同图像语义训练基线，尤其对于组合式和关系型规则效果显著。方法能够同时输出标量异常分数和规则级归因。

Conclusion: 通过神经规则评估器和chimera训练的结合，有效解决了语义约束异常检测中训练数据覆盖不足和捷径解的问题，在多种规则类型上取得了更好的检测性能，为结构化场景下的异常检测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Many practical anomalies are not merely rare inputs, but violations of semantic constraints: objects co-occur in structured ways, actions imply preconditions, and events satisfy temporal or relational regularities. We study anomaly detection in this setting, where constraints are given as logical rules over learned visual concepts, but real rule violations are rare or absent during training. We propose a neural rule evaluator that compiles each constraint into a directed acyclic graph and learns feature-aware subtree MLP gates for its internal logical operators. Each gate maps child features and edge-level negations to a parent representation and a rule-satisfaction probability, with intermediate supervision obtained from exact Boolean propagation over ground-truth concept labels. The key difficulty is that same-image training data often provide insufficient coverage of informative truth configurations and also allow shortcut solutions. To address this, we introduce chimera training: an operand-level counterfactual construction at the feature level. Instead of mixing input images, we concatenate subtree features from different samples; each operand keeps the hard truth label of the sample it came from, and the chimera target is obtained by applying the node's logical operator to those inherited labels. This supplies supervised logical counterexamples without requiring real anomalous images. Across CLEVRER, OpenImages, and VidOR, the resulting evaluator improves rule-level anomaly AUROC over independent-events and same-image semantic-training baselines, especially for compositional and relational rules. The method yields both scalar anomaly scores and rule-level attributions.

</details>


### [10] [ARBITER: Reasoning Trajectory Basins and Majority Vote Failures in Test-Time Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26172)
*Meng Cai,Lars Kulik,Farhana Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型在测试时采样中存在“推理吸引盆”现象，导致多数投票可能选出稳定但错误的答案。提出ARBITER方法，通过利用模型自身的采样输出和隐藏状态添加保守证据，在不引入外部信息的情况下有效纠正错误多数，在多个模型和数据集上实现一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在测试时采样中，语言模型生成的多个推理轨迹并非独立，而是聚集到少数几个推理吸引盆中。多数投票倾向于选择最稳定的吸引盆而非最准确的，导致“错误多数”失败——正确答案存在但被错误答案票数压倒。作者旨在解决这一问题，从样本池本身恢复被错误投票掩盖的正确答案。

Method: 提出ARBITER——一种模型无关的方法，仅利用基础模型自身的采样输出、隐藏状态和派生证据来建模吸引盆之间的交互。包含两个变体：(1) ARBITER-Δ：无参数形式，在多数共识基础上添加同模型证据；(2) ARBITER-Enc：在此基础上利用完整解决方案的隐藏状态有界残差信号进行增强。采用保守的加性证据策略而非直接纠正策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B GSM8K上，K=24样本共识约94%，同池top-2神谕约96%，ARBITER零外部信息恢复部分差距。在Llama-3.1-8B MMLU-HS-Math上，准确率从约78%提升至约82%，恢复约22%的神谕空间。跨越三个模型家族和三个数学基准，实现一致增益，无净负面案例。

Conclusion: 错误多数失败中的神谕空间可以从样本池本身部分恢复。ARBITER通过保守证据增强成功识别并纠正被稳定但错误吸引盆掩盖的正确答案，证明了模型内部蕴含的自我纠错潜力。该方法模型无关、无需外部信息，在实际应用中具有实用价值。

Abstract: When language models use test-time sampling, they generate multiple reasoning trajectories and select an answer by majority vote. We show that these trajectories are not independent: for a given question, they concentrate into a small number of clusters, or reasoning basins, each defined by a normalized final answer and the solutions that reach it. A majority vote therefore selects the most stable basin rather than the most accurate one, which creates wrong-majority failures where the correct answer is present but outvoted. We introduce ARBITER, a model-agnostic approach that models interactions between basins using only the base model's own sampled outputs, hidden states, and derived evidence. Most direct correction strategies fail; ARBITER instead uses conservative additive evidence on top of consensus. In its simplest parameter-free form, ARBITER-Δ adds same-model evidence to the majority prior, while ARBITER-Enc augments this with bounded residual signals from hidden states over complete solutions. On GSM8K with Qwen3-4B, consensus over K=24 samples achieves around the mid-94% range, while a same-pool top-2 oracle reaches around the mid-96% range. ARBITER recovers a subset of these cases using zero external information. Across three model families and three math benchmarks, it yields consistent gains with no net-negative cases; for example, on Llama-3.1-8B MMLU-HS-Math, it improves accuracy from the mid-78% range to the mid-82% range, recovering about 22% of the available oracle headroom, indicating that this headroom can be partially recovered from the sample pool itself.

</details>


### [11] [InfoQuant: Shaping Activation Distributions for Low-Bit LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26175)
*Ke Li,Dong An,Xiaoling Zang,Can Ye,Liang Xie,Qibo Qiu,Chen Shen,Xiaofei He,Wenxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出InfoQuant，一种免训练的激活量化方法，通过信息论分析定义量化友好分布，利用峰值抑制正交变换(PSOT)和自适应离群值token选择来优化激活分布，在W4A4KV4设置下平均保持97%的浮点精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特激活量化是LLM高效部署的主要瓶颈。现有PTQ方法仅关注抑制峰值、平衡通道或最小化重构误差，但未明确定义何种激活分布易于离散化，导致数值看似平滑但仍产生大量化误差（量化范围过宽或数值坍缩至均值附近少数几个级别）。

Method: 从信息论视角重新构建激活变换为面向量化器的分布设计，分析得出量化友好分布应同时满足较小数值范围和范围内充分分散。据此提出InfoQuant免训练方法：1)峰值抑制正交变换(PSOT)将激活塑造为量化友好分布；2)自适应离群值token选择提升PSOT优化鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个LLM家族上持续优于先前PTQ和端到端训练基线。W4A4KV4设置下平均保持97%浮点精度，LLaMA-2 13B性能差距较先前SOTA减少42%。

Conclusion: 信息论分析为激活量化提供了清晰理论指导，量化友好分布需兼顾数值范围压缩与分散度保持。InfoQuant证明了分布设计视角的有效性，为低比特LLM部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Low-bit activation quantization remains a major bottleneck in efficient large language model (LLM) deployment. The difficulty is not only that activations contain outliers, but that their distributions are often poorly matched to a low-bit uniform quantizer. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods suppress peaks, balance channels, or minimize reconstruction error, yet they rarely specify what activation distribution is actually easy to discretize. As a result, activations may appear numerically smoother while still incurring large quantization error because the quantization range remains wide or most values collapse into a few levels near the mean. We recast activation transformation as quantizer-facing distribution design and analyze quantization error from an information-theoretic perspective. Our analysis shows that quantization-friendly activations should jointly have a smaller numerical range and sufficient dispersion within that range. Guided by this analysis, we propose InfoQuant, a train-free method that employs Peak Suppression Orthogonal Transformation (PSOT) to shape activations into more quantization-friendly distributions. We further introduce adaptive outlier-token selection to improve the robustness of PSOT during optimization. Across multiple LLM families, InfoQuant consistently outperforms prior PTQ and end-to-end training baselines. Under W4A4KV4, it preserves 97% of floating-point accuracy on average and reduces the LLaMA-2 13B performance gap by 42% over the previous state of the art. Code is available at [https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant](https://github.com/LLIKKE/InfoQuant)

</details>


### [12] [GAC: Noise-Aware Adaptive Mixing for Hybrid SFT-RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26184)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GAC，一种基于噪声感知的自适应混合训练控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和信号分歧动态调整权重，在各基准上显著提升大模型混合后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合后训练采用固定的监督微调与强化学习混合策略，无法适应训练过程中两种信号相对噪声的动态变化，导致训练效率与效果受限。

Method: 设计GAC噪声感知控制器，通过在线估计梯度方差和两种训练信号间的分歧度，推导自适应混合权重；引入平滑、先验引导和有界更新机制，并复用现有训练张量以降低开销。

Result: 在数学、代码、科学、逻辑等基准测试中，GAC持续超越强固定策略与基于规则的基线；模型规模越大收益越显著，且训练额外开销小于1%。

Conclusion: GAC为混合后训练提供了一种高效的自适应权重调节方案，证明了噪声感知动态调度在大模型训练中的有效性与实用价值。

Abstract: Hybrid post-training usually combines supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, but fixed mixing schedules cannot adapt when the relative noise of the two signals changes over time. We propose GAC, a noise-aware controller that derives an adaptive mixing weight from online estimates of gradient variance and disagreement between the two training signals. The method adds smoothing, prior guidance, and bounded updates while reusing existing training tensors. Experiments on math, code, science, and logic benchmarks show that GAC consistently improves hybrid post-training over strong fixed and rule-based baselines, with larger gains at larger model scales and less than 1% training overhead.

</details>


### [13] [Max-Window Scale Estimation for Near-Lossless HiF8 W8A8 Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26189)
*Yingying Cheng,Jinquan Shi,Li Zhou,Zhiyang He,Zhaoyi Sun,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了OpenPangu-Embedded-1B模型在HiF8 W8A8量化感知训练（QAT）中的两种正交失效模式：amax饱和和灾难性遗忘，并提出了相应的解决策略，最终实现了与BF16基线相比仅微小性能下降的量化训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特浮点格式的量化感知训练（QAT）能够实现高效的大语言模型部署，但会引入标准训练指标无法检测的隐晦失效模式。作者旨在通过延迟张量缩放（DTS）的视角，系统研究HiF8 W8A8 QAT中的失效模式，以实现更可靠的量化模型训练。

Method: 研究通过8个受控实验识别并解耦了两种正交失效模式：(i)amax饱和：延迟的尺度估计通过前向传播裁剪静默损坏知识敏感表示；(ii)灾难性遗忘：激进的学习率独立于量化过程覆盖预训练常识知识。针对amax饱和，提出了基于64步历史窗口的保守max-algorithm DTS策略；针对遗忘，采用500步BF16预热后以lr=10^{-5}进行QAT训练。

Result: 最终配置在10,000步训练中实现了：相比匹配的BF16基线，MMLU下降0.43%，HellaSwag下降0.58%，ARC-Challenge下降0.22%，训练损失APE仅为0.11%。两种修复策略被证明是必要且充分的。

Conclusion: 研究成功识别并解决了HiF8 W8A8 QAT中的两种关键失效模式。所提出的保守DTS策略和学习率调度方案能够有效保持模型性能，使量化训练后的模型在各项基准测试中仅表现出微小的性能下降，为低比特量化训练提供了可靠的方法论指导。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) with low-bit floating-point formats enables efficient LLM deployment, yet introduces subtle failure modes invisible to standard training metrics. We present a systematic study of HiF8 W8A8 QAT for OpenPangu-Embedded-1B through the lens of Delayed Tensor Scaling (DTS). Across eight controlled experiments, we identify and disentangle two orthogonal failure modes: (i)amax saturation, where delayed scale estimates silently corrupt knowledge-sensitive representations via forward-pass clipping, and (ii)catastrophic forgetting, where an aggressive learning rate overwrites pretrained commonsense knowledge independently of quantization. Neither is detectable from training loss alone. We address amax saturation with a conservative max-algorithm DTS strategy over a 64-step history window, and mitigate forgetting via a 500-step BF16 warmup followed by QAT at lr=10^{-5}. Both fixes are necessary and sufficient: our final configuration achieves 0.43% MMLU drop, 0.58% HellaSwag drop, and 0.22% ARC-Challenge drop versus a matched BF16 baseline, with a training loss APE of only 0.11% over 10,000 steps.

</details>


### [14] [HRVConformer: Neonatal Hypoxic-Ischemic Encephalopathy Classification from the Heart Rate signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26190)
*Shuwen Yu,William P Marnane,Geraldine B. Boylan,Gordon Lightbody*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HRVConformer深度学习架构，用于缺氧缺血性脑病(HIE)分类，通过结合卷积层和Transformer注意力机制处理原始心率信号，在独立测试集上达到83.23% AUC和74.56%准确率，超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的HIE分类方法依赖手工设计的特征，存在特征表示能力有限的问题。现有方法无法充分捕捉心率信号的局部细节和长程依赖关系，需要开发能够端到端处理原始HR信号的自动化分类方法，以提高HIE诊断的准确性和效率。

Method: 提出HRVConformer混合架构，结合卷积层进行局部特征提取和Transformer注意力机制进行全局上下文建模，实现端到端的原始HR信号处理。使用改进的Pan-Tompkins算法从ECG信号中提取HR信号，提升信号质量和数据可用性。在包含1,573个一小时片段的大规模HR数据集上进行监督学习训练，其中259个片段为专家标注，其余为弱标签数据。

Result: 在215小时的独立专家标注测试集上，HRVConformer达到83.23%的AUC和74.56%的准确率，显著优于Transformer、ResNet50和全卷积网络等基线方法。实验证明了卷积与Transformer组件集成在HR信号HIE分类中的优势，验证了混合架构的有效性。

Conclusion: HRVConformer为基于心率信号的HIE自动评估提供了更准确的方法，展示了混合卷积-Transformer架构在生理信号分类任务中的潜力。该研究向自动化HIE诊断迈出了重要一步，代码已开源，有助于推动相关领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: This paper presents the HRVConformer, a novel deep learning architecture for the classification of hypoxic-ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) using the instantaneous heart rate (HR) signal. Unlike conventional approaches that rely on handcrafted features, HRVConformer directly processes raw HR signals in an end-to-end manner, capturing both local and long-range dependencies through a hybrid Convolution-Transformer framework. By integrating convolutional layers for local feature extraction and Transformer-based attention mechanisms for global context modelling, the architecture effectively enhances signal representation and classification performance. The model was trained using supervised learning on a large HR dataset consisting of 1,573 one-hour epochs, including 259 one-hour expert-annotated epochs and a substantial set of weakly labelled data. A 314-hour validation set provided a robust performance estimation, while an independent 215-hour dataset with expert annotations was reserved for final testing. HR signals were extracted from electrocardiogram (ECG) recordings using an improved Pan-Tompkins algorithm, which significantly enhanced both signal quality and data availability. Experimental results demonstrate that the HRVConformer achieves an AUC of 83.23\% and accuracy of 74.56\% on the test set. These results surpass the performance of the Transformer, ResNet50 and fully convolutional networks baselines, highlighting the advantages of integrating convolutional and Transformer-based components for HR-based HIE classification. The proposed method provides a promising step toward a more accurate and automated assessment of HIE using HR signals. The code is available at: https://github.com/syu-kylin/HRVConformer.

</details>


### [15] [When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26418)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Kai Zhao,Shahryar Sarkani,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，经过适当校准的基于规则的自动扩缩容控制器在所有六个工作负载的成本指标上都优于六种主流深度强化学习算法。DRL仅在突发和闪发流量场景下表现更好，且没有单一算法能在所有工作负载中占主导地位。瓶颈在于基线校准、奖励工程和评估协议，而非算法选择本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度强化学习（DRL）在自适应资源控制领域被广泛研究，但缺乏对其与经过良好校准的传统方法的系统性比较。研究者希望回答一个关键问题：DRL何时（以及是否）真正能提供帮助？现有文献往往忽视了基线方法的校准问题，可能导致对DRL实际价值的过高估计。

Method: 研究团队开发了RLScale-Bench，这是一个可复现的基准测试和评估协议，用于评估DRL在自适应资源控制中的表现。实验设计包括：评估六种主流DRL算法（PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3、DDPG），在匹配的架构、训练预算和奖励函数条件下与校准后的基于规则的基线进行对比；使用六种工作负载模式和五种随机种子进行240次实验运行；在Kubernetes水平Pod自动扩缩容场景中实例化基准测试；研究分布偏移泛化能力。

Result: 实验得出三个关键发现：(i) 校准后的控制器在所有六个工作负载上都实现了最低成本，尽管在突发和闪发流量场景下落后于最佳RL代理；(ii) 离散动作算法在约束违反方面比连续动作算法优一至两个数量级，这是由于动作空间不匹配造成的；(iii) 没有单一算法能在所有工作负载中占主导地位，排名差异可达四个位置。研究揭示了RL资源控制的瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和现实评估协议。

Conclusion: 研究挑战了DRL在资源控制领域普遍适用的假设，强调了严格评估协议和公平基线比较的重要性。结果表明，在投入DRL方法之前，应该优先考虑基于规则方法的适当校准。未来研究应关注奖励工程设计、基线校准方法和更现实的评估协议，而非单纯追求算法改进。这为云计算资源管理领域提供了重要的方法论指导。

Abstract: A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [16] [Modeling Dynamic Mixtures of Time-Delay Systems from Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26191)
*Ren Fujiwara,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DelayMix框架，通过将时间序列流建模为时滞系统的动态混合，使用固定长度的系统张量摘要来应对系统快速变化和输入延迟变化，实现了在非平稳数据上的快速适应和卓越预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据流中的自适应建模面临两大挑战：(1)环境因素或输入延迟变化导致的系统快速变化(regime shifts)会降低模型性能；(2)使用多个小模型处理不同时间序列模式时，需要在准确性、鲁棒性和内存使用之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出在线框架将流式时间序列视为时滞系统的动态混合。核心方法包括：(1)构建总结系统张量，使用系统的马尔可夫参数序列捕捉动态行为和延迟特性；(2)必要时通过张量分解算法从张量中提取相关的历史模型，并选择最适合当前状态的系统。该框架通过固定长度表示总结过去的regime，维持模型跟踪的鲁棒性并减少内存使用。

Result: 在真实数据集上的测试表明，DelayMix方法在预测准确性和对延迟的快速适应方面始终优于其他方法，特别是在高度非平稳数据上表现突出。该方法在保持计算效率的同时实现了对环境变化的快速适应。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了时间序列流自适应建模中的准确性与内存效率权衡问题，通过创新的张量表示和分解技术实现了对系统动态变化和输入延迟的快速适应，为处理非平稳时间序列数据提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This research addresses the problem of adaptive modeling in time-series data streams with clear input-output relationships. This problem is challenging because rapid system changes (regime shifts) caused by environmental factors or input delay changes degrade model performance, and the trade-off among accuracy, robustness, and memory usage arises when using multiple small models for each time-series pattern. To address these issues, this paper presents an online framework/method that treats streaming time series as dynamic mixtures of time-delay systems. This framework maintains robustness of model tracking and reduces memory usage by summarizing past regimes using a fixed-length representation that captures both the system dynamics and input-output delays. Concretely, this approach constructs a summary system tensor using the system's Markov parameter series, capturing both dynamic behavior and delay characteristics. If necessary, a tensor decomposition algorithm extracts relevant past models from the tensor and helps select the system that best fits the current regime. This method enables rapid adaptation to environmental changes and is computationally efficient. Tests on real datasets show that DelayMix consistently outperforms other methods, achieving superior forecast accuracy and faster adaptation to delays, especially for highly non-stationary data.

</details>


### [17] [Co-folding model guided by structural proteomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26192)
*Alon Shtrikman,Nitzan Simchi,Michal Ran Shchory,Sagie Brodsky,Eran Seger,Kirill Pevzner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIMS-Fold框架，通过将XL-MS和HDX-MS结构蛋白质组学数据整合到预训练扩散模型中，显著提升了蛋白质复合物构象预测精度，在诱导邻近靶标上优于纯计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构生成模型擅长预测单一蛋白静态结构，但在捕获蛋白质复合物的正确构象状态方面表现不佳，而这对于蛋白质设计以及抗体、PROTAC等诱导邻近模态至关重要。结构蛋白质组学技术（如XL-MS和HDX-MS）可提供有价值的空间和动态信息，但将这些稀疏、异质的测量数据整合到生成模型中仍是一个开放性挑战。

Method: 提出AIMS-Fold框架，一种推理时引导扩散方法。该方法将结构蛋白质组学数据与预训练扩散模型学习的丰富生物物理先验知识相结合，通过使用源自XL-MS空间约束和HDX-MS溶剂可及性谱的可微分物理势，主动引导生成采样轨迹。

Result: 实验证明，各结构方法可独立提升预测精度，且它们的整合能产生协同改进效果。关键在于，通过利用这些实验约束，AIMS-Fold在具有挑战性的诱导邻近靶标上实现了比纯计算、无引导的最先进模型（如Boltz-2）更高的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个强大的整合计算框架，为基于结构的诱导邻近药物设计提供了有效工具，成功弥合了结构蛋白质组学数据与深度生成模型之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Protein structure generative models excel at predicting single protein static structures from sequence, but routinely fail to capture the correct conformational state of protein complexes, critical for protein design and induced proximity modalities such as antibodies and PROTACs. While structural proteomics techniques like Cross-Linking Mass Spectrometry (XL-MS) and Hydrogen-Deuterium Exchange (HDX-MS) offer valuable spatial and dynamic insights, integrating these sparse, heterogeneous measurements into these models remains an open challenge. Here, we bridge this gap by combining structural proteomics data with the rich biophysical priors learned by pretrained diffusion models. We introduce AIMS-Fold, an inference-time guided-diffusion framework that actively steers the generative sampling trajectory using differentiable physical potentials derived from XL-MS spatial restraints and HDX-MS solvent accessibility profiles. We demonstrate that these structural methods individually enhance predictive accuracy, and their integration yields synergistic improvement. Crucially, by leveraging these experimental restraints, AIMS-Fold achieves higher accuracy on challenging induced proximity targets than purely computational, unguided state-of-the-art models like Boltz-2. This establishes our framework as a powerful, integrative computational approach for the structure based drug design of induced proximity drugs. Evaluation code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [18] [ReMoE: Boosting Expert Reuse through Router Fine-Tuning in Memory-Constrained MoE LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27081)
*Xiongwei Zhu,Xiaojian Liao,Tianyang Jiang,Yusen Zhang,Liang Wang,Limin Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReMoE路由微调框架，通过增强专家复用减少MoE模型的I/O开销，在内存受限场景下实现1.77-1.99倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE模型在内存受限推理场景下，仅能缓存小部分专家，未缓存专家需从慢速外部存储获取，导致频繁驱逐和大量I/O开销。现有路由策略未考虑缓存局部性约束。

Method: 提出ReMoE路由微调框架，通过使路由器偏向最近选择的专家，产生时间稳定的路由决策，增加短期专家复用，从而减少存储获取次数，且不增加推理时计算开销。

Result: 在DeepSeek和Qwen模型上实验表明：专家复用提升26%，下游任务性能保持；在vLLM GPU-CPU专家卸载下输出吞吐量提升8.4%；在Jetson Orin NX的llama.cpp上TPOT降低43.6-49.8%，实现1.77-1.99倍解码加速。

Conclusion: ReMoE通过优化路由策略以匹配缓存局部性约束，有效解决了MoE模型在内存受限场景下的I/O瓶颈问题，在实际系统中取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models sparsely activate only a subset of experts per token, reducing activated computation while maintaining high model capacity. However, in memory-constrained inference scenarios, only a small set of experts can be cached. Experts not in the cache must be fetched from slow external storage (e.g., UFS), leading to frequent evictions and substantial I/O overhead.
  We propose ReMoE, a router fine-tuning framework designed to boost token-wise expert reuse. ReMoE biases the router toward recently selected experts, producing temporally stable routing that better matches cache locality constraints. By increasing short-horizon expert reuse, ReMoE reduces expert fetches from storage without adding inference-time computation.
  Experiments on DeepSeek and Qwen models show that ReMoE improves expert reuse by 26% while maintaining downstream task performance. Real-system evaluations further confirm these benefits, improving output throughput by 8.4% under vLLM GPU-CPU expert offloading and reducing TPOT by 43.6-49.8% under llama.cpp on Jetson Orin NX, corresponding to a 1.77-1.99$\times$ decode speedup across diverse workloads. Checkpoints and usage instructions are available at https://github.com/BUAA-OSCAR/ReMoE.

</details>


### [19] [Bridging Classification and Reconstruction: Cooperative Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26193)
*Qideng Tang,Dai Chaofan,Wubin Ma,Yahui Wu,Haohao Zhou,Tao Zhang,Huan Li,Dalin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoAD框架，通过统一分类（OE）和重构（MAE）两种范式，解决时间序列异常检测中微妙且持续异常的检测难题，显著优于现有方法且计算高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习TSAD方法在检测微妙和持续性异常方面存在不足；OE方法受限于泛化能力差，MAE方法受限于掩码对齐问题，亟需一种能够互补两者优势并克服各自缺陷的新方法。

Method: 提出CoAD框架统一分类和重构范式：分类模块生成概率驱动的软掩码供重构模块使用，重构模块反过来缓解分类模块的泛化问题；精心设计分类模块以解决分类粒度不当和频率信息被忽视的问题。

Result: 在高质量基准数据集和严格评估协议下的广泛实验表明，CoAD显著优于深度学习和传统数据挖掘的最先进方法；同时模型轻量化，比现有SOTA方法运行速度大幅提升，适合大规模实时应用。

Conclusion: CoAD通过协同设计有效融合分类与重构范式，成功检测到现有方法常忽略的微妙复杂异常，证明了深度学习在TSAD领域的潜力，为大规模实时异常检测提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) has long been a hot research topic in data mining due to its various applications. Recent studies challenge the effectiveness of popular deep learning methods for TSAD, suggesting their failure in detecting subtle and prolonged anomalies. Outlier Exposure (OE) and Masked Autoencoder (MAE) emerge as two promising paradigms (classification and reconstruction) for solving the above problems. However, OE-based methods are constrained by poor generalization, while MAE-based methods are limited by masking misalignment issues. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a novel framework, CoAD, which unifies the two paradigms to leverage their complementary strengths while mitigating their respective weaknesses. In this framework, the classification module generates probability-informed soft masks for the reconstruction module, which in turn alleviates the generalization problem of the classification module. This cooperative design enables CoAD to effectively detect subtle and complex anomalies that are often overlooked by existing methods. Additionally, the classification module is carefully designed to resolve issues related to improper classification granularity and the neglect of frequency information. Extensive experiments on high-quality benchmark datasets, conducted under rigorous evaluation protocols, demonstrate that CoAD significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art deep learning and traditional data mining methods, highlighting the potential of deep learning in TSAD. Moreover, CoAD is lightweight and substantially faster than existing SOTA methods, demonstrating its practical value for large-scale, real-time applications.

</details>


### [20] [On the Role of Inductive Bias in Time-Series Pretraining: A Case Study in Learning Generalizable Representations for Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26194)
*Sharmita Dey,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PathoFM模型，通过结合局部补全、时序连续性和无监督上下文动力学三种预训练目标，在脊髓损伤病理步态分析中实现了跨任务类型和跨被试的鲁棒表征迁移，解决了临床时间序列数据受限场景下的表征学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列学习常受限于小规模、异质性队列和协议漂移，而下游应用同时涵盖分类和回归任务。这促使研究者探索基础模型预训练方法，但关键问题在于：预训练目标应施加何种归纳偏置，才能使表征在不同任务类型和被试间有效迁移。

Method: 提出PathoFM，一个以编码器为中心的Transformer模型，在多变量步态窗口上进行预训练，采用三个互补目标：(1)局部补全：重建连续掩码片段以强化局部结构；(2)时序连续性：从观测前缀预测掩码的中期延续，以强化平滑性和因果一致性；(3)无监督上下文动力学：通过注意力机制基于被试样本窗口进行支持-查询重建。研究对比了分组/对比、基于动力学和生成重建三类目标族。

Result: 实验表明，以动力学为中心的混合目标产生最均衡的迁移性能：分组目标有利于判别边界但可能降低连续目标所需的幅度保真度；纯重建目标保持波形结构但可能在分类任务上表现欠佳。结合局部重建与时序连续性，并在样本访问可行时加入上下文条件，能产生鲁棒的被试泛化表征。

Conclusion: 在病理步态分析的脊髓损伤应用中，动力学中心型预训练目标组合为临床时间序列学习提供了有效的归纳偏置，能够在分类和回归任务间实现平衡的迁移性能，为受限于小规模异质数据集的临床场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Clinical time-series learning is routinely constrained by small, heterogeneous cohorts and protocol drift, while its downstream use spans both classification (e.g., pathology diagnosis) and regression (e.g., temporal forecasting). These constraints make foundation-model pretraining appealing, but raises an important question of which inductive biases should the pretraining objective impose so that representations transfer across task types and subjects. We study this question in pathological gait analysis for spinal cord injury (SCI) via PathoFM, an encoder-centric transformer pretrained on multivariate gait windows with three complementary objectives: Local Completion (reconstruct contiguous masked spans to enforce local structure), Temporal Continuity (predict a masked mid-horizon continuation from an observed prefix to enforce smoothness and causal consistency), and Unsupervised In-Context Dynamics (support-query reconstruction conditioned on subject exemplar windows via attention). Empirically comparing objective families (grouping/contrastive, dynamics-based, and generative reconstruction), we find that dynamics-centric mixtures produce the most balanced transfer: grouping objectives favor discriminative margins but can degrade magnitude fidelity needed for continuous targets, whereas reconstruction-only objectives preserve waveform structure but may underperform on classification. Overall, combining local reconstruction with temporal continuity, and adding in-context conditioning when exemplar access is realistic, yields robust subject-generalizing representations.

</details>


### [21] [From Privacy to Generalization: Linear Max-Information Bounds for DP-SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26222)
*Christoph H. Lampert,Hossein Zakerinia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了DP-SGD的有限样本近似最大信息界，其随数据集大小呈线性增长，并据此推导出了显式的PAC-Bayes泛化界和DP-SGD训练模型的泛化界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解泛化与隐私之间的关系是现代机器学习理论的核心挑战，特别是对于通过差分隐私随机梯度下降(DP-SGD)训练的深度网络，这一问题仍然是一个持续存在的开放性问题。

Method: 证明DP-SGD的有限样本近似最大信息界，该界表现出与ε-差分隐私算法的经典结果相当的缩放特性，即相对于数据集大小最多呈线性增长。

Result: 获得了一个通用的PAC-Bayes泛化界，其中必要的先验分布可以通过DP-SGD学习得到；同时获得了DP-SGD训练模型本身的泛化界，其复杂度项完全显式且由优化超参数控制。

Conclusion: 通过建立DP-SGD的近似最大信息界，本研究为理解差分隐私学习中的泛化-隐私权衡提供了新的理论工具，并给出了完全由优化超参数控制的显式泛化保证。

Abstract: Understanding the relationship between generalization and privacy remains a central challenge in modern machine learning theory, particularly for deep networks trained by variants of differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD). In this work we make progress on this persistent open problem by proving a finite-sample bound on the approximate max-information of DP-SGD that exhibits scaling properties comparable with (Dwork et al, 2015)'s classic result for $ε$-differentially private algorithms, namely at most linear in the dataset size. From our result we obtain a general-purpose PAC-Bayes generalization bound in which the necessary prior distribution can be learned by DP-SGD, as well as a generalization bound for DP-SGD-trained models themselves, with a complexity term that is fully explicit and controlled by the optimization hyperparameters.

</details>


### [22] [Provably Communication-Efficient and Privacy-Preserving Federated Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26243)
*Zhishuai Guo,Wenhan Wu,Chen Chen,Lei Zhang,Olivera Kotevska,Ravi K Madduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CE-FedGNN框架，通过低频交换聚合节点表示和移动平均估计器实现通信高效的跨客户端图学习，并采用度量差分隐私提供形式化隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图数据常分布在无法共享原始数据的组织中，现有联邦GNN方法要么忽略跨客户端链接导致精度下降，要么需要频繁交换嵌入导致高昂的通信和隐私成本。

Method: 避免共享原始数据或逐轮嵌入，通过低频交换聚合节点表示；引入移动平均估计器持续跟踪节点表示以处理跨客户端依赖和延迟；采用度量差分隐私框架在学习的嵌入空间中测量隐私。

Result: 建立了收敛率为O(1/√T)、通信复杂度为O(T^{3/4})的收敛保证；推导了在公共队列威胁模型下的(ε,δ)-度量差分隐私保证；实验表明在显著减少通信和保持隐私噪声鲁棒性的同时实现了强性能。

Conclusion: CE-FedGNN在合成银行间反洗钱基准和引文网络上表现出色，成功实现了通信高效、隐私保护的跨客户端图学习，为联邦图学习提供了理论收敛保证和形式化隐私保证。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) achieve strong performance on relational data, but real-world graphs are often distributed across organizations that cannot share raw data due to privacy and policy constraints. Existing federated GNN methods either ignore cross-client links, leading to degraded accuracy, or require frequent embedding exchanges, incurring substantial communication and privacy costs. We propose CE-FedGNN, a communication-efficient and privacy-preserving federated GNN framework for learning over such coupled graphs. Our approach avoids sharing raw data or per-round embeddings by infrequently exchanging aggregated node representations. To handle cross-client dependency and staleness, we introduce a moving-average estimator that continuously tracks node representations and enables their stable reuse across rounds. To provide formal privacy guarantees for the released representations, we adopt the metric differential privacy (metric-DP) framework, which measures privacy with respect to distances in the learned embedding space rather than worst-case input perturbations. This yields meaningful guarantees at noise levels where standard differential privacy becomes overly conservative. We establish convergence to a stationary point at a rate of $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ with $O(T^{3/4})$ communication complexity. In addition, we derive $(\varepsilon,δ)$-metric-DP guarantees via Rényi differential privacy composition under a public-cohort threat model. Experiments on synthetic interbank anti-money laundering benchmarks and citation networks demonstrate that CE-FedGNN achieves strong performance while significantly reducing communication and maintaining robustness under privacy-preserving noise.

</details>


### [23] [The Bridge-Garden Dilemma in LLM Distillation: Why Mixing Hard and Soft Labels Works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26246)
*Guanghui Wang,Kaiwen Lv Kacuila,Zhiyong Yang,Zitai Wang,Jin-Wen Wu,Longtao Huang,Qianqian Xu,Qingming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bridge-Garden混合监督方法，通过结合硬标签和软标签来平衡生成步骤中的精确性和多样性，显著提高了知识蒸馏的效果和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软标签在知识蒸馏中看似信息更丰富，但作者发现混合硬标签和软标签能够持续获得更好的效果。然而，现有理论无法解释为什么这种混合策略更有效，其背后的机制尚不清晰。作者旨在揭示这一现象的根本原因，并提出理论解释。

Method: 作者提出了Bridge-Garden Decomposition理论，将生成步骤分为两类：Bridges（需要精确预测的步骤）和Gardens（允许灵活性的步骤）。基于此理论，硬标签蒸馏在Bridges步骤中表现更好（避免风险偏差），软标签蒸馏在Gardens步骤中保持多样性。作者开发了一系列Bridge-Garden混合监督方法，能够自适应地平衡硬标签和软标签。

Result: 在七组师生模型对（包括Qwen、Llama、Gemma和DeepSeek）以及推理和编程基准测试中，该方法优于基于散度和在策略的知识蒸馏基线方法，同时将训练成本降低了9.7倍，实现了高效的模型压缩。

Conclusion: 混合硬标签和软标签的知识蒸馏方法之所以有效，是因为它减少了曝光偏差（exposure bias），即训练和推理分布之间的不匹配。Bridge-Garden理论为理解和改进知识蒸馏提供了新的理论框架，混合策略能够同时处理生成过程中的精确性要求和多样性需求。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

</details>


### [24] [Unified Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26248)
*Ethan Caballero,Priyank Jaini,David Krueger,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的神经网络缩放定律（UNSL）函数形式，能够准确建模和预测深度神经网络在多个维度（参数、数据集、训练步数、推理步数、计算量、超参数等）同时变化时的缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经缩放定律函数形式在处理多个维度同时变化时的预测精度不足，需要一种更准确、统一的函数形式来预测不同架构和任务（视觉、语言、数学、强化学习）中的缩放行为。

Method: 提出了统一神经缩放定律（UNSL）函数形式，该函数能够同时建模多个维度的变化（模型参数数量、训练数据集大小、训练步数、推理步数、计算量和各种超参数），适用于不同架构和多种上下游任务。

Result: UNSL在视觉、语言、数学和强化学习等大规模任务上进行了验证，与其他神经缩放函数形式相比，其缩放行为的外推预测显著更加准确。

Conclusion: UNSL函数形式为深度神经网络的缩放行为预测提供了一个更准确、更通用的解决方案，能够有效指导多维度变化下的模型设计和资源分配决策。

Abstract: We present a functional form (that we refer to as a Unified Neural Scaling Law (UNSL)) that accurately models and extrapolates the scaling behaviors of deep neural networks as multiple dimensions all vary simultaneously (i.e. how the evaluation metric of interest varies as one simultaneously varies the number of model parameters, training dataset size, number of training steps, number of inference steps, amount of compute, and various hyperparameters) for various architectures and for each of various tasks within a varied set of upstream and downstream tasks. This set includes large-scale vision, language, math, and reinforcement learning. When compared to other functional forms for neural scaling, this functional form yields extrapolations of scaling behavior that are considerably more accurate on this set.

</details>


### [25] [Quantized Keys Steal Attention: Bias Correction for KV-Cache Compression in Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26266)
*Tuna Tuncer,Felix Becker,Thomas Pfeil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视频扩散模型中KV缓存量化导致的注意力偏差问题，提出了一种无需额外内存开销的校正方法，使INT2量化能达到接近BF16的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块自回归视频扩散模型依赖KV缓存避免冗余计算，但随着视频变长，缓存成为内存瓶颈。现有低比特量化方法虽缓解内存压力，但会严重降低视频质量，亟需解决量化导致的质量退化问题。

Method: （1）发现并理论分析了Jensen偏差：softmax注意力的指数凸性使量化噪声系统性放大缓存键的贡献，导致量化键从未量化的当前块窃取注意力；（2）推导出一种逐注意力分数的校正方法，基于缓存键的量化步长和查询范数实时计算；（3）采用二阶泰勒近似实现，额外计算开销可忽略，且无需额外内存。

Result: 在MAGI-1、SkyReels-V2和HY-WorldPlay上的INT2量化实验表明：（1）恢复了激进量化损失的大部分质量；（2）达到接近BF16的视频质量；（3）在内存减少50%的情况下可超越INT4量化性能。

Conclusion: 该工作首次揭示了KV缓存量化中Jensen偏差的本质机制，提出的校正方法有效解决了量化质量退化问题，为长视频生成中的内存优化提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: Chunk-wise autoregressive video diffusion models rely on a KV cache of previously generated chunks to avoid redundant computation, but this cache quickly becomes a memory bottleneck as videos grow longer. Methods that quantize the KV cache to low bitwidths reduce memory pressure but degrade video quality. We show that a key driver of this degradation is a systematic bias in attention weights: due to the convexity of the exponential in softmax attention, quantization noise inflates the contribution of cached keys, a phenomenon we call the Jensen bias. This effect causes quantized keys to steal attention mass from the unquantized current chunk. We derive a per-attention-score correction that removes this bias in expectation, computed on the fly from the quantization step sizes of the cached keys and the query norm. Using a second-order Taylor approximation, the additional computational overhead is negligible, and no additional memory is needed alongside the cache. Evaluated on MAGI-1, SkyReels-V2, and HY-WorldPlay at INT2 quantization, our correction recovers most of the quality lost to aggressive quantization, reaching near-BF16 video quality, and can outperform INT4 quantization while using 50% less memory.

</details>


### [26] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MBDPO框架，通过扩散策略表示统一搜索与策略优化，解决了世界模型中搜索与价值学习之间的结构错位问题，在多任务离线预训练、在线学习和离线到在线微调等场景中均表现出色，且随模型规模增大性能持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法存在一个关键但未被充分探索的瓶颈：搜索与价值学习之间的结构错位。具体而言，策略改进往往依赖于由独立非搜索策略诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致并最终导致学习次优。

Method: 提出基于模型的扩散策略优化（MBDPO）框架，将策略优化重新表述为潜在世界模型中搜索轨迹上的扩散过程。该方法不构建显式规划器，而是从收集的数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，通过扩散策略表示统一搜索与策略优化。

Result: MBDPO在多任务离线预训练、在线学习、离线到在线微调等多种场景下均表现良好。在离线场景中，通过在大规模数据集上预训练，观察到随模型容量增加性能呈现一致且单调的提升。

Conclusion: MBDPO通过统一搜索与策略优化，成功缓解了错位问题，释放了世界模型在可扩展策略学习中的潜力，证明了基于扩散的隐式规划方法的有效性。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

</details>


### [27] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了双参数流方法，通过学习采样时间传输并回归合成轨迹，解决了无轨迹信息的高维概率密度动态学习问题，该方法可扩展至高维并支持非梯度动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于解决从无标签样本学习高维概率密度时间演化动态的难题，特别是在缺乏轨迹信息的情况下。传统方法需要完整的轨迹数据或每步最优传输耦合，这在许多实际应用中难以获得，限制了高维问题的可扩展性。

Method: 提出双参数流（two-parameter flows）方法：首先学习从基础分布到每个边缘分布的采样时间传输（利用成熟的条件流匹配技术），然后通过对耦合合成轨迹进行回归来提取物理时间速度场。该方法避免了每步最优传输耦合，能够捕捉非梯度动力学现象。

Result: 证明了所得物理时间动力学的唯一性，并表明其继承了采样时间传输的正则性。方法可扩展至高维问题，支持非梯度动力学（能自然解释旋转或环流物理现象），避免了传统方法中的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过将采样时间传输与物理时间动力学分离，本文提出了一种可扩展、理论可靠的方法来学习复杂动力学系统，为理解高维概率密度演化提供了新途径，特别适用于具有旋转或环流特征的物理现象。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

</details>


### [28] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个有状态推理架构，通过持久化KV缓存、前缀缓存和推测解码，将多智能体工具调用场景下的每轮推理成本从O(n_t)降至O(Δ_t)，在6轮和35轮工作流中分别实现了2.1倍和4.2倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理框架将每个工具调用视为独立请求，即使85-95%的提示词未改变，仍需从头重新处理整个对话，导致计算资源严重浪费。多智能体工具调用正成为主流交互模式，亟需一种能高效复用已计算状态的推理架构。

Method: 提出有状态推理架构：1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅增量摄入新token；2) 基数前缀缓存支持交错多智能体流量共享；3) 提示查找推测解码器加速结构化输出生成。核心思想是将每轮O(n_t)成本转化为O(Δ_t)的增量成本。

Result: 在全新的完全生成式工作负载上对比vLLM和SGLang：6轮智能体工作流每轮加速2.1倍；35轮工作流中位轮次加速4.2倍；端到端墙钟时间减半。优势源于有状态复用与推测解码，而非传统缓存。

Conclusion: 有状态推理架构有效解决了多智能体场景下的重复计算问题，为LLM系统的主流交互模式提供了显著性能提升，该方法优于现有主流推理框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

</details>


### [29] [Dynamic Link Prediction with Temporally Enhanced Signed Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26290)
*Derek Regier,Andrew Polyak,Aresh Dadlani,Khosro Salmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模块化的时间增强框架，通过引入历史上下文集成模块（HCIM）来提升时间符号网络（TSN）中的链路预测性能，在多个真实和合成数据集上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经网络在静态或无符号链路预测任务上表现良好，但在处理时间符号图时面临挑战，主要困难在于符号关系、演化结构以及平衡理论约束之间的复杂交互作用。

Method: 提出了一个架构无关的模块化时间增强框架，核心是历史上下文集成模块（HCIM）。该模块集成了三个关键组件：（1）可学习的近期感知时间加权机制；（2）基于LSTM的嵌入轨迹建模；（3）多头时间注意力机制。通过全局或节点自适应加权方式将历史信息与当前节点表示融合，以捕获短期和长期的符号交互动态。该方法在自解释符号图Transformer（SE-SGformer）上进行了实例化实现。

Result: 在真实世界数据集（Bitcoin OTC、Bitcoin Alpha、Reddit）和合成数据集（小世界网络模型）上进行了实验，相比静态基线方法取得了持续且统计显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化框架成功地将时间感知能力集成到静态符号GNN架构中，有效解决了时间符号网络中符号关系、时间演化和平衡理论约束的综合建模问题，同时保持了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Temporal signed networks (TSNs) model the time evolution of cooperative and adversarial relationships that arise in applications such as social media analysis, trust and reputation systems, and financial transaction networks. While graph neural networks (GNNs) perform well for static or unsigned link prediction, effective learning in temporal signed graphs remains challenging due to the interaction of signed relations, evolving structure, and balance-theoretic constraints. To address this gap, we propose a \emph{modular} temporal enhancement framework for signed GNNs that integrates historical context into otherwise static architectures. The framework introduces a Historical Context Integration Module (HCIM) that combines learnable recency-aware temporal weighting, LSTM-based embedding trajectory modeling, and multi-head temporal attention to capture both short- and long-term signed interaction dynamics. Historical information is fused with current node representations using either global or node-adaptive weighting, allowing the architecture-agnostic framework to accommodate heterogeneous temporal behaviors. We instantiate the approach on the Self-Explainable Signed Graph Transformer (SE-SGformer), preserving interpretability while extending it with temporal awareness. Experiments on real-world and synthetic TSNs, including Bitcoin OTC, Bitcoin Alpha, Reddit, and small-world network models, demonstrate consistent and statistically significant improvements over the static baseline.

</details>


### [30] [Classification and detection of multiple UAVs using rational Gaussian wavelet neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26310)
*Ungvári Gergő,Ferenc Braun,Attila Ámon,Péter Kackstädter,János Volk,Péter Kovács,Tamás Dózsa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于麦克风声音信号的低成本UAV检测系统，使用可解释的自适应有理高斯小波变换与小型神经网络结合，在室内外环境中实现了对单个UAV和无人机群的有效检测与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对民用和军事基础设施的保护而言，无人机（UAV）的检测具有重要意义。现有方法成本较高或缺乏可解释性，因此需要开发一种低成本且具有物理可解释性的UAV检测系统。

Method: 采用麦克风采集声音信号，通过信号处理管道处理；设计可解释的自适应特征提取器，使用有理高斯小波（rational Gaussian wavelets）；将自适应小波变换嵌入小型神经网络中，联合训练以实现UAV的检测与分类；整个算法具有物理可解释性，并能检测无人机群。

Result: 在室内演播室和噪声室外环境中收集的数据上进行了验证；该方法在检测和分类单个UAV以及无人机群方面均优于传统机器学习方法；同时保持了高度的可解释性；代码已公开发布以确保可重复性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于自适应有理高斯小波的可解释机器学习方法，在UAV检测和分类任务中表现出色，兼具高性能和可解释性，为低成本无人机监测系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The detection of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) is important for the protection of civilian and military infrastructure. In this paper we propose a cost effective UAV detection system using sound signals obtained from microphones. The recorded signals are passed through a signal processing pipeline which employs interpretable adaptive feature extractors using so-called rational Gaussian wavelets. These adaptive wavelet transformations are embedded into and trained together with an underlying small neural network which detects and classifies UAVs based on the obtained features. This leads to a physically interpretable machine learning algorithm that in addition to classifying UAVs is also capable of detecting UAV swarms. We demonstrate our results using data collected in indoor studio and noisy outdoor environments. We conclude that the proposed method outperforms traditional machine learning approaches for detecting and classifying single UAVs as well as drone swarms, while retaining a high degree of interpretability. Our implementation of the proposed methods is made publicly available for reproducibility.

</details>


### [31] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Staged-Competence框架，通过课程学习策略组织偏好数据难度、采用基于能力的采样和渐进式更新参考模型，显著提升了DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，在减少OOD有害响应和越狱攻击成功率方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接偏好优化（DPO）在大语言模型安全对齐中被广泛应用，但现有研究表明其脆弱且在分布外（OOD）泛化能力差，这促使研究者探索如何提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence框架，该方法基于课程学习原理，包含三个关键组件：（1）按难度组织偏好数据；（2）采用基于能力的采样策略；（3）训练过程中渐进式更新参考模型。该方法与策略优化损失函数无关，可扩展至其他DPO变体和对齐领域。

Result: 在三个模型家族上的平均结果表明：OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，同时保持通用能力且近乎零过度拒绝；仅使用75%训练数据即可达到基线安全水平；在安全与不安全响应之间实现更好分离。

Conclusion: Staged-Competence成功提升了DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和泛化能力，证明了课程学习在安全对齐中的有效性，为构建更安全可靠的大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

</details>


### [32] [MULTISEISMO: A Multimodal Seismic Dataset and Model for Cross-Modal Seismic Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26320)
*Sai Munikoti,Ian Stewart,Chengping Chai,Lisa Linville,Scott Vasquez,Sameera Horawalavithana,Karl Pazdernik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MultiSeismo，一个大规模结构化多模态地震数据集，包含2010-2023年间超过1.6万次地震事件的多源数据；同时开发了MISCE指令集，并基于此微调提出了首个地震领域专用多模态模型SeisModal，验证了领域适应架构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态模型（GMMs）在专业科学领域的应用受限于缺乏整合多种数据模态的领域特定数据集。在地震学中，理解地震现象需要综合时序波形数据、地理影像和上下文元数据，这种多模态整合在现有地震数据集中缺失。

Method: 1）构建MultiSeismo数据集，包含16K+地震事件（2010-2023），整合全球台网波形记录、烈度图、人口暴露可视化及文本描述，采用标准化JSON格式；
2）开发MISCE多模态指令集，用于监督训练和评估GMMs在地震推理任务上的表现；
3）在Unified IO 2基础上增强专用时序编码器，微调得到领域专用模型SeisModal。

Result: 对最先进多模态模型在MultiSeismo上的评估显示，通用模型在时序数据处理方面存在显著挑战；SeisModal在地震多模态推理任务上表现优异，证明了领域特定架构适应的成功。MultiSeismo为未来地震学多模态研究提供了严格的基准。

Conclusion: MultiSeismo填补了地震学领域多模态数据集的空白，MISCE指令集支持了GMMs的监督训练与评估，SeisModal验证了领域专用架构适应的有效性，为地震学多模态研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The application of generalist multimodal models (GMMs) to specialized scientific domains remains limited due to the scarcity of comprehensive domain-specific datasets that integrate multiple data modalities beyond text and images. In seismology, understanding earthquake phenomena requires the synthesis of timeseries waveform data, geographical imagery, and contextual metadata, a multimodal integration absent in existing seismic datasets. We present MultiSeismo, a large scale structured multimodal seismic dataset, comprising over 16K seismic events spanning 13 years (2010 to 2023) across diverse geographical regions. Each event data integrates waveform recordings from global station networks, intensity maps, population exposure visualizations, and a comprehensive textual description within a standardized JSON format. We additionally develop MISCE, a multimodal instruction set on top of raw data to enable supervised training and evaluation of GMMs on seismic reasoning tasks ranging from basic information retrieval to complex cross modal analysis. We leverage MISCE to finetune an existing multimodal model (Unified IO 2) enhanced with a specialized timeseries encoder, which yields SeisModal, the first domain specific multimodal model for comprehensive seismic analysis. Evaluation of state of the art multimodal models on MultiSeismo reveals significant challenges, particularly with time-series data processing for general purpose models, while demonstrating SeisModal's superior performance on seismic multimodal reasoning tasks. These results prove that MultiSeismo provides a rigorous benchmark for future multimodal research in seismology and validate the success of our domain specific architectural adaptations.

</details>


### [33] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出归一化半群误差作为评估学习型物理模拟器的模型无关诊断指标，发现该指标与长时预测退化正相关（ρ=0.635），但半群正则化作为训练目标效果不一，更适合作为评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型物理模拟器的评估指标（如单步或短时预测误差）无法有效检测时间组合性和长时预测中的失败；对于自主且状态完备的系统，精确解映射应满足半群律，但现有方法缺乏对此性质的系统性评估。

Method: 提出归一化半群误差作为后验、模型无关的诊断指标，比较直接预测s+t时间步与先预测s再预测t的组合预测结果；在一维热传导和Burgers动力学实验中，使用时间条件化ConvNet和FNO作为基准模型进行验证。

Result: 在一维热传导和Burgers动力学实验中，半群误差与长时预测退化呈正相关，轨迹级Spearman相关系数ρ=0.635，95%置信区间[0.621, 0.649]；半群正则化作为训练目标效果混合，主要支持将半群一致性作为评估诊断而非通用训练目标。

Conclusion: 归一化半群误差可作为有效的评估诊断工具，用于识别学习型物理模拟器在时间组合性方面的缺陷；但半群正则化不适合作为普适的训练目标，应主要将其定位为评估指标而非训练约束。

Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

</details>


### [34] [Reparametrizing Shampoo and SOAP for Subspace Basis Updates and BFloat16 Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26327)
*Alan Milligan,Zikun Xu,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Felix Dangel,Wu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对基于Shampoo优化方法（如KL-Shampoo、SOAP）的重参数化策略，通过部分基向量更新和QR分解实现BFloat16存储支持，在降低计算开销和内存占用的同时保持了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Shampoo的方法（如KL-Shampoo和SOAP）依赖QR分解，需要FP32精度且计算开销大。当预条件矩阵较大时，这些方法在时间和内存上都很昂贵。此外，使用BFloat16存储来减少内存使用会降低这些方法的性能。

Method: 提出一种预条件器的重参数化方法：1) 支持BFP16存储；2) 将更新的基向量与未改变的基向量结合形成完整基；3) 仅在子空间中通过QR分解更新部分基向量，从而减少计算开销并缓解BFP16存储导致的性能下降。该方法适用于所有采用QR分解的Shampoo类方法。

Result: 该方法提高了SOAP和KL-SOAP在BFP16存储下的性能，使KL-SOAP能够匹配或超越KL-Shampoo的表现。总体上使基于Shampoo的方法在内存和时间效率方面都得到提升。

Conclusion: 提出的重参数化方法成功解决了Shampoo类方法在高内存需求和低精度存储之间的权衡问题，实现了更高效的训练，具有广泛的适用性（覆盖KL-Shampoo、SOAP和KL-SOAP等多种方法）。

Abstract: Shampoo-based methods, such as KL-Shampoo and SOAP, have demonstrated strong performance in training neural networks and rely on QR decomposition. Because existing QR implementations require single-precision (FP32) arithmetic and remain computationally expensive, these methods become time- and memory-intensive when their preconditioning matrices are large. Moreover, using BFloat16 (BFP16) storage to reduce memory usage can degrade the performance of Shampoo-based methods. We propose a reparametrization of the preconditioner that supports BFP16 storage and forms a complete basis by combining updated basis vectors with unchanged ones. By updating only part of the basis through QR decomposition in a subspace, our approach reduces computational overhead while mitigating the performance degradation caused by BFP16 storage. Our approach applies broadly to Shampoo-based methods that employ QR decomposition, including KL-Shampoo, SOAP, and KL-SOAP. In particular, it improves the performance of SOAP and KL-SOAP under BFP16 storage, enabling KL-SOAP to match or exceed KL-Shampoo. Overall, our approach makes Shampoo-based methods more memory- and time-efficient.

</details>


### [35] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAM-W（正交幅度调制权重编解码器），通过恢复权重行内的成对坐标结构，在约5.5 bpw下实现与BF16相当的性能，比SmoothQuant W8A8节省32%的权重比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量后训练量化器会丢弃权重行内的成对坐标结构，导致量化精度损失。本文旨在恢复这一结构以提升量化性能。

Method: QAM-W对每行权重进行L2归一化、块Hadamard旋转、配对为2D坐标，使用在单位圆高斯分布上训练的单一Lloyd-Max码本进行量化，并采用激活感知的逐通道缩放。

Result: 在跨模型研究（5个LLM，4个家族，1.1B-13B参数）中，约5.5 bpw的激活感知变体在所有模型上WikiText-2困惑度与BF16相差±0.4%，匹配SmoothQuant W8A8质量包络但减少32%权重比特；联合2D编码优于极坐标编码2-15 pp ΔPPL；成对KL与ΔPPL%高度相关（Spearman ρ=0.99）。

Conclusion: QAM-W在5-6 bpw质量保持带宽上表现优异，但在严格的4 bpw下，旋转码本前沿方法QTIP表现更优；核心贡献在于提供高质量的中等比特率量化方案。

Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

</details>


### [36] [A PAC-Bayesian View of Generalisation for Physics-Informed Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26341)
*Thien V. Nguyen,Amaury Habrard,Benjamin Guedj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对物理信息机器学习(PIML)的PAC-Bayesian框架，在无界损失下提供了高概率泛化保证，揭示了物理正则性与泛化之间的直接联系，并开发了可优化的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息机器学习虽然在实践中表现优异，但其统计泛化性质仍未被充分理解，特别是在回归设置下的无界损失场景。现有的分析依赖近似或稳定性论证，无法充分捕捉物理结构如何影响有限数据的泛化。

Method: 采用多任务视角将数据保真度、PDE残差、初始条件和边界条件联合处理，避免了标准联合界方法的松弛性。利用物理信息目标的结构推导出复杂度与损失的输入梯度范数相关的界限，在Sobolev和Poincaré型假设下实例化框架，并提出了自感知界限学习算法直接优化可处理的替代目标。

Result: 推导出了两类在统计复杂性和平滑性之间权衡的界限；提出的界限在标准PDE基准测试上是非空真的，显著优于联合界基线；开发的算法能够在训练中有效最小化这些界限；证明了物理正则性与泛化之间的直接联系。

Conclusion: 本研究为物理信息模型的泛化提供了原则性的统计基础，建立的PAC-Bayesian框架不仅提供了理论保证，还导出了实用的学习算法，填补了PIML领域统计泛化理论的重要空白。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) integrates mechanistic knowledge, typically in the form of partial differential equations (PDE), into data-driven models. Despite strong empirical performance, its statistical generalisation properties remain poorly understood, particularly in the regression setting with unbounded losses. Existing analyses rely on approximation or stability arguments and do not fully capture how physical structure influences generalisation from finite data. In this work, we develop a PAC-Bayesian framework for PIML that provides high-probability generalisation guarantees in the presence of unbounded losses. We adopt a multi-task perspective that jointly treats data fidelity, PDE residuals, initial and boundary conditions, avoiding the looseness induced by standard union-bound approaches. Our analysis leverages the structure of physics-informed objectives to derive novel bounds where the complexity scales with input-gradient norms of the losses, revealing a direct link between physical regularity and generalisation. We instantiate this framework under Sobolev and Poincaré-type assumptions, yielding two classes of bounds that trade off statistical complexity and smoothness in different regimes. Building on these results, we propose a self-bounding-aware learning algorithm that directly optimises tractable surrogates of the derived bounds, along with a practical procedure to estimate the associated constants in realistic settings. Empirical evaluations on standard PDE benchmarks demonstrate that our bounds are non-vacuous, significantly tighter than union-bound baselines, and can be effectively minimised during training. Overall, our results provide a principled statistical foundation for the generalisation of physics-informed models.

</details>
