By exploiting the min-max game structure, PANDA avoids computing UL hypergradients and does not require second-order information. We prove that PANDA converges to stationary points without convexity assumptions on either the UL or LL objectives. Moreover, PANDA reaches an $ε$-stationary point in $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-1})$ iterations with sample complexity $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-3})$, matching the best-known rates for bilevel RL with single-policy LL MDPs. Experiments demonstrate the superior performance of PANDA over closely related baselines.

</details>


### [74] [WINDQuant: Weight-Informed Neural Decision-Making for Global Mixed-Precision LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26660)
*Phong Nam Huu Nguyen,Khoi M. Le,Cong-Duy T Nguyen,Anh Tuan Luu,Thong Thanh Nguyen,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WINDQuant提出了一种基于强化学习（PPO）的超低比特LLM量化方法，通过在列块级别进行细粒度的混合精度分配，在全局存储预算下实现了有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM量化方法在超低比特场景下面临严峻挑战：训练后量化方法准确率严重下降，量化感知训练需要昂贵的重训练资源，且大多数混合精度策略依赖粗粒度或启发式的敏感度分析，忽视了权重矩阵内部的细粒度变化。

Method: WINDQuant是一个基于强化学习的分配控制器，不引入新的量化算子，而是学习如何在全局存储预算下为细粒度的列块分配比特宽度和量化处理。该方法结合了PPO算法、激活感知校准、轻量级单元量化器拟合以及对学习到的混合精度方案的有效比特数显式计算。

Result: 在LLaMA模型上的实验表明，WINDQuant在超低比特设置下实现了具有竞争力的性能，同时相比于基于重训练的方法减少了优化开销。

Conclusion: 强化学习是一种实用的自适应混合精度量化控制器，能够有效解决LLM超低比特量化中的细粒度精度分配问题。

Abstract: Quantization is an effective approach to reduce the memory footprint and inference cost of large language models (LLMs), yet maintaining performance in the ultra-low-bit regime remains challenging. Existing post-training methods often suffer from severe accuracy degradation, while quantization-aware training requires costly retraining and additional resources. Moreover, most mixed-precision strategies rely on coarse-grained or heuristic sensitivity analysis that overlooks fine-grained variations within weight matrices. We propose WINDQuant, a reinforcement-learning-based allocation controller for ultra-low-bit LLM quantization. Rather than introducing another low-level quantization operator, WINDQuant learns how to assign bit-widths and quantization treatments to fine-grained column chunks under a global storage budget. By operating at the column-chunk level, WINDQuant enables flexible and fine-grained precision assignment within layers under a global target bit-width. The implementation combines PPO with activation-aware calibration, lightweight per-unit quantizer fitting, and explicit effective-bit accounting of the learned mixed-precision plan. Experiments on LLaMA models demonstrate that WINDQuant achieves competitive performance in ultra-low-bit settings while reducing optimization overhead relative to retraining-based approaches, highlighting reinforcement learning as a practical controller for adaptive mixed-precision quantization.

</details>


### [75] [Beyond Trajectory-Level Attribution: Graph-Based Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26684)
*Xin Cheng,Shuo He,Lang Feng,HaiYang Xu,Ming Yan,Lei Feng,Bo An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphGPO方法，通过构建统一的状态转移图来捕捉全局信息，实现了细粒度的步骤级信用分配，显著提升了智能体任务训练效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于群体的强化学习方法在信用分配时过度依赖粗粒度的轨迹级归因（仅根据最终结果），难以捕捉个体步骤的贡献，尤其是那些隐藏在失败轨迹中的有价值步骤，导致无法充分利用潜在信息。

Method: 提出GraphGPO方法：(1) 将所有rollout轨迹聚合成统一的状态转移图；(2) 利用图中编码的全局信息估计每个状态到任务目标的距离；(3) 基于状态转移对目标距离的缩减程度计算基于图的优势函数，从而为每条边分配信用。

Result: GraphGPO显著提高了训练效率，并在多个具有挑战性的基准测试中达到了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过构建状态转移图并利用全局信息进行步骤级信用分配，GraphGPO有效解决了传统群体强化学习方法中信用分配粗糙的问题，为LLM智能体训练提供了更精确的优化框架。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods have achieved remarkable success in improving the performance of large language models (LLMs) and have been rapidly extended to agentic tasks. However, their credit assignment relies heavily on coarse-grained trajectory-level attribution according to final outcomes, making it difficult to capture the contribution of individual steps, such as valuable steps obscured within failed trajectories. To uncover latent information and enable more faithful step-level credit assignment, we propose Graph-based Group Policy Optimization (GraphGPO), which first aggregates all rollout trajectories into a unified state-transition graph and then estimates the distance from each state to the task goal using the global information encoded in the graph. Finally, GraphGPO assigns credit to each edge by estimating a graph-based advantage, based on how much the transition reduces the distance to the task goal. In this way, GraphGPO significantly improves training efficiency and achieves state-of-the-art performance across a range of challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [76] [Self-Improvement Imitation with Biologically Guided Search for Protein Design Under Oracle Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26690)
*Ashima Khanna,Dominik Grimm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SILO是一种基于轨迹级自改进模仿学习的蛋白质序列优化框架，通过分层编辑策略和无替换随机束搜索，在严格的oracle预算约束下，在8个测试场景中均达到最高适应度，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习和离策略生成方法在代理噪声下性能退化，且位置无关的突变提议可能破坏功能性关键残基，迫切需要一种能在有限oracle评估次数下高效探索组合空间且保护功能位点的蛋白质设计方法。

Method: SILO采用分层编辑策略，将每次突变分解为位置选择和残基选择两个步骤；通过无替换增量随机束搜索(SBS)生成候选轨迹，结合UCB代理集成和丙氨酸扫描适应度评分(AFS)筛选具有功能相关编辑的候选序列进行oracle评估；使用下一动作交叉熵模仿学习在每轮最优oracle标记轨迹上更新策略，避免价值函数估计。

Result: 在8个重现的蛋白质适应度景观和5个强基线方法的对比中，SILO在全部8个景观上均达到最高最大适应度和top-100平均适应度，且早期改进更快；在低数据和噪声代理压力测试中，当多个基线方法性能退化时，SILO保持竞争力或最优；消融实验表明SBS与AFS贡献主要增益，迭代模仿学习提供额外改进。

Conclusion: SILO成功解决了oracle预算约束下的蛋白质序列优化问题，通过分层策略、无替换搜索和功能导向筛选机制，实现了在多种测试场景下的最优性能，为受限评估预算的蛋白质设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Protein sequence optimization under tight oracle budgets requires methods that explore vast combinatorial spaces while making each evaluation informative. Existing reinforcement learning and off-policy generative approaches often degrade under surrogate noise, and position-agnostic mutation proposals risk disrupting functionally critical residues. We introduce SILO, a trajectory-level self-improvement imitation framework for oracle-budgeted protein design. SILO uses a hierarchical edit policy that decomposes each mutation into a position choice followed by a residue choice. In each active-learning round, the policy samples candidate trajectories via incremental stochastic beam search without replacement (SBS), and a UCB-based proxy ensemble, combined with an alanine-scan fitness score (AFS), selects candidates with functionally relevant edits for in silico oracle evaluation. The policy is then updated by next-action cross-entropy imitation on the round's best oracle-labeled trajectories, avoiding value-function estimation. Across eight reproduced protein fitness landscapes and five strong baselines from prior work, SILO achieves the highest maximum and top-100 mean fitness on 8 of 8 landscapes within our evaluations, often exhibiting faster early-stage improvement. In low-data and noisy-proxy stress tests on two landscapes per setting, SILO remains competitive or best when several baselines degrade. Ablations show that SBS with AFS account for much of the gains, with iterative imitation providing additional improvement. Code is available at: https://github.com/grimmlab/SILO.git

</details>


### [77] [Model Merging on Loss Landscape: A Geometry Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26693)
*Juanwu Lu,Anand Bhaskar,Brian Axelrod,Ekaterina Tolstaya,Tristan Emrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EpiMer框架，将模型合并建模为黎曼流形上的Fréchet均值求解问题，在低秩子空间内利用期望Hessian作为度量，建立了局部曲率与参数认知不确定性的联系，在8个图像分类任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法要么忽略损失景观的几何结构，要么依赖难以处理的完整空间Hessian近似，缺乏对模型参数几何特性的有效利用。

Method: 提出EpiMer框架：1）将模型合并建模为黎曼流形上Fréchet均值求解问题；2）将计算限制在由任务向量张成的低秩子空间内；3）使用期望Hessian作为度量张量，揭示局部曲率与参数认知不确定性的联系；4）理论分析将合并误差分解为子空间Fréchet方差和残差能量，并统一了曲率感知方法和谱方法。

Result: 在CLIP-ViT模型的8个图像分类任务合并实验中，EpiMer在所有三个CLIP-ViT骨干网络上均严格优于基线方法，在相同秩条件下提升了跨任务平均准确率和最差任务准确率。

Conclusion: EpiMer成功将模型合并与黎曼几何理论相结合，证明了曲率感知合并方法在理论上和实践中均优于平坦几何方法，为模型合并提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Model merging offers a promising avenue for knowledge integration and parallel development without retraining. Yet, existing methods either ignore the geometry of the loss landscape or rely on intractable full-space Hessian approximations. We propose EpiMer, a framework that casts model merging as solving the Fréchet mean on a Riemannian manifold and restricts the computation to a low-rank subspace spanned by the task vectors. With the expected Hessian as the metric, we reveal a connection between local curvature and epistemic uncertainty of the parameters. Our theoretical analysis decomposes the merging error bound into the subspace Fréchet variance and the residual energy, and provides a closed-form characterization of when curvature-aware merging provably outperforms flat-geometry methods. In addition, our framework unifies both curvature-aware methods and recent spectral methods as special cases of the subspace Fréchet mean with different geometric metrics. Merging fine-tuned CLIP-ViT models on eight image classification tasks, Epistemic Merging strictly outperforms the baselines on all three CLIP-ViT backbones at matched rank, improving the across-task average accuracy and worst-task accuracy on every backbone.

</details>


### [78] [SL-BiLEM: Structured Learnable Behavior-in-the-Loop Epidemic Modeling for Forecasting and Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26704)
*Haochun Wang,Sendong Zhao,Jingbo Wang,Yanrui Du,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SL-BiLEM框架，通过物理约束解决流行病预测中的分布偏移问题，在政策干预场景下预测准确度提升76%，并实现准确的反事实分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病预测面临一个根本性挑战：人类行为会动态响应疾病传播，形成反馈回路，在政策干预点引发分布偏移，导致纯数据驱动模型在分布偏移下不可靠。

Method: 提出结构化可学习行为闭环流行病模型(SL-BiLEM)，将有效传播率分解为β_eff(t,g) = β_0(g) × m_policy(t) × m_media(t) × m_comp(t,g)，对学习到的依从性函数施加单调性、平滑性和有界跳跃约束，确保在新政策机制下保持预测有效性。

Result: 在三个真实数据集和合成基准上验证：(1)相比神经-机制基线提升76%，在政策诱导偏移下OOD退化仅53%而神经基线达1142%；(2)在27个合成反事实实验中bootstrap置信区间覆盖率达100%；(3)处理效应准确度超过0.85。

Conclusion: SL-BiLEM建立了面向公共卫生决策者的可解释工具，能够提供准确预测和有原则的干预规划支持。

Abstract: Epidemic forecasting faces a fundamental challenge: human behavior dynamically responds to disease spread, creating feedback loops that induce distribution shifts at policy intervention points. This renders data-driven models unreliable under distribution shift. We propose \textbf{SL-BiLEM} (Structured Learnable Behavior-in-the-Loop Epidemic Model), leveraging physical constraints as regularization for robust extrapolation. The framework decomposes effective transmission as $β_{\text{eff}}(t,g) = β_0(g) \times m_{\text{policy}}(t) \times m_{\text{media}}(t) \times m_{\text{comp}}(t,g)$, where monotonicity, smoothness, and bounded-jump constraints on the learned compliance function maintain predictive validity under novel policy regimes. Beyond forecasting, SL-BiLEM enables counterfactual analysis for intervention decision support. We validate forecasting on three real-world datasets (cruise ship, school influenza, and school-district COVID-19 surveillance) and evaluate counterfactual recovery on synthetic benchmarks with known ground truth. SL-BiLEM demonstrates: (1) 76\% improvement over neural-mechanistic baselines, with only 53\% OOD degradation versus 1142\% for neural baselines under policy-induced shift; (2) 100\% bootstrap CI coverage across 27 synthetic counterfactual experiments; and (3) Treatment Effect Accuracy exceeding 0.85. These results establish SL-BiLEM as an interpretable tool for public health decision-makers seeking accurate prediction and principled intervention planning.

</details>


### [79] [Image Feature Fusion-based Federated Client Unlearning (FCU)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26715)
*Hangyi Shen,Yizhi Pan,Tiansuo Li,Weiqi Jiang,Guanqun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IFF-FCU方法，通过图像特征融合机制（Mixup）在联邦遗忘中平衡遗忘有效性与模型泛化能力，有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘技术面临灾难性遗忘问题：在删除目标知识时，往往会同时破坏需要保留的知识，损害模型的全局泛化能力。需要在不遗忘效果与模型性能之间取得更好平衡。

Method: 提出Image Feature Fusion-based Federated Client Unlearning (IFF-FCU)方法，引入线性图像特征融合机制（Mixup）动态创建混合样本，弥合遗忘分布与保留分布之间的差距，从理论上拓宽并正则化遗忘边界。

Result: 在医学影像基准数据集（RSNA-ICH和ISIC2018）上的广泛实验表明，该方法实现了良好的遗忘效果。在ICH数据集上，IFF-FCU相对于重训练金标准的错误偏差具有高度竞争力，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: IFF-FCU方法通过图像特征融合机制有效缓解了联邦遗忘中的灾难性遗忘问题，在遗忘有效性与模型泛化能力之间取得了良好平衡，为数据保护法规中的"被遗忘权"提供了更优的技术实现方案。

Abstract: Major data protection regulations all mention the "right to be forgotten," and that's what pushed federated unlearning (FU) techniques forward. But one stubborn issue remains: catastrophic forgetting--you erase the target knowledge, yet somehow you also end up throwing out essential retained knowledge, which then hurts the model's global generalization.
  To get a better balance between unlearning effectiveness and generalization ability, we propose something called Image Feature Fusion-based Federated Client Unlearning (IFF-FCU). The idea is to bring in a linear Image Feature Fusion mechanism (Mixup) that dynamically creates mixed samples, bridging the gap between forget-distribution and retain-distribution. What this strategy does isn't just deleting a few discrete data points--it theoretically widens and regularizes the forgetting boundary.
  We ran extensive experiments on medical imaging benchmarks (RSNA-ICH and ISIC2018), and the results show that our approach achieves reasonably good unlearning. For instance, on the ICH dataset, IFF-FCU achieves a highly competitive Error deviation from the retrained gold standard, demonstrating robust improvements over existing baselines.

</details>


### [80] [MTL-FNO: A Lightweight Multi-Task Fourier Neural Operator for Sparse Field Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26718)
*Siyu Ye,Shihang Li,Zhiqiang Gong,Benrong Zhang,Weien Zhou,Yiyong Huang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了轻量级多任务傅里叶神经算子(MTL-FNO)，通过硬参数共享和低秩任务特定参数实现模型压缩，采用极坐标分解和Cayley变换优化策略，在保持精度的同时将模型大小减少76%和60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在航空航天器自主运行的多场稀疏重建中面临两个关键挑战：(1)部署多个独立模型导致模型尺寸急剧增长；(2)无法利用场间相关性，特别是在少样本条件下性能受限。

Method: 提出轻量级多任务傅里叶神经算子(MTL-FNO)：采用硬参数共享框架，每层参数分为共享和任务特定组件，任务特定参数以低秩项实现压缩。从极坐标视角重新审视FNO的谱权重，通过极分解将谱权重解耦为相位信息(酉张量)和幅度信息(半正定张量)，并引入Cayley变换对酉张量进行重参数化，将约束优化问题转化为无约束问题。

Result: 在两个代表性工程案例验证下，MTL-FNO在小样本条件下实现了与标准FNO相当甚至更优的精度，同时总模型大小分别减少了76%和60%。

Conclusion: MTL-FNO成功解决了多场重建中的模型尺寸增长和任务冲突问题，通过物理意义明确的解耦优化策略有效缓解了任务冲突，为航空航天器机载多场稀疏重建提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient onboard multi-field sparse reconstruction is essential for the autonomous operation of aerospace vehicles. While existing deep learning models exhibit promise for single-field reconstruction, deploying multiple independent models leads to prohibitive model size growth and fails to exploit cross-field correlations, particularly under few-shot conditions. To address these challenges, we first propose a lightweight multi-task Fourier neural operator (MTL-FNO), an end-to-end joint training framework based on hard parameter sharing. In each layer, the parameters are divided into shared and task-specific components to capture common features across fields while preserving task-specific characteristics. Moreover, the task-specific fine-tuning parameters are implemented as low-rank terms, achieving substantial model compression. Second, to address the difficulty of co-optimizing shared and task-specific parameters along with their real and imaginary parts, we revisit the FNO's spectral weight from a polar-form perspective and devise a physically meaningful decoupled optimization scheme. Specifically, we apply polar decomposition to slice-wise disentangle the spectral weight into a unitary tensor encoding phase information and a positive semi-definite tensor characterizing amplitude. By decoupling the optimization of phase and amplitude, our method can effectively mitigate tasks conflict. Meanwhile, to preserve unitary geometric fidelity during training, the Cayley transform is introduced to reparameterize the unitary tensor, converting the constrained optimization problem to an unconstrained one. Finally, the effectiveness of the proposed method under few-shot conditions is validated on two representative engineering cases. Results show that MTL-FNO achieves accuracy comparable to or even surpassing that of standard FNO, while reducing total model size by 76% and 60%, respectively.

</details>


### [81] [APEX: Amplitude Anchors and Phase Priors for Target-Scarce Higher-Frequency Wave Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26732)
*Yifan Sun,Lei Cheng,Sijie Chen,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出APEX框架解决高频波场预测中目标监督稀缺的问题，通过保留低频预测的幅度作为结构锚点，并结合格林函数启发的相位先验来增强重建高频波场，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习代理模型在观测频率范围内表现良好，但在目标监督稀缺的高频预测方面研究不足。核心难点在于跨频率迁移的内在不对称性：粗粒度幅度结构在不同频率间保持相对稳定，而相位敏感的振荡结构随频率增加而快速退化。此外，高频数据的仿真或测量成本远高于低频数据。

Method: 提出APEX框架：(1)首先使用低频神经算子在目标频率域提供粗粒度预测，仅保留其幅度作为可迁移的结构锚点；(2)设计条件流匹配增强器，在格林函数启发的相位先验指导下重建目标高频场。该方法显式重用可迁移的粗粒度结构，同时独立恢复缺失的振荡细节。

Result: 在SimpleWave、Helmholtz和Maxwell基准测试上，APEX在有限目标频率监督下始终优于直接低频到高频外推、目标自适应算子和联合生成基线方法。实验验证了该方法在目标监督稀缺场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 振荡波场的可靠高频预测不应依赖于复数场的直接端到端迁移，而应显式重用可迁移的粗粒度结构，同时独立恢复缺失的振荡细节。这一发现为波场预测领域提供了新的方法论指导。

Abstract: Learning-based surrogates have become increasingly effective for wave-field prediction, and neural operators in particular have shown strong performance within observed frequency regimes. However, higher-frequency prediction under scarce target supervision remains comparatively underexplored, especially in wave problems where higher-frequency data are substantially more expensive to simulate or measure than lower-frequency data. A central difficulty is that cross-frequency transfer is inherently asymmetric: coarse amplitude structure remains relatively stable across frequencies, whereas phase-sensitive oscillatory structure deteriorates much more rapidly as frequency increases. Motivated by this asymmetry, we propose APEX, Amplitude-anchored and Phase-prior-guided Enhancement from eXtrapolated coarse predictions, a framework for target-scarce higher-frequency wave-field prediction. A lower-frequency neural operator first provides a coarse prediction in the target-frequency regime, from which we retain only the amplitude as a transferable structural anchor. A conditional flow-matching enhancer then reconstructs the target higher-frequency field under the guidance of a Green's-function-inspired phase prior. Experiments on SimpleWave, Helmholtz, and Maxwell benchmarks show that APEX consistently outperforms direct lower-to-higher extrapolation, target-adapted operator, and joint generative baselines under limited target-frequency supervision. Our results suggest that reliable higher-frequency prediction of oscillatory wave fields should not rely on direct end-to-end transfer of the full complex field, but instead on explicitly reusing transferable coarse structure while separately recovering the missing oscillatory detail.

</details>


### [82] [Stabilizing Recurrent Dynamics for Test-Time Scalable Latent Reasoning in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26733)
*Xiao-Wen Yang,Ziyu Han,Xi-Hua Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Jie-Jing Shao,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对循环语言模型在测试时扩展中存在的性能崩塌问题，提出STARS训练框架，通过雅可比谱半径正则化约束潜在状态收敛至稳定不动点，实现了可靠的测试时扩展和峰值性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环语言模型虽然能通过深度递归实现高效的潜在推理，但存在不可靠的测试时扩展行为：性能常在某个迭代深度达到峰值后，随着进一步递归而崩塌。潜在动力学分析揭示了现有架构和策略中稳定性与有效性之间存在固有的权衡。

Method: 将推理概念化为不确定性降低过程，提出STARS（稳定性驱动的递归扩展）训练框架，通过雅可比谱半径正则化约束潜在状态逼近渐近稳定不动点。该方法采用随机循环采样实现高效计算，在确保严格稳定性的同时最大化有效性。

Result: 在算术任务上，STARS实现了可靠的测试时扩展；在复杂数学推理任务上，随着递归深度增加，STARS显著缓解了性能退化问题，同时提升了峰值性能。

Conclusion: 通过将潜在状态约束至稳定不动点，STARS成功解决了循环语言模型中的性能崩塌问题，为实现可靠的测试时扩展提供了有效途径，证明了稳定性与有效性可以协同优化。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) enable efficient latent reasoning through depth recurrence, yet exhibit unreliable test-time scaling behavior: performance often peaks at a certain iteration depth and then collapses with further recurrence. Through latent dynamics analysis, we find an inherent trade-off between stability and effectiveness in existing architectures and strategies. By conceptualizing reasoning as uncertainty reduction, we propose that convergence toward stable fixed points while preserving effectiveness represents a promising way. To this end, we propose STARS (STAbility-driven Recurrent Scaling), a training framework that constrains latent states to approach asymptotically stable fixed points. This is realized via efficient Jacobian Spectral Radius Regularization with random loop sampling, enabling STARS to maximize effectiveness while ensuring rigorous stability. Experiments on arithmetic tasks show that STARS achieves reliable test-time scaling, and on complex mathematical reasoning it substantially mitigates performance degradation as recurrence depth increases while also improving peak performance.

</details>


### [83] [Localizing Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Coordinate-Wise Curvature Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26756)
*Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于曲率差异的方法来定位扩散模型中的局部记忆化现象，通过消除数据内在约束的影响，实现了比现有注意力方法更准确的记忆化区域检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型可能会无意中记忆训练样本，引发隐私和版权担忧。现有的记忆化检测方法通常依赖全局或模型特定信号，对记忆化在生成图像中的具体位置提供有限洞察。此外，局部方差坍缩可能源于数据内在约束而非过拟合，需要区分这两种情况。

Method: 1. 将局部记忆化几何表征为坐标级方差坍缩；2. 提出曲率差异方法，通过减去欠拟合基线（无条件模型或训练较少的版本）的曲率来隔离过拟合驱动的记忆化；3. 推导出分数差异代理，为广泛使用的基于分数差异的检测指标提供几何解释。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion上的实验表明，与地面真实记忆化掩码对比，本文方法优于先前的基于注意力的定位方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过几何方法表征和检测扩散模型的局部记忆化现象，能够有效区分过拟合驱动的记忆化和数据内在约束，为记忆化检测提供了更精确的定位能力。

Abstract: Diffusion models can unintentionally memorize training samples, raising concerns about privacy and copyright. While recent methods can detect memorization, they often rely on global or model-specific signals and provide limited insight into where memorization appears within a generated image. We provide a geometric characterization of local memorization as a coordinate-wise variance collapse. However, such collapse can also arise from intrinsic data constraints rather than overfitting. To isolate overfitting-driven memorization, we propose curvature-difference methods that subtract the curvature of an underfitted baseline, either the unconditional model or a less-trained version of itself. We further derive a score-difference proxy that provides a geometric explanation for the widely used score-difference-based detection metric. Experiments on Stable Diffusion, evaluated against ground-truth memorization masks, show that our method outperforms the prior attention-based localization method. Code is available at https://github.com/Gwangho99/mem-curv-diff.

</details>


### [84] [Time Series Causal Discovery via Context-Conditioned and Causality-Augmented Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26759)
*Biao Ouyang,Tengxue Zhang,Zhihao Zhuang,Yang Shu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PTCD，一个用于时间序列因果发现的预训练框架，通过上下文条件建模和可迁移因果增强技术，显著提升了跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列因果发现方法通常依赖特定数据集的优化，难以将因果发现能力迁移到由不同因果机制支配的新时间序列数据中，缺乏跨任务的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了PTCD预训练框架，核心包括：1）双尺度迭代注意力机制捕获窗口级因果关系；2）带有上下文级路由机制的高斯混合模型处理异质外生分布；3）在合成数据集上采用预训练范式，结合干预学习和因果混合策略来处理因果图间的分布偏移。

Result: 在多个真实世界的分布外(OOD)数据集上的广泛实验表明，PTCD在因果发现和根因识别任务上均表现出色，证明了其强大的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: PTCD成功解决了时间序列因果发现中的跨任务泛化难题，通过预训练范式和创新的建模技术实现了稳定的因果发现性能，为实际应用中的异常根因追踪提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Causal discovery from time series is critical for many real-world applications, such as tracing the root causes of anomalies. Existing approaches typically rely on dataset-specific optimization, making it difficult to transfer their causal discovery capabilities to new time series governed by diverse causal mechanisms. In this paper, we propose \textbf{PTCD}, a novel \textbf{P}retraining framework for \textbf{T}ime-series \textbf{C}ausal \textbf{D}iscovery, which improves cross-task generalization through context-conditioned modeling and transferable causal augmentation. To model complex temporal causal dependencies, PTCD employs a dual-scale iterative attention mechanism to capture window-level causal relationships, and a Gaussian mixture with a context-level routing mechanism to handle heterogeneous exogenous distributions. To further address distribution shifts across causal graphs, PTCD adopts a pretraining paradigm on synthetic datasets that integrates intervention-based learning and a causal mixup strategy, promoting stable causal discovery and stronger generalization. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world out-of-distribution (OOD) datasets demonstrate that PTCD excels in both causal discovery and root cause identification.

</details>


### [85] [Adversarial Training for Robust Coverage Network under Worst-case Facility Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26763)
*Changhao Miao,Yuntian Zhang,Tongyu Wu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对抗学习的双智能体深度强化学习（DADRL）框架，用于求解计算上难以处理的最大覆盖位置-阻断问题（MCLIP），实现了卓越的计算效率和极具竞争力的解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大覆盖位置-阻断问题（MCLIP）是韧性基础设施规划中的核心双层优化问题，但由于上下层之间的强耦合性以及各自的高度组合复杂性，传统方法难以有效求解，存在计算不可行的问题。

Method: 提出了双智能体深度强化学习（DADRL）框架：1）设计位置智能体和阻断智能体分别对应上下层，通过对抗学习让位置智能体在训练中对抗不断演进的阻断智能体；2）提出基于代理模型的集成推理策略，利用训练好的阻断智能体作为高保真代理来指导位置智能体的决策。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法相比其他基线方法在保持极具竞争力的解质量的同时，实现了卓越的计算效率。框架对网络结构具有模型无关性。

Conclusion: DADRL框架成功解决了MCLIP问题的计算难题，其底层的对抗学习范式为求解其他双层优化问题展示了强大的潜力，具有良好的通用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The Maximal Covering Location-Interdiction Problem (MCLIP) is a classic bi-level optimization problem, which is fundamental to resilient infrastructure planning yet remains computationally intractable. Specifically, the upper level determines facility locations to maximize coverage, while the lower level executes worst-case interdiction to minimize the coverage. The strong coupling between the upper and lower levels, combined with their respective high combinatorial complexity, renders traditional methods ineffective. To bridge this gap, we propose a Dual-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning (DADRL) framework based on adversarial learning, comprising a location agent corresponding to the upper level and an interdiction agent corresponding to the lower level. Our contributions are threefold: (1) The location agent is trained simultaneously against an evolving interdiction agent, making it effectively capture the dynamic competitive interplay between the upper and lower levels; (2) To fully exploit the learned capabilities of the interdiction agent, we propose a Surrogate-based Ensemble Inference Strategy that utilizes the trained interdiction agent as a high-fidelity surrogate to guide the decisions of location agent; (3) Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior computational efficiency while maintaining highly competitive solution quality compared to other baselines. Furthermore, our DADRL framework is model-agnostic to network structures, while its underlying adversarial learning paradigm demonstrates strong potential for solving other bi-level optimization problems.

</details>


### [86] [Towards Generalization-Oriented Models for Vehicle Routing Problems with Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26776)
*Changhao Miao,Yuntian Zhang,Tongyu Wu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出R2E-IG框架，通过残差精炼专家、实例级门控机制和混合分布训练，解决了深度强化学习在车辆路径问题中的分布泛化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习方法在车辆路径问题上的训练通常依赖于均匀分布生成的实例，这限制了模型在现实世界分布偏移下的性能表现，因此需要开发具有跨分布泛化能力的模型。

Method: 提出了三个核心方法：(1) 残差精炼专家(R2E)架构，通过残差精炼增强专家表达能力；(2) 实例级门控机制，学习分布感知的实例表示并将输入路由到合适的模块；(3) 配备动态权重自适应(DWA)的混合分布训练机制，动态重加权来自不同分布的训练数据以强调更具信息量的样本。

Result: 在合成数据集和基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明R2E-IG在分布内和分布外实例上均达到了与最先进基线方法竞争的性能，且该方法具有通用性，可以轻松集成到现有的DRL方法中以进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: R2E-IG成功解决了VRP问题中的分布泛化挑战，通过模块化策略网络和自适应重组机制实现了良好的跨分布性能，为DRL在实际VRP应用中的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has achieved substantial progress on Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs). However, existing DRL-based methods are typically trained on instances generated from a uniform distribution, which limits their performance under real-world distribution shifts. In this paper, we aim to develop a generalization-oriented model that partitions the policy network into multiple modules and adaptively recombines modules to form specific policies during inference. Specifically, we propose Residual Refined Experts with Instance-level Gating (R2E-IG) to improve cross-distribution generalization. Our contributions are threefold: (1) We introduce a Residual Refined Expert (R2E) architecture that enhance expert expressiveness via residual refinement; (2) We design an instance-level gating mechanism that learns distribution-aware instance representations and routes inputs to suitable modules; (3) We propose a mixed-distribution training mechanism equipped with Dynamic Weight Adaption (DWA), which dynamically reweights training data from different distributions to emphasize more informative ones. Extensive experiments show that R2E-IG achieves competitive performance against state-of-the-art baselines on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution instances across synthetic and benchmark datasets. Moreover, R2E-IG is generic and can be easily integrated into existing DRL-based methods to further improve performance.

</details>


### [87] [Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26784)
*Yu Luo,Shuo Han,Yihan Hu,Lei Lv,Huaping Liu,Fuchun Sun,Jianye Hao,Dong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R²VPO算法，通过约束策略比率方差替代启发式裁剪，在LLM推理和机器人控制任务中显著提升性能和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准on-policy强化学习依赖启发式裁剪来强制信任区域，但这种机制通过不加区分地截断高回报但高散度的更新，施加了严重的代价，导致关键梯度信号丢失。

Method: 提出R²VPO（比率方差正则化策略优化）方法，明确约束策略比率方差作为信任区域约束的原则性局部近似，通过原始-对偶优化框架实现该约束，替代二元硬裁剪机制。

Result: 在7个LLM规模（涵盖快速和慢速推理范式）和10个机器人控制任务上验证了方法的通用性。R²VPO在数学推理基准上获得显著性能提升（小模型改进尤为明显），样本效率显著提高；在连续控制领域持续优于PPO基线，特别是在稀疏奖励和动态环境中。

Conclusion: 比率方差正则化为稳定且数据高效的策略优化建立了原则性基础，可作为信任区域约束的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Standard on-policy reinforcement learning relies on heuristic clipping to enforce trust regions, but this mechanism imposes a severe cost by indiscriminately truncating high-return yet high-divergence updates. We demonstrate that explicitly constraining the policy ratio variance provides a principled local approximation to trust-region constraints, eliminating the need for binary hard clipping. By acting as a distributional ``soft brake'', this approach preserves critical gradient signals from novel discoveries while naturally down-weighting and enabling the reuse of stale, off-policy data. We introduce ${\bf R}^2{\bf VPO}$ (Ratio-Variance Regularized Policy Optimization), which implements this constraint via a primal-dual optimization framework. Extensive evaluations across $7$ LLM scales, spanning both fast and slow reasoning paradigms, and $10$ robotic control tasks demonstrate the generality of the proposed approach. R$^2$VPO achieves substantial performance gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, with particularly pronounced improvements on smaller models, while significantly improving sample efficiency. Furthermore, it consistently outperforms PPO baselines in continuous control domains, particularly in sparse-reward and dynamic environments. Together, these findings establish ratio-variance regularization as a principled foundation for stable and data-efficient policy optimization.

</details>


### [88] [Pretrained Approximators for Low-Thrust Trajectory Cost and Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26790)
*Zhong Zhang,Giacomo Acciarini,Dario Izzo,Hexi Baoyin,Francesco Topputo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了基于机器学习代理模型的方法，可以快速准确地预测低推力轨迹优化中的燃料消耗和转移可行性，并发现了性能与数据规模、网络参数呈对数线性关系的缩放定律，实现了跨轨道环境的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低推力轨迹设计严重依赖重复评估燃料消耗和转移可行性，而传统方法需要昂贵的最优控制求解，计算成本高且难以扩展到大规模场景。研究旨在通过机器学习代理模型来加速这一过程，实现快速且可扩展的评估。

Method: 1）提出homotopy-ray策略构建大规模数据集以适应任务设计需求；2）引入自相似变换（self-similar transformation），使模型能够跨半长轴、倾角和中心天体泛化，避免重新训练；3）通过增加数据集规模和模型容量，验证低推力轨迹优化遵循缩放定律（性能随训练数据和网络参数的对数线性提升）。

Result: 所提出的神经网络代理模型能够准确预测单圈和多圈转移的最优燃料消耗和最小转移时间，在公共数据集、全球轨迹优化竞赛的多小行星飞掠问题以及小行星交会任务设计中均展示了良好的性能和泛化能力，且在探索范围内未观察到性能饱和现象。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了机器学习代理模型在低推力轨迹优化中的有效性和可扩展性，通过自相似变换实现了跨轨道环境的泛化能力，模型和数据集已开源发布以支持航天社区应用。

Abstract: Low-thrust trajectory design relies heavily on repeated evaluations of fuel consumption and transfer feasibility, which require expensive optimal control solutions. In this work, we show these quantities can be accurately approximated by machine learning surrogates, enabling fast and scalable evaluation across a wide range of scenarios. By increasing both dataset size and model capacity, we observe that low-thrust trajectory optimization follows a scaling law, with performance improving linearly with the logarithm of training data and network parameters, and no evidence of saturation within the explored regime. Guided by this observation, we construct a large-scale dataset using the proposed homotopy-ray strategy tailored to mission design requirements. A key is the introduction of a self-similar transformation, which allows generalization across semi-major axes, inclinations, and central bodies avoiding retraining. As a result, the same neural approximator can be applied to diverse orbital environments and mission classes. The proposed models accurately predict optimal fuel consumption and minimum transfer time for single- and multi-revolution transfers. Their performance and generalization are demonstrated on a public dataset, a multi-asteroid flyby problem from the Global Trajectory Optimization Competition, and an asteroid rendezvous mission design. The models and datasets are released as open-source to support the space community.

</details>


### [89] [Latent Recurrent Transformer: Architecture Exploration, Training Strategies, and Scaling Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26797)
*Zeyi Huang,Xuehai He,LiLiang Ren,Yiping Wang,Baolin Peng,Hao Cheng,Shuohang Wang,Pengcheng He,Jianfeng Gao,Yong Jae Lee,Yelong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜循环Transformer（LRT），通过复用高层源层隐藏状态作为循环记忆，在不增加暂停令牌或额外深度循环的情况下，以仅增加0.3%参数的代价，在匹配计算量下显著提升语言建模和上下文学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归Transformer缺乏跨位置的循环记忆机制，而现有方法通常需要插入暂停令牌或额外深度循环，增加了推理延迟和训练复杂度。本文旨在设计一种轻量级增强方法，在不破坏标准注意力机制和KV缓存接口的前提下引入循环记忆。

Method: LRT通过复用前一令牌的高层源层隐藏状态作为下一令牌的循环记忆，构建跨层循环潜路径。提出交错并行训练策略：先用单次全序列前向传播构建共享缓冲区，然后并行优化不相交的位置子集并写回，使所有令牌都能接受循环记忆感知监督，计算开销约为基线的2倍。

Result: 在nanochat风格主干网络和广泛的令牌-参数预算范围内，LRT在匹配有效计算量下同时改善了语言建模损失和上下文学习能力，仅增加约0.3%的参数。

Conclusion: LRT成功地将循环记忆机制轻量级地集成到自回归Transformer中，在保持标准注意力机制和KV缓存接口的同时，以极小的参数开销实现了性能提升，为Transformer架构的循环增强提供了一种高效方案。

Abstract: We study Latent Recurrent Transformer (LRT), a lightweight augmentation of autoregressive transformers that reuses a high-level source-layer hidden state from the previous token as recurrent memory for the next token. Because this source state is already computed during ordinary decoding, LRT adds a cross-layer recurrent latent pathway across positions without inserting pause tokens or extra depth loops, and the standard attention mechanism and KV-cache interface are preserved. To pretrain this recurrence at scale without sequentially unrolling the transformer, we introduce interleaved parallel training: a single full-sequence initialization forward pass builds a shared buffer; then disjoint position subsets are refined in parallel and written back, so that all tokens receive recurrent-memory-aware supervision at roughly 2 times baseline compute. Across nanochat style backbones and a wide range of tokens-per-parameter budgets, LRT improves both language-modeling loss and in-context learning under matched effective compute while adding as little as 0.3% parameters.

</details>


### [90] [PATE-TabTransGAN: Differentially Private Synthetic Tabular Data Generation via Transformer-Based Student Discrimination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26802)
*M. Youssef,M. Woźniak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATE-TabTransGAN是一种结合PATE机制与Transformer判别器的差分隐私表格数据合成框架，在四个基准数据集上实现了最佳或并列最佳的AUROC性能，有效平衡了隐私保护与数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私表格数据合成方法存在核心矛盾：提供强理论保护的方法牺牲了特征间依赖关系的建模能力，而擅长捕获复杂列关系的方法仅提供经验性隐私保证，缺乏能同时满足强隐私保护和高质量合成的统一框架。

Method: 提出PATE-TabTransGAN框架：(1)使用在互不相交数据分区上训练的Logistic Regression集成教师模型；(2)通过噪声聚合标签监督基于Transformer的学生判别器；(3)采用GNMax RDP记账器实现数值稳定的隐私核算；(4)残差生成器针对差分隐私学生模型进行优化，通过后处理继承(ε, δ)-DP保证。

Result: 在四个表格基准数据集(Adult, Breast, Cardio, Cervical)上的实验表明：(1)AUROC指标在所有四个数据集上达到最佳或并列最佳；(2)AUCPR在Cardio上匹配最强基线，在Cervical上领先，在Breast上略逊；(3)Adult数据集上的AUCPR差距源于评估管道中正类约定差异，而非合成质量缺陷。

Conclusion: PATE-TabTransGAN成功解决了差分隐私表格数据合成中隐私保护与合成质量的两难困境，通过PATE机制与Transformer架构的结合实现了形式化差分隐私保证下的高质量表格数据合成，在多个基准上优于当前最先进方法。

Abstract: Generating high-fidelity synthetic tabular data under formal differential privacy guarantees remains an open challenge. Methods that provide strong theoretical protection typically sacrifice the modeling of inter-feature dependencies required for realistic synthesis, while architectures that excel at capturing complex column relationships offer only empirical privacy guarantees. We present PATE-TabTransGAN, a generative framework that integrates the Private Aggregation of Teacher Ensembles (PATE) mechanism with a Transformer-based student discriminator to jointly address both requirements, and employs a GNMax RDP accountant for numerically stable privacy accounting. An ensemble of Logistic Regression teachers trained on disjoint partitions supervise the student via noisy-aggregated labels, and a residual generator is optimized against this differentially private student, inheriting formal (ε, δ)-DP guarantees by post-processing. PATE-TabTransGAN was compared with PATE-GAN, DP-GAN, and DP-CTGAN, considered state-of-the-art in differentially private tabular synthesis. Experiments conducted on four tabular benchmarks (Adult, Breast, Cardio, Cervical) confirmed the high quality of the proposed method: PATE-TabTransGAN attains the best or tied-best AUROC on all four datasets. On AUCPR it matches the strongest baseline on Cardio, leads on Cervical, and trails on Breast; on Adult, we demonstrate that AUCPR is highly sensitive to positive-class convention, and that the observed gap is consistent with a convention difference between evaluation pipelines rather than a synthesis deficit.

</details>


### [91] [Innovation: An Almost Characterization of Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26808)
*Nishant P. Das,Piyush Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入“创新性(innovation)”属性来刻画大语言模型的幻觉现象，证明创新性几乎是幻觉的充要条件，并基于此给出了新的幻觉率下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明校准的LLMs不可避免会产生幻觉，但缺乏对其内在机制的深入理解。本文旨在回答两个核心问题：(1)校准LLM的何种属性导致幻觉不可避免？(2)放弃校准是否可以避免幻觉？

Method: 引入“创新性(innovation)”这一新属性来衡量模型产生训练数据之外输出的倾向性。通过理论分析证明创新性与幻觉之间的双向关系，并将创新率与创新性相联系，进而建立与缺失质量(missing mass)的联系。

Result: 证明创新性是Kalai和Vempala所识别幻觉条件的必然结果；建立了创新性与幻觉的几乎充要关系：幻觉蕴含创新性，创新性以高概率蕴含幻觉；基于创新率给出了幻觉率的下界；通过将创新率与缺失质量相关联，获得了基于缺失质量的新幻觉率下界，扩展了Kalai和Vempala的结果。

Conclusion: 创新性是理解和刻画LLM幻觉现象的关键属性，它几乎完全刻画了幻觉现象。这一发现不仅解释了为何校准模型会产生幻觉，还为预测和控制幻觉率提供了新的理论工具和下界。

Abstract: Hallucination is a central limitation of large language models (LLMs), and substantial effort has been devoted to understanding and mitigating it. Towards this, Kalai and Vempala (STOC 2024) introduced a probabilistic framework formalizing calibration and hallucination, and showed that, with high probability, calibrated LLMs hallucinate roughly at the rate of the "missing mass", a measure of how incomplete the training data is relative to its source. This raises two fundamental questions: (i) what property of a calibrated LLM makes hallucinations unavoidable? and (ii) can hallucinations be avoided by giving up calibration? We answer these questions by introducing a simpler property we call innovation that measures the tendency of a model to produce outputs outside the training data. We show that innovation is implied by the condition for hallucination identified by Kalai and Vempala, and, further, that it is an almost characterization of hallucination: hallucination implies innovation, and conversely, innovation implies hallucination with high probability. We also provide lower bounds on the hallucination rate based on the "innovation rate", and by relating innovation rate back to missing mass, we obtain new hallucination rate lower bounds based on missing mass that extend the results of Kalai and Vempala.

</details>


### [92] [The Kalman Evolve: Closing the Gap in Kalman Filtering via Interpretable Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26830)
*Vasileios Saketos,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kalman Evolve框架，通过联合优化噪声参数和更新结构来改进卡尔曼滤波器，在多普勒雷达、激光雷达定位等任务中，相比优化卡尔曼滤波器基线，RMSE最高降低12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波器在线性动态、高斯噪声和已知噪声协方差假设下是最优的，但在多普勒雷达、激光雷达等现实感知场景中，这些假设常不成立，导致固有非线性问题。仅调整噪声协方差参数无法消除性能差距，最优估计器本质上是非线性的。

Method: 提出Kalman Evolve框架，利用大语言模型(LLM)作为程序空间的结构化先验，联合优化噪声参数和更新结构。该方法生成可解释的、对经典卡尔曼滤波器的非仿射修改，同时保持其递归形式，实现结构感知的更新。

Result: 在合成和真实世界跟踪基准测试中（包括多普勒雷达、激光雷达定位和行人跟踪），发现的算法一致性地改进了强大的基线方法（如优化卡尔曼滤波器），RMSE最高降低12%。提供了分析结果，证明了仿射估计器在常见非线性感知模型下的次优性。

Conclusion: 优化卡尔曼滤波器的结构（而非仅优化参数）为改进状态估计提供了一种实用且可解释的方法，在处理非线性感知场景时具有显著优势。

Abstract: State estimation is a fundamental problem in control and signal processing, for which the Kalman Filter provides an optimal solution under linear dynamics, Gaussian noise, and known noise covariances. However, these assumptions often fail in realistic sensing settings such as Doppler radar and LiDAR. In these cases, the optimal estimator is inherently nonlinear, which leads to systematic performance degradation. This creates a performance gap that cannot be eliminated by tuning the noise covariance parameters (i.e., the process and measurement noise in the Kalman Filter) alone. To address this limitation, we propose Kalman Evolve, a framework for discovering improved filtering algorithms by jointly optimizing both noise parameters and the update structure. Our approach leverages large language models (LLMs) as a structured prior over program space, enabling the generation of interpretable, non-affine modifications to the classical Kalman filter while preserving its recursive form. We provide analytical results establishing the suboptimality of affine estimators under common nonlinear sensing models, motivating the need for structure-aware updates. Across a range of synthetic and real-world tracking benchmarks, including Doppler radar, LiDAR-based localization, and pedestrian tracking, the discovered algorithms consistently improve over strong baselines such as the Optimized Kalman Filter, achieving up to 12\% reduction in RMSE. These results suggest that optimizing the structure of the Kalman filter, rather than only its parameters, provides a practical and interpretable way to improve state estimation.

</details>


### [93] [Periodic Topological Deep Learning for Polymer Design and Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26833)
*Yasharth Yadav,Tze Kwang Gerald Er,Atsushi Goto,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Periodic-TDL深度学习框架，通过周期性Vietoris-Rips复形和分层单纯形消息传递编码器捕获多尺度多体相互作用，显著提升了聚合物性能预测精度，并成功预测验证了酯-酰胺替换和α-甲基化对热稳定性的增强作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习方法将聚合物表示为单个重复单元的分子图，忽略了聚合物链的周期性和超越成对键的多体相互作用，导致无法全面捕捉聚合物的化学空间特性，阻碍了系统性的聚合物发现。

Method: 构建了Periodic-TDL框架：1）基于周期性Vietoris-Rips复形捕获多空间尺度的多体相互作用；2）使用分层单纯形消息传递(HSMP)编码器将长程相互作用信息传播到共价键；3）生成包含48,208个结构的计算合成数据集进行训练。

Result: 在涵盖电子、光学、物理和热学目标的聚合物性能预测任务中全面超越所有最先进模型；定量验证了酯-酰胺替换使$T_g$平均提高约55°C，主链α-甲基化提高约14°C；通过6组新型聚合物的独立实验测量成功验证了模型预测。

Conclusion: Periodic-TDL成功捕获了特定官能团修饰的潜在物理效应，而非仅在基准数据集上优化预测性能，为聚合物设计和发现提供了有效的理论指导和预测工具。

Abstract: Polymers underpin applications across energy, healthcare, and materials science, yet their vast chemical space makes systematic discovery challenging. Most machine learning approaches represent polymers as molecular graphs of a single repeating unit, thereby missing both the periodicity of polymer chains and many-body interactions beyond pairwise bonds. We introduce Periodic-TDL, a deep learning framework built on periodic Vietoris-Rips complexes that capture many-body interactions across multiple spatial scales, followed by a hierarchical simplicial message-passing (HSMP) encoder that propagates information from long-range interactions to covalent bonds, yielding representations enriched by higher-order topological features. Periodic-TDL outperforms all state-of-the-art models across polymer property prediction tasks spanning electronic, optical, physical, and thermal targets. Furthermore, we quantitatively validate how ester-to-amide substitution and $α$-methylation enhance thermal stability. Using a computationally synthesized dataset of 48,208 structures-generated via systematic substitution of acrylate and acrylamide polymers-we observed a mean $T_g$ increase of $\sim 55^\circ$C for ester-to-amide substitutions and $\sim 14^\circ$C for backbone $α$-methylation across matched polymer pairs. To verify these predicted trends, we use our Periodic-TDL model to analyze six novel polymer pairs from independent experimental measurements, including three newly synthesized polymers previously unreported in the literature. The experimental data successfully confirmed the model's predictions. Ultimately, these findings demonstrate that Periodic-TDL captures the underlying physical effects of specific functional group modifications, rather than merely optimizing predictive performance on benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [94] [MONA: Muon Optimizer with Nesterov Acceleration for Scalable Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26842)
*Jiacheng Li,Jianchao Tan,Hongtao Xu,Jiaqi Zhang,Yifan Lu,Yerui Sun,Yuchen Xie,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MONA是一种结合Muon正交化框架与曲率感知加速的新型优化器，通过在Muon梯度处理流程中加入加速项（基于梯度差的指数移动平均），实现了从尖锐局部最小值的逃逸，在混合专家模型预训练和下游任务中均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器虽然通过矩阵正交化为大语言模型训练提供了几何感知更新，但作为一阶方法仍会被困在尖锐局部最小值中。作者旨在解决这一局限性，提升优化器的泛化能力和训练稳定性。

Method: MONA在Muon的正交化框架基础上，直接在梯度处理流程中引入加速项。该加速项由梯度差的指数移动平均计算得到，使优化器具备曲率感知能力，能够在保持Muon谱范数正则化的同时逃离尖锐最小值。

Result: 在参数规模从1B到68B的三种规模混合专家模型预训练（最大模型在1万亿token上训练）中，MONA在收敛性和下游任务性能上均优于Muon和AdamW。在MOE-68B-A3B模型的监督微调评估中，MONA在通用能力、数学推理和代码生成基准上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: MONA成功将Muon的正交化优势与曲率感知加速相结合，不仅提供了理论收敛性保证，还在大规模实验中验证了其优越性，为大语言模型训练提供了一种更优的优化器选择。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has recently offered a promising alternative to AdamW for large language model training, leveraging matrix orthogonalization to produce geometry-aware updates. However, like all first-order methods, Muon can become trapped in sharp local minima. In this work, we present MONA, an optimizer that bridges Muon's orthogonalization framework with curvature-aware acceleration. MONA adds an acceleration term directly into Muon's gradient processing pipeline. This term is calculated from the exponential moving average of gradient differences. We provide a detailed convergence analysis for MONA, showing that the acceleration term enables escape from sharp minima while preserving Muon's spectral-norm regularization. Empirically, MONA achieves better convergence and downstream task performance compared to both Muon and AdamW across three scales of Mixture-of-Experts pretraining, spanning from 1B to 68B parameters, with the largest model trained on 1 trillion tokens. Furthermore, we conduct supervised fine-tuning on the MOE-68B-A3B model and evaluate it on general capability, mathematical reasoning, and code generation benchmarks, where MONA achieves SOTA performance.

</details>


### [95] [Not All Disagreement Is Learnable: Token Teachability in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26844)
*Yuanyi Wang,Su Lu,Yanggan Gu,Pengkai Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhaoyi Yan,Congkai Xie,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TA-OPD方法，通过识别可学习的token位置进行选择性蒸馏，仅保留5%的token即可超越全token蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性在线策略蒸馏(OPD)方法基于高熵或高分歧token选择，但作者质疑：哪些token级别的教师信号实际上是可学习的？研究发现原始KL分歧是学习价值的粗糙代理，混淆了可学习分歧与不兼容分歧。

Method: 提出token可教性(teachability)概念，衡量教师是否将修正质量分配给学生top-K候选；开发TA-OPD方法，选择高可教性位置应用OPD损失，无需奖励模型或验证器。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Qwen3师生设置中，TA-OPD仅保留5%token即可超越全token OPD；优于基于熵和分歧的基线方法；可教性比原始KL更能预测固定上下文改进。

Conclusion: 选择性OPD应重新定义为选择可学习的教师信号而非仅仅显著的token，为蒸馏过程提供了新的理论视角和实践方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts with token-level teacher supervision. Recent selective OPD methods exploit the non-uniformity of OPD signals by prioritizing high-entropy or high-disagreement tokens. We revisit this principle and ask: which token-level teacher signals are actually learnable? Using a fixed-context diagnostic that measures same-context teacher-student KL reduction, we show that raw KL disagreement is a coarse proxy for learning value. It conflates learnable disagreement, where the teacher assigns corrective mass to the student's top-K candidates, with incompatible disagreement, where the teacher places mass mostly off the student's current support. We formalize this local compatibility as token teachability and show that it better predicts fixed-context improvement than raw KL alone. Motivated by this finding, we propose Teachability-Aware OPD (TA-OPD), a lightweight token-position selection method that applies OPD loss to high-teachability positions without reward models or verifiers. Across Qwen2.5 and Qwen 3 teacher-student settings, TA-OPD often surpasses full-token OPD with only 5% retained tokens and improves over entropy- and divergence-based baselines. Our results reframe selective OPD as selecting learnable teacher signals rather than merely salient tokens.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Energy-Based Models from Stochastic Interpolants using Spatiotemporal Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26850)
*Hanlin Yu,RuiKang OuYang,Partha Kaushik,Arto Klami,Michael U. Gutmann,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了stNCE框架，通过联合时空差分学习能量函数，解决了现有空间或时间差分方法的失效模式，在图像和分子实验中达到了与最先进密度估计方法竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于随机插值的能量模型学习方法（如去噪分数匹配）通常通过空间或时间差分来学习联合密度能量，但这些方法存在不同的失效模式，需要一种统一的框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出时空噪声对比估计框架，通过联合时空差分学习能量函数，该方法统一了多种现有方法并导出了新的训练目标。

Result: 在图像和分子数据上的实验表明，stNCE方法与当前最先进的密度估计方法相比具有竞争性的性能表现。

Conclusion: stNCE框架成功解决了现有方法的失效模式，为能量模型学习提供了统一的理论框架，并在实际应用中取得了良好效果。

Abstract: Learning an energy-based model from data samples is a central problem in machine learning. Many recent and popular methods, such as denoising score matching for training energy-based diffusion models, use stochastic interpolants to corrupt data samples at different noise levels indexed by a time variable. This defines a joint density over both the data space and time, and most methods learn its energy through either spatial or temporal differences. We identify distinct failure modes for both of these approaches. To solve them, we propose Spatiotemporal Noise-Contrastive Estimation (stNCE), a framework for learning the energy through joint spatiotemporal differences. stNCE unifies many existing methods and leads to new training objectives. Experiments on images and molecules demonstrate performance competitive with state-of-the-art density estimation methods.

</details>


### [97] [RAPNet: Accelerating Algebraic Multigrid with Learned Sparse Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26854)
*Yali Fink,Ido Ben-Yair,Lars Ruthotto,Eran Treister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了RAPNet，一种图神经网络框架，通过学习生成稀疏且鲁棒的粗网格算子，解决了代数多重网格(AMG)中稀疏性与收敛质量之间的权衡问题，在多种PDE离散化和图拉普拉斯算子上优于经典非伽辽金基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模稀疏线性系统的可扩展求解是科学计算和图分析的瓶颈。代数多重网格(AMG)虽提供最优线性扩展，但其性能严重受限于粗网格算子的稀疏性与收敛质量之间的权衡。经典AMG启发式方法难以平衡这些目标，常为稀疏性牺牲稳定性或性能。

Method: 提出RAPNet图神经网络框架，直接从稀疏代数系统学习生成稀疏、鲁棒的粗算子。采用层次级训练策略，能够从小规模子图学习并泛化到百万节点域，避免了先前神经AMG方法的瓶颈。该方法仅在求解器设置阶段执行，确保求解阶段保持良好的计算特性。

Result: 在多种PDE离散化和图拉普拉斯算子上表现优于经典非伽辽金基线方法。该方法对多查询任务特别有效，如特征值问题、时间相关模拟以及逆问题或设计问题。

Conclusion: RAPNet成功解决了AMG中稀疏性与收敛质量的权衡难题，通过图神经网络学习的粗算子同时具备稀疏性和鲁棒性，为多查询科学计算任务提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The scalable solution of large sparse linear systems is a bottleneck in scientific computing and graph analysis. While algebraic multigrid (AMG) offers optimal linear scaling, its performance is severely constrained by the trade-off between the sparsity and convergence quality of coarse-grid operators. Classical AMG heuristics struggle to balance these objectives, often sacrificing stability or performance for sparsity. We propose RAPNet, a graph neural network (GNN) framework that resolves this trade-off by learning to generate sparse, robust coarse operators directly from the sparse algebraic system. Key to our approach is a level-wise training strategy that enables learning from small subgraphs and generalization to million-node domains, bypassing the bottlenecks of prior neural AMG attempts. RAPNet executes exclusively during the solver setup phase, ensuring that the solve phase retains its favorable computational properties. We show that our method outperforms classical non-Galerkin baselines on diverse PDE discretizations and graph Laplacians, making it particularly effective for multi-query tasks such as eigenproblems, time-dependent simulations, and inverse or design problems.

</details>


### [98] [Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection via Prototype-Based Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26857)
*Yiming Xu,Zihan Chen,Zhen Peng,Song Wang,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProMoS，首个无监督通用图异常检测框架，通过知识蒸馏从未标注数据中学习正常模式，实现了零样本跨图异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用图异常检测方法依赖稀缺且昂贵的标注数据进行训练，部分方法甚至在推理阶段需要少样本支持，这限制了模型对多样化和未见异常模式的鲁棒性。因此需要一种能够利用未标注数据中丰富正常性信息的无监督方法。

Method: 提出ProMoS框架，采用知识蒸馏范式，从冻结的自监督图神经网络教师模型中提取正常性先验知识，迁移至混合学生模型；该模型包含共享全局分支和轻量级个性化分支，无需从头学习即可高效建模正常性；进一步提出原型引导的软标签蒸馏方法，在共享原型空间中对齐教师与学生模型，提升跨图泛化能力。

Result: 实验证明ProMoS在无需标签数据的情况下，能够通过蒸馏偏差和原型几何偏差在未见过的图上进行零样本异常检测，展现了良好的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: ProMoS成功实现了首个无监督通用图异常检测框架，为无标签、零样本的通用图异常检测开辟了一条实用路径，突破了现有方法对标注数据的依赖限制。

Abstract: Driven by the pressing demand for graph anomaly detection (GAD) in high-stakes domains, the generalist GAD paradigm, which trains a single detector transferable across new graphs, has recently gained growing attention. However, existing methods often rely on scarce and costly annotations for training and sometimes even require few-shot support at inference, which limits their robustness to diverse and unseen anomaly patterns. To address this limitation, we introduce ProMoS, the first unsupervised generalist GAD framework, which detects anomalies by modeling the abundant normality in unlabeled data. ProMoS adopts a knowledge-distillation paradigm to distill normality priors from a frozen self-supervised graph neural network (GNN) teacher to a mixture-of-students model with shared global and lightweight personalized branches, enabling efficient and expressive normality modeling without learning from scratch. We further propose prototype-guided soft-label distillation to align teacher and student in a shared prototype space, enhancing cross-graph generalizability. During inference, ProMoS performs zero-shot anomaly detection on unseen graphs via distillation bias and prototype geometric deviation. Extensive experiments show the effectiveness and efficiency of ProMoS, charting a practical path toward label-free, zero-shot generalist GAD.

</details>


### [99] [The Strongest Teacher Is Not Always the Best Teacher: Student-Centric Answer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26872)
*Zhengyu Hu,Zheyuan Xiao,Linxin Song,Fengqing Jiang,Yutai Li,Zhengyu Chen,Zhihan Xiong,Yue Liu,Junhao Lin,Yao Su,Lijie Hu,Kaize Ding,Xiao Teng,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出学生中心答案采样(SCAS)框架，根据学习成本而非教师强度选择训练数据，在30个教师模型、6个学生模型和8个任务上持续提升学生性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练中普遍假设性能最强的教师产生的监督数据质量最高，但本文发现该假设可能失效：即使多个教师对同一问题给出正确答案，最强教师的答案未必最适合特定学生学习。

Method: 提出学生中心答案采样(SCAS)框架：基于token级梯度分解推导出高效的前向传播学习成本代理指标，根据估计的学生中心学习成本从已验证的教师生成答案中选择训练数据，而非简单选择最强教师的答案。

Result: 在30个教师模型、6个学生基础模型和8个任务上的实验表明，SCAS方法持续改善学生模型性能，验证了有效蒸馏应优先选择匹配当前学生能力的监督数据。

Conclusion: 有效的知识蒸馏不应仅依据教师模型强度选择监督数据，而应优先考虑与学生当前能力相匹配的监督信号，这为LLM训练数据选择提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: LLM training increasingly relies on teacher-generated supervision, from synthetic responses to reasoning traces and tool-use demonstrations. Current practice often chooses the highest-performing teacher to generate student training data, implicitly treating teacher test performance as a proxy for teaching quality. We show that this assumption can fail: even when multiple teachers provide correct answers to the same question, the answer from the strongest teacher is not necessarily the best supervision for a given student. To address this gap, we propose Student-Centric Answer Sampling (SCAS), a framework that selects from verified teacher-generated answers according to their estimated student-centric learning cost. Motivated by a token-wise gradient decomposition, we derive an efficient forward-only proxy for this cost and use it to guide answer selection during training. Experiments across 30 teacher models, 6 student base models, and 8 tasks show that SCAS consistently improves student performance, suggesting that effective distillation should prioritize supervision matched to the current student rather than teacher strength alone.

</details>


### [100] [Negligible in Size, Significant in Effect: On Scale Vectors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26895)
*Mingze Wang,Shuchen Zhu,Yuxin Fang,Binghui Li,Kai Shen,Shu Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM中归一化层的缩放向量，发现其虽参数量极小但对预训练至关重要；提出了三种改进策略并整合为统一方案，在多种模型规模上实现了更低的终端损失和更好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM归一化层中的缩放向量虽然被广泛使用，但缺乏深入理解。研究者希望从表达能力、优化和架构结构三个角度系统探究缩放向量的作用机制，以填补这一认知空白。

Method: 1) 理论分析：研究Pre-Norm架构中缩放向量对表达能力和优化的影响，揭示其自放大预条件效应；2) 区分Input-Norm和Output-Norm层，分析权重衰减的不同作用；3) 提出三种改进：分支特异性异质性、线性映射周围的改进位置配置、幅值-方向重参数化；4) 整合为统一策略并在0.12B-2B参数规模的密集和MoE模型上验证。

Result: 理论分析表明缩放向量通过预条件效应改善优化而非提升表达能力；权重衰减对Input-Norm层有益但对Output-Norm层有害；三种改进策略各自均带来一致增益；统一策略在多种优化器、学习率调度和工业规模token预算下均实现了比精心调优的基线更低的终端损失，并展现出更优的扩展行为。

Conclusion: 缩放向量是LLM预训练中的关键组件，其作用机制已被系统阐明；基于理论洞察提出的统一缩放向量策略以可忽略的参数和计算开销，在各种设置下均提升了模型性能，为LLM架构设计提供了新的理论指导和实践方案。

Abstract: Normalization layers in modern large language models (LLMs) consist of a deterministic normalization operation and a learnable scale vector. While the normalization operation has been extensively studied, the scale vector remains poorly understood despite its ubiquitous use. In this work, we present a systematic study of scale vectors in LLMs from the perspectives of expressivity, optimization, and architectural structure. First, we show empirically that although scale vectors constitute only a negligible fraction of model parameters, removing them substantially degrades LLM pre-training. Our theory further shows that, in Pre-Norm architectures, scale vectors do not increase expressivity; instead, they improve optimization through a self-amplifying preconditioning effect on subsequent linear mappings. Second, we investigate the role of weight decay for scale vectors. By distinguishing Input-Norm and Output-Norm layers, we theoretically show that weight decay is beneficial for the former but harmful for the latter, due to their distinct roles in optimization and expressivity. Third, motivated by this understanding, we propose three lightweight and complementary improvements to scale vectors: branch-specific heterogeneity, improved placement around linear mappings, and magnitude-direction reparameterization. Both theory and experiments show that each improvement yields consistent gains. Finally, we combine these improvements into a unified scale-vector strategy and evaluate it through extensive LLM pre-training experiments on dense and mixture-of-experts models ranging from 0.12B to 2B parameters, across multiple optimizers and learning rate schedules, under industrial-scale token budgets. The unified strategy consistently achieves lower terminal loss than well-tuned baselines and exhibits more favorable scaling behavior, while adding negligible parameter and computational overhead.

</details>


### [101] [SPHERE-JEPA: Spherical Prediction with Homogeneous Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26900)
*Léo Nicollier,Max Dunitz,Marc Pic,Pablo Musé,Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SPHERE-JEPA自监督学习框架，证明了在黎曼流形上均匀分布优于高斯分布，在ImageNet-1K上取得+1.8%线性探测提升，纹理检索mAP提升超6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL方法（如LeJEPA）将高斯分布视为最优几何结构，但该结论仅在欧氏空间成立。对于支撑在低维流形（如超球面）上的分布，最优表征几何尚不明确，这是SSL领域的根本性开放问题。

Method: 将极小极大分析从欧氏空间扩展到黎曼流形上的光滑分布，证明均匀分布在流形上是k近邻的最优解，超球面上的均匀分布是指数点积核和线性核核岭回归的最优解。基于此理论发现，提出SPHERE-JEPA框架，将LeJEPA的Cramér-Wold投影机制从高斯先验改为超球面均匀性约束。

Result: 理论结果表明高斯嵌入的非均匀密度会导致各向异性的k近邻邻域，严重偏置估计器。实验结果表明SPHERE-JEPA在纹理检索mAP上提升超过6%，在ImageNet-1K (ViT-B/14)上线性探测准确率提升1.8%，在标准基准测试上持续匹配或超越LeJEPA。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了SSL中表征几何的关键洞察：在流形设置下均匀分布优于高斯分布，为SSL的理论基础提供了新的认识，并提出了实用的SPHERE-JEPA框架验证了理论发现的有效性。

Abstract: A fundamental open question in self-supervised learning (SSL) is the explicit characterization of the optimal geometry of the learned representations. Recently, LeJEPA identified isotropic Gaussian embeddings as optimal for minimizing downstream prediction risk in Euclidean spaces. However, the corresponding problem for distributions supported on lower-dimensional manifolds, such as the hypersphere, remains unexplored. In this work, we demonstrate that extending this minimax analysis to smooth distributions on Riemannian manifolds fundamentally changes the optimal solution. We show that, under a worst-case formulation, both k-nearest neighbors and kernel ridge regression induce hyperspherical uniformity. More precisely, we show that uniform distributions on manifolds are optimal for k-nearest neighbors, and that the uniform distribution on the sphere is optimal for kernel ridge regression with both the exponential dot-product kernel and the linear kernel. This theoretical insight reveals a fundamental limitation of Gaussian embeddings: their non-uniform density induces anisotropic k-NN neighborhoods, severely biasing the estimator. To correct this, we introduce SPHERE-JEPA, a theoretically grounded SSL framework. We adapt LeJEPA's Cram{é}r-Wold projection mechanism to enforce hyperspherical uniformity rather than a Gaussian prior. Empirically, SPHERE-JEPA yields significant improvements, boosting texture retrieval mAP by over 6%, while consistently matching or outperforming LeJEPA on standard benchmarks-including a +1.8% linear probing gain on ImageNet-1K (ViT-B/14).

</details>


### [102] [EEG-FM-Audit: A Systematic Evaluation and Analysis Pipeline for EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26910)
*Xianheng Wang,Yige Yang,Damien Coyle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了EEG-FM-Audit评估分析管道，系统性地评估EEG基础模型，发现经过良好调优的监督学习基线模型可以匹配甚至超越先进的基础模型，且参数量显著更少；学习范式的有效性高度依赖数据集规模和架构；通过神经生理学探测揭示了模型如何依赖特定生理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG基础模型研究存在三个关键局限性：监督学习基线调优过程不透明、复杂学习范式的贡献未经验证、模型决策过程缺乏可解释性。这些限制阻碍了对EEG基础模型真实性能和机制的理解。

Method: 提出EEG-FM-Audit综合评估分析管道，包含三个主要组件：（1）基于ASHA的基准测试协议，通过透明优化监督学习基线确保公平比较；（2）范式层面的消融研究，评估基础模型中学习范式的有效性；（3）神经生理学探测（NPP）框架，探索模型是否利用有效的EEG时域、频域和空域特性。在三个公共数据集上对四个先进的EEG基础模型和五个代表性监督模型进行应用评估。

Result: 主要发现包括：（1）经过适当调优的监督学习基线模型能够匹配甚至超越先进的基础模型，尽管参数量显著更少；（2）基础模型学习范式的有效性高度依赖于数据集规模和模型架构；（3）神经生理学探测分析揭示了基础模型如何依赖特定的生理特征，为更可解释的神经解码建立了框架。

Conclusion: EEG-FM-Audit为EEG基础模型的系统化评估提供了标准化框架，揭示了当前EEG基础模型的实际性能边界和学习机制，强调了监督学习基线的重要性，为构建更透明、可解释的神经解码模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large EEG Foundation Models (FMs) have shown great potential for decoding EEG signals across diverse cognitive tasks. However, existing EEG-FM studies exhibit three critical limitations: opaque supervised baseline tuning, unverified contributions of complex learning paradigms, and a lack of transparency in model decision-making. To address these, we propose EEG-FM-Audit, a comprehensive evaluation and analysis pipeline designed to systematize the assessment of EEG-FMs. EEG-FM-Audit consists of three primary components: (1) an ASHA-driven benchmarking protocol that ensures fair comparisons by transparently optimizing supervised baselines; (2) paradigm-level ablation studies to evaluate the effectiveness of learning paradigms in FMs; and (3) a neurophysiological probing (NPP) framework, which explores whether FMs leverage valid temporal, spatial, and spectral EEG properties. We apply EEG-FM-Audit to four state-of-the-art EEG-FMs and five representative supervised models across three public datasets. Our results reveal that properly tuned supervised baselines can match or outperform advanced FMs, despite requiring significantly fewer parameters. Furthermore, we find that the effectiveness of learning paradigms of FMs is highly dependent on dataset scale and architecture. Finally, NPP analysis demonstrates how FMs rely on specific physiological features, establishing a framework for more interpretable neural decoding.

</details>


### [103] [Agile Online Model Selection: Resolving Adaptation Lag via Safeguarded Large Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26919)
*Kei Takemura,Ryuta Matsuno,Keita Sakuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有后验惩罚机制的乐观在线镜像下降算法，通过使用高达Θ(T)的大学习率并动态排除导致过高遗憾的学习率，解决了现有无调参算法在鲁棒性和敏捷性之间的权衡问题，显著减少了突变环境下的适应延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳环境中，现有的无调参在线模型选择算法面临鲁棒性与敏捷性之间的根本性权衡：为保证动态遗憾边界，必须将学习率限制为小常数（如O(1)），这导致在分布突变时产生显著的适应延迟。

Method: 提出一种新颖的乐观在线镜像下降算法，使用高达Θ(T)的大学习率，并引入后验惩罚机制：动态监控不稳定更新，排除导致过高遗憾的学习率，从而无需先验约束。

Result: 理论分析表明累积惩罚为O(log T)，算法在保持近似最优最坏情况保证的同时，在良性情况下获得更优的收敛率。在合成数据和11个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，该方法将适应延迟从数百轮减少到几轮，持续优于无调参基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的后验惩罚机制成功解决了在线学习中的鲁棒性-敏捷性权衡问题，使得算法能够自主适应未知分布变化，在理论保证和实际性能方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Maintaining predictive accuracy in non-stationary environments requires online model selection to adapt autonomously to unknown distribution shifts. However, existing tuning-free algorithms face a fundamental trade-off between robustness and agility. Specifically, to ensure dynamic regret bounds, they must restrict learning rates to small constants (e.g., $O(1)$). This restriction inevitably causes significant adaptation lag during abrupt changes. To resolve this, we propose a novel optimistic online mirror descent that utilizes safeguarded large learning rates up to $Θ(T)$, where $T$ is the number of rounds. Our key technical contribution is a post-hoc penalty mechanism that dynamically monitors unstable updates and excludes learning rates incurring excessive regret, eliminating the need for restrictive a priori constraints. We show that the cumulative penalty remains $O(\log T)$, allowing our algorithm to match near-optimal worst-case guarantees while achieving superior rates in benign cases. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and eleven diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that our approach reduces the adaptation lag from hundreds of rounds to a few rounds, consistently outperforming tuning-free baselines.

</details>


### [104] [When Muon Optimizer Meets Adversarial Training: A Theoretical and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26929)
*Jun Yan,Weiquan Huang,Jiankai Zuo,Yujian Mo,Xi Fang,Chengliang Wu,Zeming Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器（通过极分解正交化矩阵更新）在对抗训练中的应用，发现其在多种架构和威胁模型下优于AdamW且媲美SGD，揭示了优化器几何特性是影响对抗鲁棒性的安全关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练(AT)是对抗攻击最可靠的防御方法之一，其鲁棒性严重依赖于底层min-max目标的优化方式。虽然SGD是AT的默认优化器，但自适应优化器虽能提升标准训练却可能导致鲁棒性下降。Muon优化器在大规模训练中表现优异，这引发了关键问题：正交化优化能否在强且异构的威胁模型下改善AT？

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明Muon通过近似极分解对矩阵更新施加谱范数稳定性上限，限制训练动态中的不受控谱增长，且不显式收缩学习权重；2. 实验验证：在5种架构和3种ℓ_p威胁模型（ℓ_∞、ℓ_1、ℓ_2）及其组合下进行全面评估，对比Muon、SGD和AdamW的性能表现。

Result: 在CNN上，Muon与SGD性能相当且显著优于AdamW；在ViT上，Muon显著优于AdamW。理论结果表明Muon施加谱范数稳定性约束，限制无控谱增长。实验证实优化器几何特性是对抗训练中的安全相关因素，明确了正交化更新有效的实证条件。

Conclusion: 优化器设计是对抗训练的安全关键组件。Muon通过正交化矩阵更新实现谱范数稳定性，在多种架构和威胁模型下表现出色，为AT提供了新的优化器选择视角。研究识别出优化器几何特性作为安全相关因素，并明确了正交化更新有益的经验制度。

Abstract: Adversarial training (AT) remains one of the most reliable empirical defenses against adversarial attacks. Its robustness critically depends on how the underlying min-max objective is optimized. In practice, Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) optimizer remains the default optimization choice for AT, whereas adaptive optimizers often improve standard training but may yield inferior robustness. Recently, the Muon optimizer, which orthogonalizes matrix-valued updates via an approximate polar decomposition, has achieved notable success in large-scale training at a memory cost comparable to SGD. This raises a security-relevant question: \textit{can orthogonalized optimization improve AT under strong and heterogeneous threat models?} Focusing on this problem, we conduct a comprehensive theoretical and empirical study. Theoretically, we show that Muon imposes a spectral-norm stability ceiling on matrix updates, limiting uncontrolled spectral growth in the training dynamics without explicitly shrinking the learned weights. Empirically, across five architectures and three $\ell_p$ threat models ($\ell_\infty$, $\ell_1$, $\ell_2$) and their union, Muon is competitive with SGD on CNNs and substantially outperforms AdamW on both CNNs and ViTs. These results identify optimizer geometry as a security-relevant factor in adversarial training, while clarifying the empirical regimes in which orthogonalized updates are beneficial. Overall, our findings highlight optimizer design as a security-critical component of AT.

</details>


### [105] [RLVR Datasets and Where to Find Them: Tracing Data Lineage for Better Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26971)
*Hsiu-Yuan Huang,Weijie Liu,Chenming Tang,Sanwoo Lee,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS框架用于追溯RLVR数据集的原子来源，发现99.7%的数据仅源自20个原子源，存在严重的数据同质化和污染风险；基于此构建了新数据集DAPO++并设计了质量评估指标Q。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR数据集的快速增殖导致了谱系崩溃（provenance collapse）问题，数据集之间的血缘关系不清晰，使得研究者难以了解数据的真实来源和潜在的污染风险，阻碍了该领域的健康发展。

Method: 提出ATLAS（Atomic-source Tracing via Lineage-Aware Search）框架追溯数据集到原子源；引入Source-level Counterfactual Attribution（SCA）原则，通过对比每个原子源的RL检查点与共享基础模型来衡量样本的边际效用；基于SCA信号设计复合数据集质量评分Q。

Result: 成功将1.45M实例中的99.7%追溯到20个原子源；发现大多数RLVR数据集是少数共享上游源的变体，很少有真正的新数据；DAPO++在Qwen系列模型上持续提升性能；质量评分Q与下游RLVR性能强相关。

Conclusion: RLVR数据集领域存在严重的同质化问题，数据谱系透明度不足；通过谱系感知的视角可以更好地评估和构建数据集；SCA原则和Q评分为数据集质量评估和去污染提供了有效工具。

Abstract: The proliferation of Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) datasets has exacerbated provenance collapse due to unclear lineage among existing datasets. To bridge this fragmented RLVR data landscape, we propose Atomic-source Tracing via Lineage-Aware Search (ATLAS), a systematic framework for tracing RLVR datasets back to their atomic sources, attributing over 99.7% of 1.45M instances to 20 atomic sources. Our analysis reveals that most RLVR datasets are variants of a small set of shared upstream sources, with few introducing genuinely new data, and many facing data contamination risks. These findings naturally motivate us to curate a new RLVR dataset, DAPO++, and to benchmark existing datasets from a lineage-aware perspective. To this end, we propose Source-level Counterfactual Attribution (SCA) as a guiding principle to curate a decontaminated training dataset with concentrated learning signals. Essentially, SCA measures a sample's marginal utility by comparing per-atomic-source RL checkpoints against a shared base model. Building upon these attribution signals, we further design a composite dataset quality score Q that strongly correlates with downstream RLVR performance. Experiments on Qwen3 series models verify that DAPO++ consistently improves performance on held-out benchmarks, while Q reliably predicts downstream RLVR training effectiveness. Our code and data is available at https://github.com/Celine-hxy/ATLAS.

</details>


### [106] [Convergence of Spectral Descent for Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26977)
*Yixuan Yang,Yuqing He,Song Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器的简化变体——谱下降(SD)及其截断版本(TSD)，在非光滑凸优化设置下建立了全局线性收敛保证，并分析了带解耦权重衰减的正则化变体的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在训练大语言模型方面展现出卓越的实证成功，但其理论理解仍然有限。现有收敛保证严重依赖光滑性假设，而非光滑收敛行为很大程度上未被探索，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 研究谱下降(SD)和截断谱下降(TSD)两种简化变体；在凸性、Lipschitz连续性和尖锐性条件下分析收敛性；通过Frank-Wolfe方法的联系研究带解耦权重衰减的正则化变体；应用于混合稀疏和稠密噪声下的鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复问题。

Result: 在非光滑凸优化设置下为SD和TSD建立了全局线性收敛保证；推导出正则化变体的次线性收敛保证；为混合噪声下的鲁棒低秩矩阵恢复提供了严格的恢复保证；数值实验验证了理论发现并展示了Muon类型方法在非光滑优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为Muon类型优化器在非光滑优化设置下的收敛性提供了理论保证，拓展了其在凸性、Lipschitz连续性和尖锐性条件下的理论理解，并通过低秩矩阵恢复应用验证了框架的实用价值。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer has recently demonstrated remarkable empirical success in training large language models. However, the theoretical understanding of its mechanisms remains limited. Current convergence guarantees for Muon rely heavily on smoothness assumptions, leaving its non-smooth convergence behavior largely unexplored. In this work, we take a step toward bridging this gap by investigating Spectral Descent (SD), a simplified variant of Muon, together with its truncated counterpart, Truncated Spectral Descent (TSD). Under convexity, Lipschitz continuity, and sharpness conditions, we establish global linear convergence for both SD and TSD in non-smooth convex formulations. We also study regularized variants equipped with decoupled weight decay and derive sublinear convergence guarantees through their connection with Frank-Wolfe methods. Finally, we apply our theoretical framework to robust low-rank matrix recovery under mixed sparse and dense noise regimes and provide rigorous recovery guarantees. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings and demonstrate the effectiveness of Muon-type methods for non-smooth optimization.

</details>


### [107] [TED: Related Party Transaction guided Tax Evasion Detection on Heterogeneous Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26984)
*Yiming Xu,Bin Shi,Bo Dong,Jiaxiang Wang,Hua Wei,Qinghua Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于异构图神经网络的偷税检测方法，通过建模税务场景为异构图并利用层次注意力机制，在真实数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偷税检测方案主要依赖公司的统计特征，未能充分利用税务场景中丰富的交互信息，影响了检测性能。偷税行为导致政府税收严重损失并扰乱公平竞争的经济秩序，亟需更有效的检测方法。

Method: 将税务场景建模为异构图，提出新的图神经网络模型。具体包括：使用异构复杂关联方交易群过滤低层噪声信息；设计层次注意力机制捕获关联方交易群中隐藏的深层结构和语义信息。

Result: 方法应用于税务局真实风险管理系统，在两个人工标注的真实税务数据集上评估，结果表明该方法在偷税检测任务中显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于异构图神经网络的偷税检测方法能够有效提取异构图的综合信息，充分利用税务场景的交互信息，大幅提升了偷税检测性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Tax evasion causes severe losses of government revenues and disturbs the economic order of fair competition. To help alleviate this problem, the latest tax evasion detection solutions utilize expert knowledge to extract features and then train classifiers to determine whether a company is suspected of tax evasion. However, existing solutions mainly focus on the statistical features of the company, but fail to exploit the rich interactive information in tax scenarios, which affect the detection performance. In this paper, we first model the tax scenario as a heterogeneous graph and study the tax evasion detection problem under the heterogeneous graph model. To improve the performance of tax evasion detection, a novel graph neural network model is proposed to extract the comprehensive information of heterogeneous graphs. Specifically, we use heterogeneous and complex related party transaction groups to filter low-level noise information. Moreover, a hierarchical attention mechanism is designed to capture the deeper structure and semantic information hidden in the related party transaction group. We apply our method to the real risk management system of the tax bureau, and evaluate it on two human-labeled real-world tax datasets. The results demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art in the tax evasion detection task.

</details>


### [108] [Probabilistic Recurrent Intention Switching Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26998)
*Wenyuan Sheng,Hao Zhu,Joschka Boedecker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM模型，用轻量级循环网络替代传统方法中的马尔可夫链或固定历史窗口机制来处理意图切换，实现了O(nK)复杂度的精确E步，在多个任务中验证了其在多意图IRL中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的逆强化学习方法假设单一平稳奖励函数，无法捕捉一个回合内的目标切换；现有的多意图IRL方法通过分割轨迹解决此问题，但将意图转移建模为无记忆马尔可夫链或手动状态增强，表达能力有限。

Method: 提出概率循环意图切换模型（PRISM），使用轻量级循环网络将观测历史映射到每步意图分布，证明了EM目标函数可精确分解为独立的每意图奖励子问题，每个子问题有闭式解，无需变分近似。

Result: 在非马尔可夫网格世界、小鼠迷宫和BridgeData V2机器人操作三个任务上，PRISM均取得了最高的留出对数似然值，并成功从无标签演示中恢复了可命名、时间连贯的意图。

Conclusion: 离散目标切换在生物和人工智能体中都普遍存在，PRISM为多意图IRL提供了有效的解决方案，并首次在大规模机器人应用中验证了多意图IRL的可行性。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) recovers reward functions from observed behavior, yet traditional methods assume a single stationary reward that cannot capture goal switching within an episode. Recent multi-intention IRL methods address this by segmenting trajectories, but model intention transitions as either a memoryless Markov chain or via manual state augmentation with a fixed history window. We propose the Probabilistic Recurrent Intention Switching Model (PRISM), which replaces both mechanisms with a lightweight recurrent network that maps observation history to a per-step intention distribution. We prove that the resulting EM objective decomposes exactly into independent per-intention reward subproblems, each solvable in closed form, yielding an $\mathcal{O}(nK)$ E-step with no variational approximation. We evaluate PRISM on a non-Markovian gridworld, a mouse labyrinth, and BridgeData~V2 robotic manipulation, the first large-scale robotic application of multi-intention IRL. Across all settings PRISM achieves the highest held-out log-likelihood while recovering nameable, temporally coherent intentions from unlabeled demonstrations, suggesting that discrete goal switching is present in both biological and artificial agents.

</details>


### [109] [Sampling Data with Chains of Forward-Backward Diffusion Steps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27006)
*Hyunmo Kang,Noam Itzhak Levi,Corinna Elena Wegner,Daniel J. Korchinski,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了U-turn chains方法，通过扩散模型的前向-后向步骤采样高维分布。研究发现最小U-turn动态存在遍历性破坏相变，且高层特征松弛较慢，只有在大的U-turn幅度时才能恢复遍历性和高效的混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从学习的高维分布中采样是一个基础性的计算问题。扩散模型虽然强大，但其采样动态的性质尚不清楚。作者希望理解扩散模型在学习数据流形上的采样行为，特别是遍历性、特征松弛顺序等基本性质，以便更好地理解扩散模型的采样机制和局限性。

Method: 提出U-turn chains：通过迭代扩散模型的短前向-后向步骤构建马尔可夫链，每一步提出的移动保持在学习的流形上，并配以Metropolis-Hastings校正从能量修改的目标分布中采样。在合成语言、自然语言和自然图像上测试该方法，分析不同U-turn幅度下的遍历性、特征松弛速度和层序反转现象。

Result: 在合成语言中发现最小U-turn动态经历遍历性破坏相变，由数据流形碎片化驱动；遍历性在更大U-turn幅度时恢复。在非遍历区域，低层特征比高层特征松弛更快，这种顺序仅在足够大的U-turn幅度时反转。在自然语言和自然图像上验证：最小U-turn松弛缓慢（尤其对CNN或LLM中的深层表示）；层序反转仅在大噪声下出现，此时混合高效。这些特征与强约束、弱混合的局部动态一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明扩散模型的采样动态受到数据流形结构的强烈约束，存在遍历性破坏现象。最小U-turn幅度下，系统表现出弱混合行为，高层特征难以松弛；只有在大噪声水平下才能实现高效混合。这些发现对理解扩散模型的采样性质、设计更好的采样策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sampling from learned high-dimensional distributions is a foundational computational problem. We introduce U-turn chains: Markov chains obtained by iterating short forward-backward steps of a diffusion model, in which each step proposes a move that remains on the learned data manifold and, paired with a Metropolis-Hastings correction, samples from energy-modified targets. For synthetic languages, we show that minimal U-turn dynamics undergoes an ergodicity-breaking phase transition driven by fragmentation of the data manifold; ergodicity is restored at larger U-turn magnitude. In the non-ergodic regime, low-level features relax faster than high-level ones, an ordering that inverts only at sufficiently large U-turn magnitude. We test these predictions on natural language and natural images. In both modalities, minimal U-turns relax slowly, especially for high-level features approximated by deep representations in CNNs or LLMs. The layer-ordering inversion appears only at large noise when mixing is efficient -- signatures consistent with strongly constrained, weakly mixing local dynamics. We discuss the implications of these results for sampling with diffusion models.

</details>


### [110] [SCENT: Aligning Mass Spectra with Molecular Structure for Olfactory Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27009)
