*Ziqi Zhang,Eunyeong Jin,Miguel Vasco,Farzaneh Taleb,Nona Rajabi,Alexandra Gutmann,Jonathan Williams,Antônio H. Ribeiro,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCENT框架，通过多模态对比学习将EI-MS质谱表示与预训练的化学结构嵌入对齐，实现了仅依赖质谱数据即可达到与基于化学结构模型相当的嗅觉预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从分子结构预测人类嗅觉感知的方法在推理时需要明确的化学结构，但在实际传感场景中这些结构信息往往不可用。因此需要探索能够直接利用传感数据进行嗅觉预测的替代方案。

Method: 提出Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT (SCENT)框架，这是一个多模态对比学习框架，将电子轰击质谱(EI-MS)表示与预训练的化学结构嵌入进行对齐，在推理阶段仅需要质谱数据而无需明确的分子结构信息。

Result: 在多标签气味描述符预测任务中，SCENT显著优于仅使用质谱的基线方法，并且达到了与基于结构模型相当的性能。学习到的表示还能更好地近似连续的人类感知评分，并能泛化到真实实验室测量的质谱数据。

Conclusion: 跨模态对齐是将分析光谱与化学语义建立联系的有效策略，证明了质谱数据可以作为化学结构的替代输入模态用于嗅觉感知预测。

Abstract: Predicting human olfactory perception from molecular structure has seen remarkable progress, yet these approaches require explicit chemical structure at inference, which is not available in practical sensing settings. We address this gap by exploring direct electron ionization mass spectrometry (EI-MS), a sensing technique that acquires chemically informative fragmentation fingerprints in seconds, as an alternative input modality for olfactory prediction. We contribute Spectrum-to-Chemical Embedding alignmeNT (SCENT), a multi-modal contrastive learning framework that aligns EI-MS representations with pretrained chemical structure embeddings, while requiring only mass spectra at inference. On the multi-label odor descriptor prediction task, SCENT significantly outperforms MS-only baselines and achieves performance comparable to structure-based models, despite requiring no explicit molecular structure at test time. The learned representations also better approximate continuous human perceptual ratings and generalize to real-world lab-measured spectra, suggesting that cross-modal alignment is an effective strategy for grounding analytical spectra in chemical semantics.

</details>


### [111] [SQARL: A Size-Agnostic Reinforcement Learning approach for Circuit Allocation in Distributed Quantum Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27027)
*Víctor Carballo,Júlia López-Closa,Mario Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的深度学习架构解决分布式量子计算中的量子比特分配问题，无需重新训练即可适应任意硬件配置，相比启发式算法HQA在特定电路上降低33%分配成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子处理器扩展受限于退相干和串扰等技术挑战。分布式量子计算通过互连小型量子处理器解决此问题，但面临核间通信成本高的挑战。现有的强化学习方法缺乏硬件拓扑灵活性，且性能不如手工启发式算法HQA，亟需开发既灵活又高性能的学习方法。

Method: 提出基于Transformer的神经网络架构，能够处理任意数量的量子比特和核心配置而无需重新训练。该架构作为强化学习策略网络，学习量子电路分配策略以最小化核间通信成本。

Result: 训练策略在性能上持续超越先前RL方法，并缩小了与HQA的差距。在Cuccaro加法器上实现比HQA降低33%分配成本，随机电路上平均降低25%。学习方法的性能首次有效匹配手工启发式算法。

Conclusion: 基于学习的量子比特分配方法能够有效匹敌甚至超越手工启发式算法性能，证明了深度学习方法在分布式量子计算中的实用潜力，为实际应用场景奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: The scaling of quantum processors is currently limited by technical challenges such as decoherence and cross-talk. As the number of qubits grows, interference increases the computational noise. Distributed quantum computing addresses these limitations by interconnecting smaller, easier-to-handle quantum processors (cores), but it introduces the challenge of minimizing slow, error-prone inter-core communication. The task of distributing quantum circuits across cores while minimizing communication costs is known as the Qubit Allocation problem. This work focuses on developing a deep learning approach to this problem, emphasizing flexibility to quantum hardware topology and improving state-of-the-art performance.
  Heuristic and non-learning algorithms, such as the Hungarian Qubit Allocation (HQA), currently represent the state of the art. Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches leverage learned allocation policies but often lack flexibility, requiring retraining when hardware configurations change, and they fall short of the solution quality achieved by non-learning methods. However, learning mechanisms could outperform human-crafted heuristics.
  To overcome these limitations, this work proposes a flexible, transformer-based architecture that can handle arbitrary numbers of qubits and cores without retraining. Results show that the trained policy consistently outperforms the previous RL state of the art and narrows the gap between RL and HQA for the most common circuits. It achieves a 33% reduction in allocation cost relative to the HQA for the Cuccaro Adder and 25% on average for random circuits. These findings show that learning-based approaches can effectively match the performance of hand-crafted heuristics, a crucial step towards their application in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [112] [Less is More: Early Stopping Rollout for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27028)
*Zhou Ziheng,Jiaqi Li,Huacong Tang,Ying Nian Wu,Demetri Terzopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了同策略蒸馏（OPD）中存在“Off-policy Teacher Decay”问题，并提出了一种简单有效的解决方案——早停展开（ESR），通过限制学生模型仅生成前几个token来避免教师模型评分能力的下降，从而显著提升蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同策略蒸馏（OPD）中，学生模型使用自身的生成轨迹作为上下文，这对教师模型来说是离策略的。随着生成长度增加，教师模型产生纠正性评分的能力会衰减，甚至退回到预训练阶段的token补全行为，这被称为“Off-policy Teacher Decay”问题，严重影响了蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出早停展开（ESR）策略：仅限制学生模型生成响应的前几个token进行蒸馏训练，而不是生成完整的响应序列。这种简单的位置限制策略避免了教师模型在长序列上评分能力的衰减问题。

Result: ESR在跨模型规模、模型族、任务和训练机制下均超越了完整展开OPD的性能；在GPU效率和训练稳定性方面表现更优，特别是在跨模型族场景下；发现了“Cascading Alignment”和“Sub-mode Commitment”效应解释其有效性；有时甚至超越教师模型性能；该策略无法完全用KL散度和熵信号解释。

Conclusion: ESR是一种简单而有效的蒸馏策略，通过限制生成长度成功解决了Off-policy Teacher Decay问题，不仅提升了性能和效率，还展现出跨模型族的强大泛化能力，其机制涉及级联对齐和子模式承诺效应，为序列级蒸馏提供了新的视角。

Abstract: On-policy distillation has recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard sequence-level imitation, training a student by scoring its own rollouts with a teacher model. However, we observe ``Off-policy Teacher Decay'' problem in this paradigm: for the later tokens, with student's earlier trajectory as context that is off-policy to the teacher, the teacher's ability to produce a corrective score would decay, and may fall back to token-completion behavior learned in the pre-training stage. We empirically verify this problem, and we propose Early Stopping Rollout (ESR) to fix it: a simple yet effective distillation strategy that simply restricts the rollout generation to the first
  response tokens. We show that ESR both surpasses the full rollout OPD performance across model size, family, tasks and training regime, and exhibit much higher GPU efficiency and training stability, especially under cross model family scenarios. We further investigate the mechanism behind this surprising performance and discovered "Cascading Alignment" and "Sub-mode Commitment" effect of ESR that may explain why it works effectively and even sometimes exceeding the teacher model performance. Besides, we show that this position-based token selection strategy cannot be fully explainable by KL divergence and entropy signals.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Dynamic Graph Representations through Timespan View Contrasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27063)
*Yiming Xu,Zhen Peng,Bin Shi,Xu Hua,Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLDG和CLDG++动态图表示学习框架，通过时间平移不变性实现高效时空建模，在节点分类和异常检测任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督图表示学习方法主要依赖静态图的节点特征和拓扑属性，忽视了现实图数据中边的时间戳等时间成分。动态图数据包含丰富的时序信息，需要开发能够优雅建模时间演化的方法。

Method: 提出时间平移不变性这一新的归纳偏置，假设同一节点在不同时间跨度上保持相似标签；开发CLDG框架，通过不同时间跨度的对比学习鼓励节点保持局部一致的时间平移不变性；进一步提出CLDG++，利用图扩散揭示全局上下文关联，设计包含local-local、local-global和global-global的多尺度对比学习目标。

Result: 在节点分类和动态图异常检测等下游任务上均展现优异性能；相比复杂序列模型，CLDG通过隐式利用时间线索显著降低了时间和空间复杂度；通过测量不同时间跨度的一致性构建异常指标，实现了与异常检测任务的无缝集成。

Conclusion: CLDG和CLDG++成功地将时间平移不变性引入动态图表示学习，在保持性能的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，为金融、网络安全、医疗等高影响力领域的动态图异常检测应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The rich information underlying graphs has inspired further investigation of unsupervised graph representation. Existing studies mainly depend on node features and topological properties within static graphs to create self-supervised signals, neglecting the temporal components carried by real-world graph data, such as timestamps of edges. To overcome this limitation, this paper explores how to model temporal evolution on dynamic graphs elegantly. Specifically, we introduce a new inductive bias, namely temporal translation invariance, which illustrates the tendency of the identical node to keep similar labels across different timespans. Based on this assumption, we develop a dynamic graph representation framework CLDG that encourages the node to maintain locally consistent temporal translation invariance through contrastive learning on different timespans. Except for standard CLDG which only considers explicit topological links, our further proposed CLDG++ additionally employs graph diffusion to uncover global contextual correlations between nodes, and designs a multi-scale contrastive learning objective composed of local-local, local-global, and global-global contrasts to enhance representation capabilities. Interestingly, by measuring the consistency between different timespans to shape anomaly indicators, CLDG and CLDG++ are seamlessly integrated with the task of spotting anomalies on dynamic graphs, which has broad applications in many high-impact domains, such as finance, cybersecurity, and healthcare. Experiments demonstrate that CLDG and CLDG++ both exhibit desirable performance in downstream tasks including node classification and dynamic graph anomaly detection. Moreover, CLDG significantly reduces time and space complexity by implicitly exploiting temporal cues instead of complicated sequence models.

</details>


### [114] [Learning to Orchestrate Agents under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27073)
*Mary Chriselda Antony Oliver,Lan Jiang,Aaron Bundi Anampiu,Elaf Almahmoud,Francesco Quinzan,Umang Bhatt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BOT-Orch框架，将异构Agent的自适应编排问题转化为带有OT距离正则化的多臂老虎机问题，在不确定环境下实现了理论遗憾界保证和优于基线的实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent编排研究主要关注性能或成本，而在编排层面通常没有显式建模Agent可靠性和输出分布的不确定性。在协调具有不同可靠性、成本和响应质量的专用AI模型时，需要解决不确定和演化的Agent行为下的序列委托决策问题。

Method: 提出BOT-Orch框架，将编排问题重构为Agent上的多臂老虎机问题，并引入最优传输(OT)距离作为正则项，用于衡量Agent输出分布与任务特定参考分布之间的差异。

Result: 证明了在标准假设下，正则化编排方案具有O(√T)的遗憾界，并且能够诱导具有相同平均奖励但分布对齐不同的Agent之间的偏好排序。在具有异构、非独立同分布Agent行为的合成对抗性任务分配设置中，BOT-Orch优于标准老虎机和启发式基线。

Conclusion: BOT-Orch成功地将不确定性显式建模纳入Agent编排框架，通过理论分析和实验验证证明了该方法在处理异构Agent不确定性编排问题上的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents requires making sequential delegation decisions under uncertain and evolving agent behaviour, e.g., coordinating specialised AI models with varying reliability, cost, and response quality. While prior work on agent orchestration focuses on performance or cost, uncertainty in agent reliability and output distributions is typically not modelled explicitly at the orchestration level. In this work, we study the problem of adaptive orchestration of heterogeneous agents under uncertainty, where a meta-controller must decide when to delegate to an agent, accounting for reliability, cost, and uncertainty. We propose BOT-Orch, a lightweight framework that recasts orchestration as a bandit problem over agents, regularized by OT distances between agent output distributions and task-specific reference distributions. We show that the regularised orchestration enjoys $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under standard assumptions, and provably induces preference ordering among agents with identical mean rewards but differing distributional alignment. Empirically, we demonstrate that BOT-Orch outperforms standard bandit and heuristic baselines in synthetic but adversarial task allocation settings with heterogeneous, non-i.i.d. agent behaviour.

</details>


### [115] [Two Speeds of Learning: A Representation-Readout Decomposition of Grokking and Double Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27078)
*Chi-Ning Chou,Oscar Uzdelewicz,Neng-Chun Chiu,Yao-Yuan Yang,SueYeon Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征-读出分解框架，通过分析编码器表征学习和最终分类器读出校准这两个竞争过程的相对速度波动，统一解释了深度学习训练中的grokking和epoch-wise双下降等异常泛化现象，并能够诊断虚假泛化与真实泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对深度神经网络训练中出现的grokking（训练损失快速下降但测试性能延迟突增）和epoch-wise双下降（训练损失单调递减但测试损失非单调变化）现象的解释往往任务特定，缺乏一个任务无关的分析框架来诊断和解释这些现象在不同任务和架构中的出现。

Method: 提出表征-读出分解方法，分析学习动态中的两个竞争过程：编码器中的表征学习和最终分类器中的读出校准。结合表征几何、神经切线核和线性探测等工具，追踪这两个过程在训练过程中的相对速度变化，从而解释异常泛化动态的产生机制。

Result: （1）在grokking现象中，读出在grokking发生前存在训练偏差，表征学习是渐进的而非缺失的，这与lazy-to-rich解释相矛盾；（2）该框架提供了区分虚假泛化与真实泛化的诊断特征，发现先前报告的MNIST grokking和epoch-wise双下降例子中的异常泛化实际上源于非标准训练配方导致的表征退化和读出错位。

Conclusion: 表征-读出分解为理解学习动态和揭示可解释性研究中的潜在算法提供了一个自上而下的统一框架，能够有效诊断和区分真正的泛化现象与训练配方不当导致的虚假现象。

Abstract: Training loss and accuracy are the standard signals used to monitor generalization during deep neural network training. Two well-documented phenomena complicate this picture: in grokking, train loss falls rapidly while test performance improves abruptly only after a long delay; in epoch-wise double descent, train loss decreases monotonically while test loss or error rises and falls. Existing accounts are often task-specific, and a task-agnostic analysis framework for diagnosing and explaining these phenomena across realistic tasks and architectures is missing. We address this challenge by analyzing two competing processes that underlie learning dynamics: representation learning in the encoder and readout calibration in the final classifier. Using tools from representational geometry, neural tangent kernels, and linear probing, we show that both processes are active throughout training, with the fluctuations of their relative speed giving rise to seemingly anomalous generalization dynamics. Applying the representation-readout decomposition to grokking across a wide range of tasks and architectures, we find that the readout is train-biased before grokking onset, and representation learning is gradual but not absent, contrary to the lazy-to-rich account. The framework further provides diagnostic signatures distinguishing spurious from genuine generalization: in a previously reported MNIST grokking example and an epoch-wise double descent example, apparent delayed or non-monotone generalization is shown to arise from representation degradation and readout misalignment induced by non-standard training recipes. Together, these results establish the representation-readout decomposition as a top-down framework for understanding learning dynamics and revealing underlying algorithms for interpretability research.

</details>


### [116] [Trust Region Q Adjoint Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27079)
*Yonghoon Dong,Kyungmin Lee,Changyeon Kim,Jaehyuk Kim,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM)算法，通过自适应控制路径空间KL散度来解决预训练流策略离策略微调中的不稳定问题，在50个OGBench任务上离线RL成功率达68%，大幅超越最强基线46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练流策略的离策略强化学习因多步采样过程导致优化不稳定。现有方法Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM)虽通过转化为无记忆随机最优控制问题解决了部分问题，但继承了评论家引导改进的基本脆弱性：当评论家条件不良时，小的评论家误差会被放大，常导致模型崩溃。

Method: 提出Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM)算法，通过投影对偶下降自适应控制与预训练流策略的路径空间KL散度。具体地，优化随机最优控制动力学中的置信域参数λ，理论上证明路径空间KL可由λ的闭式函数表示，从而精确控制与预训练流策略的偏差。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上的实验表明，TRQAM在离线RL和离线到在线RL中均持续优于现有方法。特别地，TRQAM在离线RL中达到68%的总体成功率，大幅超越最强基线的46%。

Conclusion: TRQAM通过精确控制路径空间KL散度实现了稳定的离策略强化学习，有效解决了评论家条件不良导致的模型崩溃问题，为预训练流策略的离策略微调提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Off-policy reinforcement learning of pretrained flow policies remains challenging due to the instability of optimization arising from the multi-step sampling process. Recently, Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) addressed this issue by reformulating into a memoryless stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem with a learned critic. However, QAM inherits a fundamental fragility of critic-guided improvement: small critic errors are amplified when critics are ill-conditioned, often leading to model collapse. This paper introduces Trust Region Q-Adjoint Matching (TRQAM), a stable off-policy fine-tuning algorithm that adaptively controls the path-space KL with pretrained flow policies through projected dual descent. Specifically, we optimize the trust-region parameter $λ$ in SOC dynamics, and theoretically show that the path-space KL can be represented by a closed-form function of $λ$. As a result, our method can precisely control the exact deviation from pretrained flow policies, achieving stable off-policy RL. Through experiments on 50 OGBench tasks, TRQAM consistently outperforms prior arts in both offline RL and offline-to-online RL. In particular, TRQAM achieves an overall success rate of 68% in offline RL, substantially improves the strongest baseline at 46%.

</details>


### [117] [Adversarial Dual On-Policy Distillation from Expressive Flow-based Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27095)
*Zhenglin Wan,Jingxuan Wu,Xingrui Yu,Chubin Zhang,Mingcong Lei,Bo An,Ivor W. Tsang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FA-OPD方法，通过对抗性双在线策略蒸馏解决演示学习中的分布偏移问题，在六个机器人基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为克隆方法（包括扩散/流匹配策略）本质上是离线监督学习，仅在专家状态上训练，无法在策略实际访问的状态上获得纠正信号。标准在线策略蒸馏(OPD)假设有强固定的教师，但在仅有演示的控制任务中无法获得这样的教师。

Method: 提出FA-OPD（对抗性双在线策略蒸馏）：1) 从演示中学习流匹配(FM)教师，与轻量级MLP学生协同训练；2) 教师在学生轨迹上提供两个互补信号：奖励通道学习状态-动作对的专家相似性目标并通过长期策略优化驱动在线探索，动作通道在学生访问状态提供稠密局部目标以稳定利用；3) 两者耦合使奖励蒸馏能推广到逐点演示之外，同时动作蒸馏将探索锚定在类专家行为附近。

Result: 在六个机器人导航、操作和运动基准测试中击败强基线方法，在噪声或有限演示条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FA-OPD通过对抗性双通道设计成功将在线策略蒸馏应用于演示学习场景，奖励通道促进泛化，动作通道确保稳定，两者协同实现了优于传统行为克隆方法的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning from demonstrations in embodied control is often cast as behavioral cloning, and recent diffusion or flow-matching policies improve this paradigm by modeling multi-modal expert actions. Yet these methods remain offline supervised learners: the policy is trained only on expert states and receives no corrective signal on the states it actually visits. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural remedy, but standard OPD assumes a strong fixed teacher, which is unavailable in demonstration-only control. We propose \textbf{FA-OPD}, an \emph{adversarial dual on-policy distillation} method in which a Flow Matching (FM) teacher is learned from demonstrations and co-trained with a lightweight MLP student. The teacher provides two complementary signals on student rollouts. The reward channel learns an expert-likeness objective over state-action pairs and drives online exploration through long-horizon policy optimization. The action channel supplies dense local targets at student-visited states, stabilizing exploitation. FA-OPD couples them so that reward distillation enables generalization beyond point-wise demonstrations, while action distillation keeps exploration anchored near expert-like behavior. Across six robot navigation, manipulation, and locomotion benchmarks, FA-OPD beats strong baselines and shows much stronger robustness under noisy or limited demonstrations.

</details>


### [118] [Mildly Overparameterized ReLU Networks on Orthogonal Data: Incremental Learning and Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27097)
*James Town,Etienne Boursier,Ben Lewis,Matthias Englert,Ranko Lazic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次严格证明了ReLU网络的增量学习过程，在小初始化和正交数据条件下，网络收敛至插值解且其复杂度与最优插值器同阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络训练依赖一阶优化方法，但其理论刻画尚不完整，特别是在轻度过参数化场景下。作者旨在理解两层ReLU网络在小初始化下的梯度流动态，以填补理论空白。

Method: 研究两层ReLU网络在小初始化、正交训练数据条件下的梯度流动态。通过分析初始化尺度趋于零时的极限流，证明其收敛到一个鞍点到鞍点的跳跃过程，展现出增量学习现象——每个鞍点处激活一个新神经元。

Result: （1）恢复了已知结论：当网络宽度m ≳ log(n)时，网络以高概率插值训练数据；（2）获得新的隐式偏倚结果：学习到的插值器具有平方ℓ₂范数，其缩放为O(√n)，与最小ℓ₂范数插值器相差常数因子。

Conclusion: 本文首次为ReLU网络提供了增量学习过程的严格证明，表明轻度过参数化网络可收敛到复杂度与最优插值器同阶的插值解，为理解神经网络的优化动力学和泛化行为提供了新见解。

Abstract: The successful training of neural networks hinges on the use of first order optimization methods, yet the theoretical characterization of these methods remains incomplete. This is especially true in settings with mild overparameterization. In this work, we study the gradient flow dynamics of two-layer ReLU networks from small initialization with orthogonal training data. We prove the limiting flow converges to a saddle-to-saddle jump process as the initialization scale tends to zero, revealing an incremental learning phenomenon in which a new neuron activates at each saddle. This analysis recovers the known result of Dana et al. (2025, arXiv:2502.16977) that the network interpolates the training data with high probability as soon as $m \gtrsim \log(n)$, where $m$ is the network width and $n$ is the number of training samples. This incremental process characterization also allows us to derive a novel implicit bias result: the learned interpolator has a squared $\ell_2$-norm scaling as $\sqrt{n}$, which is within a constant factor of the minimal $\ell_2$-norm interpolator. More broadly, our work provides the first rigorous proof of an incremental learning process for ReLU networks, whilst suggesting mildly overparameterized networks can converge to interpolating solutions whose complexity is of the same order as that of the optimal interpolator.

</details>


### [119] [High-Quality Synthetic Financial Time-Series using a GAN-Diffusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27113)
*Giuseppe Masi,Andrea Coletta,Novella Bartolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个质量感知生成框架，通过结合C-GAN和扩散模型来生成更逼真的金融市场合成数据，特别是改进了跨资产相关性的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构和企业日益使用合成数据解决数据稀缺和生成反事实市场场景，但现有的通用架构难以重现金融时间序列的所有统计特性（即典型事实），特别是资产间的相关性结构。

Method: 提出了CoMeTS-GAN（相关多变量时间序列GAN），一种条件生成对抗网络，用于联合生成相关股票的中间价和成交量时间序列；然后将GAN架构集成到最先进的扩散模型中，利用GAN的判别器作为质量评估模块指导扩散过程，在生成的时间序列中强制执行学习到的相关性结构。

Result: 实验验证表明，该框架相比领先的生成架构能更有效地捕捉股票市场的典型事实，并更好地建模资产间相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架为现实的股票市场模拟提供了一个轻量级且响应迅速的解决方案，能够显式建模资产间相关性结构，在生成高质量金融市场合成数据方面具有优势。

Abstract: In recent years, financial institutions and firms have increasingly adopted synthetic data to address data scarcity and to generate counterfactual market scenarios. However, reproducing all the statistical properties of financial time series, commonly known as stylized facts, remains an open challenge for many existing general-purpose architectures. In this paper, we present a quality-aware generative framework that combines two classes of generative methods, demonstrating how their integration addresses existing limitations while enhancing the realism of synthetic data. Specifically, we first introduce CoMeTS-GAN (Correlated Multivariate Time Series GAN), a Conditional Generative Adversarial Network (C-GAN) designed to jointly generate mid-price and volume time-series for correlated stocks. We then show how our GAN architecture can be incorporated into state-of-the-art diffusion models to enhance the quality of generated correlation structures. Specifically, the GAN's Critic serves as a quality evaluation module that guides the diffusion process, enforcing learned correlation structures in the generated time-series. Our framework offers a lightweight and responsive solution for realistic stock market simulation, explicitly modeling inter-asset correlation structures. We experimentally validate our framework against leading generative architectures, showing that it more effectively captures the stylized facts of stock markets and models inter-asset correlations.

</details>


### [120] [DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference for Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27130)
*John Donaghy,Shikhar Rastogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEI分布式质量-多样性搜索框架，通过异构大语言模型协作实现显著性能提升，证明了模型多样性（而非单纯并行）是分布式LLM搜索的关键驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的同构并行搜索方法将单一模型的归纳偏置复制到所有工作节点，限制了搜索空间的探索广度。不同LLM具有独特的创造性先验，可以作为行为新颖性的互补来源，但现有框架未能有效利用这种异构性。

Method: 1. 构建DEI（Diversity in Evolutionary Inference）分布式质量-多样性搜索框架，将异构LLM作为变异算子分配到各节点
2. 采用非阻塞集体操作实现节点间通信
3. 扩展数字红皇后框架，节点在每轮结束时共享局部最优解作为下一轮种群的种子
4. 创建跨模型对抗压力以提升鲁棒性

Result: 在Core War竞争编程基准测试中，四节点异构集成（GPT-5.4-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.2、Claude Haiku 4.5）相比单节点基线：
- 合并档案QD-Score提升124%（45.90 vs 20.46）
- 覆盖率提升28%（80.6% vs 63.0%）
- 在QD-Score、覆盖率和泛化能力上均优于同等预算的同构集成

Conclusion: 首次提供实证证据表明模型多样性（而非单纯并行性）是分布式基于LLM的质量-多样性搜索中性能提升的关键驱动因素，为异构AI系统协作提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We present DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference, a distributed Quality-Diversity (QD) search framework that assigns heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) as mutation operators across peer nodes communicating with non-blocking collective operations. Unlike homogeneous parallel search, which replicates a single model's inductive biases across all workers, DEI treats each LLM's distinct creative prior as a complementary source of behavioral novelty. Extending the Digital Red Queen framework with DEI, nodes share local optimal solutions at the end of each round to seed the next round's population. This creates cross-model adversarial pressure that drives robustness beyond intra-model self-play. Evaluated on the Core War domain, a competitive programming benchmark in which Redcode warrior programs battle inside a simulated machine, a four-node heterogeneous ensemble (GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieves 124 percent higher merged-archive QD-Score (45.90 vs. 20.46) and 28 percent higher coverage (80.6 percent vs. 63.0 percent of cells) than a single-node baseline at equal total LLM-call budget. The heterogeneous ensemble also outperforms an equally-budgeted homogeneous ensemble on QD-Score, coverage, and held-out solution generality across all four model families. These results provide the first empirical evidence that model diversity, not merely parallelism, is the key driver of gain in distributed LLM-based QD search.

</details>


### [121] [Deep-layer limit and stability analysis of the basic forward-backward-splitting induced network (II): learning problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27133)
*Xuan Lin,Chunlin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了基本前向-反向分裂(FBS)诱导网络的训练问题向深层极限系统学习问题的Γ-收敛性，证明了网络最优学习参数的聚点为深层极限系统学习问题的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度展开神经网络已引起广泛关注，其中许多重要架构基于前向-反向分裂(FBS)算法构建。在前期对前向系统分析的基础上，需要进一步研究相应的学习问题的理论性质。

Method: 基于差分/微分包含表述，在温和假设下建立基本FBS诱导网络的训练问题向深层极限系统学习问题的收敛性质，采用Γ-收敛论证方法，并定性分析学习问题的扰动稳定性。

Result: 证明了网络最优学习参数的任何聚点都是深层极限系统学习问题的解，建立了严格的收敛性理论框架。通过简单数值实验验证了主要收敛结果。

Conclusion: 成功建立了FBS诱导网络训练的理论基础，为深度展开网络的学习问题提供了严格的数学分析框架。

Abstract: Deep unfolding neural networks derived from iterative optimization schemes and numerical ordinary/partial differential equations (ODEs/PDEs) have attracted much attention in data science over the last decade. Therein, numerous important network architectures were constructed from the basic forward-backward-splitting (FBS) algorithm. In this paper, we continue our research on the most basic FBS-induced network, an architecture unrolled from the original FBS algorithm by incorporating direct parameter relaxations. Following the difference/differential inclusion formulations in our previous forward system analyses, we here consider some theoretical aspects of corresponding learning problems. Under some mild assumptions, we establish a general convergence property of the training problem of the basic FBS-induced network to the learning problem of the deep-layer limit system, implying a $Γ$-convergence argument showing that any cluster point of the optimal learning parameters for the network is a solution to the learning problem of the deep-layer limit system. A qualitative analysis of perturbation stabilities of these learning problems is also presented. A simple numerical experiment is conducted to validate our main general convergence result.

</details>


### [122] [The Role of Causal Features in Strategic Classification for Robustness and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27163)
*Antonio Gois,Sophia Gunluk,Nir Rosenfeld,Nidhi Hegde,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了因果模型与策略分类的理论联系，证明因果分类在大规模用户适应后具有最优性能，并发现因果特征能使机构与用户的长远利益一致，突破了以往认为此类方法存在社会成本的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略分类中，用户会改变特征以提升分类任务的效用（如贷款审批），导致分布偏移。因果模型已被证明能够约束最坏情况下的分布外风险，因此本文旨在建立因果模型与策略分类的理论联系，以应对用户适应带来的分布偏移挑战。

Method: 采用因果建模方法分析策略分类问题，在特定噪声有界条件下推导因果分类的最优性；当假设不成立时，通过分解最优分类器的分布外交叉熵风险为偏差项和非全特征使用项来分析；同时从激励对齐角度分析因果特征对机构与用户长期关系的影响。

Result: （1）在特定噪声有界条件下，因果分类在大规模用户适应后达到最优分类误差；（2）当假设不成立时，分布外交叉熵风险可分解为分布外偏差项和因未使用所有可观测特征产生的项，揭示了因果分类器的优势条件；（3）因果特征能使机构与用户的长期激励对齐，与以往强调社会成本的研究形成对比。理论预测通过合成数据实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 本文为策略分类问题提供了因果建模的理论框架，证明了因果方法在用户适应场景下的优越性和激励对齐潜力，为策略分类系统的设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: In strategic classification, an institution (e.g., a bank) anticipates adaptation from users who change their features to increase utility in a classification task (e.g., loan repayment). Since a key challenge is the distribution shift induced by users, we turn to causal models, which have been shown to bound the worst-case out-of-distribution (OOD) risk, and establish several new results that link causality and strategic classification. First, we show that causal classification leads to optimal classification error after any sufficiently large adaptation, when the noise is bounded in a certain way. Second, when these assumptions do not hold, we show OOD cross-entropy risk of optimal classifiers decomposes into an OOD bias term and a term arising from not using all observable features, allowing us to understand when causal classifiers have an advantage. Finally, we show that the use of causal features can allow alignment of long-term incentives between institutions and users, contrasting with previous work that highlights social costs of such approaches. We validate our theory empirically on synthetic data, finding that our results predict behavior in practice.

</details>


### [123] [Nonlinear Data Integration via Kernel Methods for Data Collaboration Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27219)
*Yamato Suetake,Yuta Kawakami,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非线性核积分(NKI)方法解决数据协作分析中非线性降维后的表示对齐问题，通过图正则化和中心化约束提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据协作分析方法主要依赖线性变换，存在重构风险；非线性降维虽可降低风险，但传统线性积分方法无法准确对齐非线性变换产生的中间表示；且现有方法未显式利用几何信息或目标变量信息。

Method: 首先将线性核积分(LKI)形式化为线性积分方法，然后将其核化得到非线性核积分(NKI)；NKI通过核岭回归和特征值问题获得全局最优解；引入图正则化和中心化约束，使目标表示能捕获对下游分析有用的几何和目标变量信息。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，NKI在非线性降维条件下的分类准确率优于现有线性积分方法；目标变量感知的图正则化和中心化进一步提升性能；降维选择显著影响分类准确率和重构风险。

Conclusion: NKI方法有效解决了非线性降维后的表示对齐问题，在保证隐私安全的同时提升了数据协作分析的下游任务性能，为隐私保护的数据协作提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Collaborative analysis of decentralized confidential datasets is important, but direct sharing of original datasets is often restricted by privacy and institutional constraints. Data collaboration (DC) analysis transforms each dataset into privacy-preserving intermediate representations via party-specific obfuscation functions and integrates them into common collaboration representations using an anchor dataset. However, many existing DC analysis methods rely on linear transformations for data obfuscation and integration, which may increase reconstruction risk. Although nonlinear dimensionality reduction can mitigate this risk, conventional linear integration methods cannot accurately align intermediate representations produced by nonlinear transformations. Moreover, existing integration methods mainly minimize discrepancies among parties and do not explicitly incorporate geometric or target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. To overcome these limitations, we first formulate linear kernel integration (LKI) as a linear integration method and then kernelize it to obtain nonlinear kernel integration (NKI). NKI admits a globally optimal solution via kernel ridge regression and an eigenvalue problem. We also introduce graph regularization and a centering constraint so that the target representation can capture geometric and target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. Experiments on image classification tasks demonstrate that NKI improves classification accuracy over existing linear integration methods under nonlinear dimensionality reduction, with further gains from target-variable-aware graph regularization and centering. The results also show that dimensionality reduction choices substantially affect both classification accuracy and reconstruction risk.

</details>


### [124] [Explainable Comparison of Feature-Based and Deep Learning Models for TROPOMI Methane Plume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27236)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Joannes D. Maasakkers,Berend J. Schuit,Ilse Aben*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了基于特征的传统机器学习模型与基于图像的深度学习模型在甲烷羽流-伪影分类任务上的表现，为卫星观测甲烷排放检测工作流提供了模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星观测（如S5P/TROPOMI）结合羽流检测算法是检测大型甲烷排放的关键手段，但数据中存在大量由高程变化、反照率梯度、气溶胶、海岸线等因素引起的伪影，这些伪影在视觉上与真实甲烷羽流相似。传统的支持向量机分类器依赖专家设计的标量特征，限制了算法接收的信息范围，破坏了像素间的空间关系，并在统计聚合过程中丢失信息。

Method: 研究对比了两类模型：基于特征的模型（支持向量机SVC、随机森林Random Forest、XGBoost）和基于图像的深度学习模型（ResNet-18、ResNet-34），在平衡和不平衡评估设置下进行甲烷羽流-伪影分类。同时应用基于SHAP的可解释性分析方法来解释模型结果。

Result: 研究对特征基模型和图像基模型在平衡和非平衡评估设置下的分类性能进行了系统比较，并通过SHAP可解释性分析揭示了不同模型家族的决策机制。

Conclusion: 研究成果为实际甲烷筛选工作流（如CAMS甲烷热点探测器）中的模型选择提供了实用指导，有助于提高全球甲烷排放检测的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Continuous and global detection of large methane emissions is a crucial step for global warming mitigation. Satellite observations, such as from S5P/TROPOMI, combined with plume detection algorithms, can play a key role in this effort. However, not all TROPOMI plume detections that look like methane emission plumes are the result of actual emissions. A significant part of the plume-like features in the data are retrieval artifacts. Such artifacts could be the result of variations in elevation or albedo gradients, high concentrations of aerosols, coastal lines, water bodies, etc. Previous work approached the problem of plume-artifact classification by means of a Support Vector Machine Classifier (SVC), trained on an extensive set of observation-based scalar features designed by domain experts. However, such an approach limits the information scope received by the algorithm to what is deemed to be important by the experts, breaks the spatial relationship between pixels, and loses information during the process of statistical aggregation. In this study, we compare feature-based (SVC, Random Forest, XGBoost) and image-based (ResNet-18, ResNet-34) models for methane plume-artifact classification under balanced and imbalanced evaluation settings. To interpret the results, we apply SHAP-based explainability to both model families. Our findings provide practical guidance for model selection in operational methane-screening workflows such as the CAMS Methane Hotspot Explorer.

</details>


### [125] [Symbolic Regression via Latent Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27245)
*Xieting Chu,Sriram Vishwanath,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出潜在方程嵌入（LEE）框架，通过在功能锚定的潜在空间中进行迭代摊销推理，缩小神经符号回归中的摊销差距，生成比强基线方法简洁2-10倍的表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号回归方法通过训练编码器将观测数据一次性映射到表达式来实现摊销搜索，但这种一次性预测与真实后验之间存在残留的摊销差距，影响了预测准确性。

Method: LEE框架包含三个核心组件：联合嵌入符号标记和数值观测的编码器f_θ、从潜在向量重建公式的表达式解码器g_expr、以及预测函数值的评估解码器g_eval。在推理时，通过重编码解码后的表达式与观测数据进行迭代优化，并结合可微的g_eval进行连续梯度下降与离散重编码的混合迭代细化。

Result: 在SRBench基准测试中，跨越三个噪声级别，对比19个基线方法（涵盖遗传规划、符号-神经混合方法和预训练Transformer），LEE生成的表达式比最强的准确性导向基线方法简洁2-10倍，复杂度为8-11 versus 20-90，推进了准确性-复杂度帕累托前沿的低复杂度区域，并在噪声增加时表现出优雅的性能退化。

Conclusion: LEE成功缩小了神经符号回归的摊销差距，通过迭代摊销推理在功能锚定的潜在空间中实现了表达式质量和复杂度的良好平衡，为符号回归领域提供了有效的新方法。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks closed-form mathematical expressions that fit observed data. Neural SR methods amortize the search by training an encoder to map observations directly to expressions in a single pass, but this amortized inference leaves a residual amortization gap between its one-shot prediction and the true posterior. We propose Latent Equation Embedding (LEE), a framework that closes this gap through iterative amortized inference in a functionally grounded latent space. LEE learns a shared latent space Z equipped with three components: an encoder f_theta that jointly embeds symbolic tokens and numerical observations into a single latent vector z; an expression decoder g_expr that reconstructs formulas from z; and an evaluation decoder g_eval that predicts function values from z, explicitly grounding the latent space in functional behavior. At inference, LEE performs iterative refinement by re-encoding decoded expressions jointly with observations, progressively improving the latent estimate. LEE uses the encoder itself as a learned inference optimizer: each re-encoding step implicitly computes the mismatch between the candidate and the data. Because g_eval is differentiable in z, we additionally interleave continuous gradient descent with discrete re-encoding, yielding a hybrid iterative and gradient refinement procedure. On SRBench across three noise levels, against 19 baselines spanning genetic programming, symbolic-neural hybrids, and pre-trained Transformers, LEE produces expressions 2--10x simpler than the strongest accuracy-oriented baselines, including Operon, GP-GOMEA, TPSR, RAG-SR, and GenSR, with complexity 8--11 versus 20--90. These results advance the low-complexity region of the accuracy-complexity Pareto frontier and show graceful degradation as noise increases.

</details>


### [126] [LUCoS: Latent Unsupervised Context Selection for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27254)
*Oroel Ipas,Guillermo Gomez-Trenado,Rocío Romero-Zaliz,Isaac Triguero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LUCoS方法，利用无监督Prior-Fitted Network的潜在嵌入空间进行表格数据的上下文选择，解决了原始特征空间中距离度量不可靠的问题，在67个数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型（如TabPFN）的预测性能直接依赖于标注上下文集的选择。现有方法在原始特征空间中选择实例，但表格数据具有异构类型、混合尺度、非线性交互等特点，导致原始空间距离度量不可靠，随着标注预算增加，选择效果甚至低于随机选择。冷启动设置下的实例选择问题在TFM文献中鲜有研究。

Method: 提出LUCoS（Latent Unsupervised Context Selection）方法：（1）使用无监督Prior-Fitted Network将表格数据嵌入到潜在表示空间；（2）在该潜在几何空间中，基于代表性选择medoids作为上下文；（3）避免了原始特征空间几何的不可靠性，利用嵌入空间提供有意义的表示几何。

Result: 在67个OpenML-CC18数据集和六个低标注预算设置下，LUCoS在平均AUC、ACC和F1指标上均排名第一，结论在不同指标和数据集级鲁棒性检验下保持稳定。增益分解揭示：最小预算时主要收益来自覆盖性保证；随着预算增加，决定因素变为测量覆盖性的表示空间质量。

Conclusion: 可靠的无监督上下文选择较少依赖于选择器的复杂性，而更多地依赖于在有意义的表示几何中定义代表性。LUCoS通过用潜在嵌入几何替代原始特征几何，有效缓解了原始特征空间选择的失败问题。

Abstract: Selecting which instances to label is a key challenge in low-label tabular learning. For recent Tabular Foundation Models such as TabPFN, context selection directly determines predictive performance. Supervised oracle experiments show that carefully chosen labeled context sets can strongly outperform random selection under the same labeling budget. However, the cold-start setting, where instances must be selected before any labels are available, has received little attention in the TFM literature. This problem is fundamentally geometric. In vision and language, foundation models induce embedding spaces where simple geometric selection methods are effective. In contrast, tabular instance selection has so far been performed predominantly in the original tabular space, which lacks a natural metric; heterogeneous types, mixed scales, and nonlinear interactions make raw-space distances unreliable for context construction, and original-space selection falls below random on the majority of datasets as the budget grows. We propose LUCoS (Latent Unsupervised Context Selection), which replaces raw-feature geometry with the latent geometry induced by embeddings from an unsupervised Prior-Fitted Network (PFN) and selects representative medoids as context. Evaluated on 67 OpenML-CC18 datasets across six low-label budgets, LUCoS ranks first under mean AUC, ACC, and F1, with conclusions stable across metrics and dataset-level robustness checks. A gain decomposition reveals a simple mechanism: at the smallest budgets, the main benefit comes from enforcing coverage; as the budget increases, the decisive factor becomes the representation space in which coverage is measured. LUCoS mitigates failures of original feature space selection, showing that reliable unsupervised context selection depends less on selector sophistication than on defining representativeness in a meaningful representation geometry.

</details>


### [127] [Kan Extension Transformers: A Categorical Unification of Attention, Diffusion, and Predict-Detach Self-Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27259)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kan Extension Transformers (KETs)作为统一多种Transformer实现的范畴论框架，将Transformer层视为加权结构扩展算子，并在实验中验证了其有效性，发现预测-分离机制带来的提升最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer实现种类繁多，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解和连接不同的架构设计。作者希望找到一种数学上严谨的方式来统一标准注意力机制、几何Transformer等不同实现，并探索它们之间的内在联系，特别是与扩散式补全方法的关联。

Method: 提出Kan Extension Transformers框架，将Transformer层建模为加权结构扩展算子：标准注意力是单点邻域情况，几何Transformer的关联混合是稀疏边限制情况，KET则是高阶单纯形情况。引入预测-分离机制，通过在分离的预测载体而非教师强制的隐藏状态上操作扩展算子，实现有效的自条件化，在不泄露黄金未来token的情况下暴露非因果结构。

Result: 在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103数据集上对12种不同的Transformer实现进行了全面实验验证。在严格因果设置中，二次KET在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上是表现最强的因果架构。在所有数据集上，最大的性能提升来自于预测-分离机制而非仅改变邻域族。

Conclusion: KET框架成功统一了多样化的Transformer实现，为理解不同架构提供了清晰的数学视角。实验表明，通过改变邻域族（从单点到高阶单纯形）可以带来改进，但更重要的是改变训练机制（预测-分离），这为未来Transformer架构设计提供了重要启示：训练策略的创新可能比架构本身的修改带来更大的收益。

Abstract: We propose Kan Extension Transformers (KETs) as a unifying categorical framework for a diverse group of Transformer implementations. The core claim is that a Transformer layer can be viewed as a weighted structured extension operator: standard attention is the singleton-neighborhood case, Geometric Transformer style incidence mixing is a sparse edge-restricted case, and KET is the higher-order simplicial case. This lens also clarifies a bridge to diffusion-style completion. When the extension operator acts on detached predictive carriers instead of teacher-forced hidden states, it becomes a valid self-conditioning mechanism that exposes noncausal structure without leaking gold future tokens. We include a comprehensive experimental validation of 12 different Transformer implementations varying across strict-causal and predict-detach regimes on Penn Treebank, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. In the strict-causal setting, quadratic KET is the strongest model among the compared causal architectures on WikiText-2 and WikiText-103. Across all datasets, however, the largest gains come from the predict-detach regime rather than from changing the neighborhood family alone.

</details>


### [128] [Transfer Learning using 66 Diseases for Disease Forecasting Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27269)
*Lauren J Beesley,Alexander C Murph,Dave Osthus,Lauren A Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过整合66种传染病和多源数据流训练机器学习模型，发现84.9%的情况下引入其他数据流能改善预测性能，但数据质量至关重要，差异过大的数据可能损害预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病预测模型通常依赖单一数据流，在历史数据短或噪声大时表现脆弱。虽然近期研究显示整合多报告系统或使用跨疾病迁移学习可改善性能，但这些方法尚未被大规模扩展应用。本研究旨在大幅扩展这些方法，探索跨大规模疾病和多数据流训练的潜力。

Method: 收集涵盖66种传染病和多个数据流的数据集，构建机器学习预测模型，系统研究整合不同数据流对20种不同疾病数据流预测的影响，并创建公开可用的传染病预测数据库。

Result: 在84.9%的时间序列和模型结构中，引入其他数据流改善了预测性能。但研究同时发现，添加数据的质量至关重要，当添加的数据与目标数据流差异极大时，反而会降低预测性能。

Conclusion: 多数据流整合在传染病预测中具有广泛价值，但需谨慎选择相关数据源。研究的重要贡献在于建立了面向传染病预测社区的公开数据库，为该领域后续研究提供重要资源。

Abstract: Disease forecasting models typically rely on a single data stream, making models brittle when histories are short or noisy. Recent top-performing models have shown that synthesizing multiple reporting systems for the same disease improves performance. Other recent work takes this idea a step further, using transfer learning to train a forecasting model for one disease using data from a different disease. We expand upon each of these approaches greatly, training machine learning models on data that span 66 infectious diseases and several data streams. We investigate the value of incorporating different data streams for forecasting 20 different disease data streams. We find that incorporating other data streams improves forecasting in the vast majority (84.9%) of time series and model structures considered. However, our work highlights that the quality of the added data matters, where adding data extremely different from the target data stream can sometimes degrade forecast performance. A major contribution of this work is in compiling a publicly-available database of data for use by the infectious disease forecasting community.

</details>


### [129] [Falcon-X: A Time Series Foundation Model for Heterogeneous Multivariate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27286)
*Yiding Liu,Yifan Hu,Hongjie Xia,Peiyuan Liu,Hongzhou Chen,Xilin Dai,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Falcon-X提出了一种新的多变量时间序列预测范式，通过将变量映射到统一的潜在原型空间并引入正负语义亲和力评估机制，解决了现有模型在语义对齐和关系表达方面的局限性，在基准测试中达到了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型多为单变量模型，最近的多变量建模尝试直接在原始变量空间操作，存在两个根本性局限：(1) 原始空间群组混合缺乏对齐异构物理量的专用机制；(2) 标准非负注意力无法捕获现实世界系统中普遍存在的复杂协同和对抗性交互。这些限制严重影响了语义对齐和关系表达能力。

Method: 提出Falcon-X框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 将变量从原始空间解耦并映射到统一潜在原型空间；(2) 统一原型差分注意力机制，显式评估正负语义亲和力以对齐异构变量；(3) 潜在实体注意力在共享空间中高效执行跨变量交互，实现零样本结构迁移；(4) 变量重组路由器通过请求-分发机制重建变量特定轨迹。

Result: 在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准测试上进行广泛评估，Falcon-X实现了最先进的预测性能。该方法为复杂多变量环境提供了一个有原则且可扩展的范式，并已公开发布以支持未来研究。

Conclusion: Falcon-X成功解决了多变量时间序列预测中的语义对齐和关系表达挑战，通过潜在原型空间的创新设计实现了更好的跨变量建模能力，为复杂多变量环境下的预测任务建立了新的技术范式。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are transforming the forecasting paradigm through large-scale cross-domain pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs remain univariate, and recent efforts to enable cross-variate modeling still operate directly within the raw variate space. This design introduces fundamental limitations in semantic alignment and relational expressivity. Specifically, raw-space group mixing lacks a dedicated mechanism to align heterogeneous physical quantities, while standard non-negative attention fails to capture the complex synergistic and antagonistic interactions ubiquitous in real-world systems. To address these challenges, we propose Falcon-X, decouples variates from the raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. Falcon-X employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism that explicitly evaluates both positive and negative semantic affinities to explicitly align heterogeneous variates. Cross-variate interactions are then efficiently performed within this shared space via Latent Entity Attention, naturally facilitating zero-shot structural transfer. Finally, a Variate Reassembly Router robustly reconstructs variate-specific trajectories via a request-and-dispatch mechanism. Extensive evaluations on the GIFT-Eval and fev-bench benchmarks demonstrate that Falcon-X achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance, offering a principled and scalable paradigm for complex multivariate environments. Falcon-X is publicly released to support future research.

</details>


### [130] [Causal Risk Minimization for High-Dimensional Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27281)
*Nikita Dhawan,Arnav Paruthi,Andrew Kim,Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova,Chris J. Maddison*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高维干预空间（如文本）的因果推断方法，通过将因果误差分解为递增阶数的矩平衡误差来改进估计，并实现了将高维干预效果投影到低维属性上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的因果估计器通常假设所有可能的干预都被观测到，这在干预空间变化巨大时（例如所有文本字符串的空间）是不可行的。因此需要解决高维干预空间的因果推断问题。

Method: 将因果推断重新表述为学习问题，证明因果误差可以分解为递增阶数的矩平衡误差序列，并设计了直接改进因果估计的目标函数。同时提出了将高维干预效果投影到低维干预属性的方法，使单一模型能够回答多个因果问题而无需额外的属性特定训练。

Result: 在具有高维连续、离散和文本干预的设置中进行了经验评估（文本干预使用了Amazon Reviews的半合成数据集），实验证明了高阶平衡误差优化的优势，以及投影因果估计与属性特定估计器相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法有效地解决了高维干预空间中的因果推断问题，通过高阶平衡误差优化改进了因果估计，并且投影方法能够用单一模型处理多个因果问题，无需额外的属性特定训练。

Abstract: Predicting the effect of interventions with many possible variations, e.g., therapeutic content that affects mental health outcomes or an earnings call transcript that drives movement in share price, is useful across several domains. However, classical causal estimators tend to assume that all possible interventions are observed, which is infeasible when interventions vary widely, for instance, in the space of all text strings. We adapt a well-known approach of recasting causal inference as a learning problem, to address high-dimensional treatment spaces. Specifically, under standard assumptions like no unobserved confounding, we show that causal error decomposes into a series of moment-balancing errors of increasing order, and design objectives that directly improve causal estimation. We also show how to project the effect of a high-dimensional treatment onto lower-dimensional treatment attributes, which allows a single model to answer several causal questions without additional attribute-specific training. We empirically evaluate our estimators in settings with high-dimensional continuous, discrete, and text treatments, the last of which used a semi-synthetic dataset of Amazon Reviews. Our experiments demonstrate the benefit of higher-order balance error optimization and competitive performance of projected causal estimates with attribute-specific estimators.

</details>


### [131] [Guiding LLM Post-training Data Engineering with Model Internals from Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27354)
*Yi Jing,Zao Dai,Jinwu Hu,Zijun Yao,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li,Xiaozhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAERL框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从LLM内部状态提取多样性、难度和质量三种数据属性，用于强化学习中的数据工程，相比基准方法准确率提升3.00%，训练步数减少20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM后训练数据工程方法主要依赖外部信号，忽略了模型内部蕴含的丰富信息。作者认为模型内部状态编码了关于模型如何处理训练数据的丰富信息，应利用这些内在信号指导数据工程。

Method: 提出SAERL框架，使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从模型内部提取三种数据属性：(1)多样性：通过SAE空间聚类和适度的批次混合控制批次多样性；(2)难度：构建难度代理用于易到难的课程学习排序；(3)质量：训练质量探测器用于数据过滤。基于这些属性执行具体的数据工程操作。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上，SAERL相比vanilla GRPO平均准确率提升3.00%，达到目标准确率所需训练步数减少20%。在模型规模和RL算法上均有稳定提升。实验表明SAE在不同模型家族和规模间有效迁移，可作为轻量级可复用的数据工程工具。

Conclusion: 模型内部状态是后训练数据工程中强大且实用的信号来源，基于SAE的数据工程方法能够有效提升LLM强化学习的效率和效果。

Abstract: Model internals encode rich information about how a large language model (LLM) processes its training data; however, post-training data engineering largely relies on external signals and ignores rich intrinsic signals lying in model internals. We propose SAERL, a data engineering framework for LLM reinforcement learning (RL). It models three intrinsic data properties: diversity, difficulty, and quality, using model internals extracted with Sparse Autoencoder (SAE), an advanced mechanistic interpretability tool. Each property grounds a concrete data engineering operation: SAE-space clustering with moderate batch mixing for batch diversity control, a difficulty proxy for easy-to-hard curriculum ordering, and a quality probe for data filtering. SAERL improves average accuracy by 3.00% over vanilla GRPO and reaches target accuracy with 20% fewer training steps on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, with consistent gains across model scales and RL algorithms. Experiments show that SAE transfers effectively across model families and scales, serving as a lightweight and reusable data engineering tool. These results demonstrate that model internals are a powerful and practical source of signals for post-training data engineering.

</details>


### [132] [MobileMoE: Scaling On-Device Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27358)
*Yanbei Chen,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Jacob Szwejbka,Digant Desai,Zechun Liu,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobileMoE，一系列面向端侧部署的亚十亿活跃参数MoE语言模型，在14个基准测试中以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs达到或超越现有密集型端侧LLM，并在商用智能手机上实现了2-4倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)架构已在千亿参数规模的模型中成为主流，但在亚十亿规模的端侧部署场景中，MoE的优势尚未被充分探索。现有研究缺乏对端侧约束下MoE架构的系统性分析与优化。

Method: 1) 提出了面向端侧的MoE扩展定律，在移动设备内存和计算约束下联合优化MoE架构，识别出"适度稀疏性配合细粒度共享专家"的最优配置；2) 设计了四阶段训练方案：预训练、中期训练、指令微调和量化感知训练；3) 实现了首个在商用智能手机上的高效MoE推理系统。

Result: MobileMoE系列模型（0.3-0.9B活跃参数，1.3-5.3B总参数）在14个基准测试中：以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs达到或超越领先的端侧密集型LLM；以最多60%更少的参数达到或超越SOTA MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B；在INT4量化下，相比密集基线MobileLLM-Pro实现1.8-3.8倍预填充加速和2.2-3.4倍解码加速。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统性地探索了端侧MoE架构，证明了MoE在亚十亿参数规模下的有效性，建立了端侧LLM的新Pareto前沿，并为实际移动端部署提供了完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the de facto architecture for hundred-billion-parameter language models, yet its advantages at sub-billion scales for on-device deployment remain largely unexplored. To close this gap, we present MobileMoE, a family of on-device MoE language models with sub-billion active parameters (0.3-0.9B active and 1.3-5.3B total) that establish a new Pareto frontier for on-device LLMs. We first formulate an on-device MoE scaling law that jointly optimizes MoE architecture under mobile memory and compute constraints, identifying an on-device sweet spot - moderate sparsity with fine-grained and shared experts - that is simultaneously memory and compute-optimal. Building on the derived architectures, we train MobileMoE with a four-stage recipe covering pre-training, mid-training, instruction fine-tuning, and quantization-aware training, all on open-source datasets. Across 14 benchmarks, MobileMoE matches or exceeds leading on-device dense LLMs with 2-4$\times$ fewer inference FLOPs, and matches or surpasses the state-of-the-art MoE OLMoE-1B-7B with up to 60% fewer parameters. To bridge the last mile to mobile deployment, we provide the first efficient MoE inference on commodity smartphones with comprehensive on-device profiling. At comparable INT4 weight memory, MobileMoE-S delivers $1.8$-$3.8\times$ faster prefill and $2.2$-$3.4\times$ faster decode than the dense baseline MobileLLM-Pro.

</details>


### [133] [Detectability in Diversity: Improved Canary Crafting for Privacy Auditing in One Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27292)
*Mathieu Dagréou,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单次运行隐私审计的金丝雀样本优化方法，通过结合影响力函数的贪心初始化和双层优化过程，生成既高可检测性又低干扰的金丝雀样本，从而在更低计算成本下实现更强的隐私泄露估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次运行隐私审计方法中，金丝雀样本之间的相互干扰会导致隐私泄露估计弱于多次运行方法。为了解决这一问题，需要设计既能被高效检测又相互干扰最小的金丝雀样本，以提高单次运行隐私审计的效率和准确性。

Method: 提出了一种金丝雀样本优化方法：首先使用基于影响力函数的贪心初始化策略，然后通过双层优化过程最大化样本的可区分性，同时在嵌入空间促进样本多样性。这种方法可以使用计算高效的双层优化算法来实现。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在比现有金丝雀样本构建方法更低的计算成本下，实现了更强的隐私泄露估计，证明了优化后的金丝雀样本在减少相互干扰的同时保持了高可检测性。

Conclusion: 通过优化金丝雀样本以同时实现高可检测性和低干扰性，可以显著提升单次运行隐私审计的性能，为机器学习模型的隐私评估提供了一种更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy auditing aims to empirically assess privacy leakage in machine learning models using membership inference attacks (MIAs), and to derive lower bounds on differential privacy (DP) parameters. Recent one-run auditing methods address the high cost of standard approaches by relying on a single training run with multiple "canary" points whose inclusion or exclusion must be detected by the auditor. In this work, we study the problem of efficiently crafting canaries for one-run privacy auditing. Motivated by recent theoretical insights suggesting that interference between canaries contributes to weaker leakage estimates compared to multi-run methods, we propose to optimize canaries to be both highly detectable and minimally interfering. Our approach combines a greedy initialization based on influence functions with a bilevel optimization procedure that maximizes distinguishability while promoting diversity in embedding space, enabling the use of computationally efficient bilevel algorithms. Experiments show that our method achieves stronger privacy leakage estimates at a lower computational cost than existing canary crafting approaches.

</details>


### [134] [BASIS: Batchwise Advantage Estimation from Single-Rollout Information Sharing for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27293)
*Shijin Gong,Erhan Xu,Kai Ye,Francesco Quinzan,Giulia Livieri,Chengchun Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了BASIS算法，一种无评论家（critic-free）的后训练方法，通过利用整个批次中的丰富信息来改进价值函数估计，在单次采样下实现了高效且高性能的强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于可验证奖励的强化学习算法在价值估计和策略学习中面临着计算效率与样本效率之间的权衡问题：多轮采样方法样本效率高但计算成本高，单轮采样方法计算效率高但价值估计不准确。

Method: 提出BASIS（一种无评论家的后训练算法），在每个在线训练步骤中，每个提示仅采样一次展开（rollout），但利用整个批次中跨提示的丰富信息来改进价值函数估计。

Result: 实验表明：1）与单轮基线REINFORCE++相比，BASIS将价值函数估计的MSE降低了69%；2）BASIS仅用1次展开就能达到比8次展开的组均值估计器更低的MSE；3）在显著减少训练时间的情况下，BASIS达到了接近多轮GRPO类基线的性能，并经常优于单轮REINFORCE类基线。

Conclusion: BASIS成功解决了强化学习中计算效率与样本效率的权衡问题，通过跨提示信息共享实现了高效的价值估计和策略优化，为大语言模型的后训练提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a standard recipe for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. Existing algorithms face a tradeoff between computational efficiency and sample efficiency in value estimation and policy learning. We introduce BASIS, a critic-free post-training algorithm designed to address this tradeoff. At each online training step, BASIS samples only one rollout per prompt, but leverages rich information across prompts in the entire batch to improve value function estimation. Our experiments demonstrate that BASIS reduces MSE in value function estimation by 69% compared to REINFORCE++, a representative single-rollout baseline, and achieves lower MSE with one rollout than group mean estimators with 8 rollouts. This improvement in value estimation translates to better policy optimization: using substantially less training time, BASIS achieves performance close to multi-rollout GRPO-type baselines and often outperforms single-rollout REINFORCE-type baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Normal Guidance is what Attention Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27306)
*Ethan Harvey,Dennis Johan Loevlie,Michael C. Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对弱监督3D医学图像分类，提出了一种名为“正态引导（Normal Guidance）”的正则化技术，使注意力分布遵循钟形曲线，从而显著提升了切片级定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅使用整个3D体积的二值标签进行弱监督学习的设置下，现有多实例学习（MIL）方法在切片级预测上表现不佳，甚至不如忽略图像内容的中心聚焦基线方法。这促使研究者思考如何改进注意力机制以实现更准确的切片级分类。

Method: 提出了正态引导（Normal Guidance）正则化技术，该方法鼓励学习到的注意力分布遵循钟形曲线。这一技术被应用于基于注意力和基于Transformer的MIL方法中，以改进切片级预测。

Result: 在包含超过400万张2D切片的三个医学影像数据集上进行的实验表明，应用正态引导后，基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法在切片级定位任务上显著优于现有最先进方法，同时在全扫描分类任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 正态引导是一种有效的正则化技术，能够使MIL方法在弱监督设置下实现更准确的切片级预测，克服了现有方法在切片级分类上的局限性，为3D医学图像分析提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider training classifiers for 3D medical images using only one binary label for the entire volume rather than a label for each 2D slice. In such weakly supervised settings, can we learn accurate classifiers for slice-level predictions? Attention-based multiple instance learning (MIL) can produce an attention score for every slice. Yet recent work demonstrates that a simple center-focused baseline that ignores image content can outperform attention-based and transformer-based MIL at slice-level classification of 3D brain scans. We show this baseline also outperforms existing MIL at slice-level classification of thoracic and abdominal CT scans. Motivated by this baseline, we propose Normal Guidance, a regularization technique that encourages the learned attention distribution to follow a bell-shaped curve. Across three medical imaging datasets totaling over 4 million 2D slices, we show our Normal Guidance enables attention-based and transformer-based MIL methods to deliver significantly better slice-level localization than the state-of-the-art while remaining competitive at whole-scan classification.

</details>


### [136] [Greening AI Inference with Accuracy and Latency-aware User Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27309)
*Vasilios A. Siris,Adamantia Stamou,George D. Stamoulis,Konstantinos Varsos,Ramin Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个AI推理激励机制框架，通过考虑用户对推理质量、延迟和环保意识的估值，平衡碳排放与用户体验质量之间的权衡，并通过双层订阅服务实现碳减排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI服务的广泛应用，其环境可持续性引起了广泛关注，近期研究表明AI推理是碳排放的主要来源，因此需要设计激励机制来减少AI推理过程中的碳排放。

Method: 提出一个基于用户对推理质量、延迟和环保意识估值的激励设计框架，考虑碳排放与两个体验质量参数之间的权衡；通过双层服务订阅模式，向用户提供折扣以换取减少的碳排放，允许AI提供商在高碳强度时期以较低质量和较高延迟服务部分推理请求。

Result: 该框架能够适应不同的权衡关系（取决于AI模型的大小和复杂性以及服务推理请求的资源分配），并通过折扣服务选项给予AI提供商灵活性，在高碳强度期间以较低质量和较高延迟服务一定比例的推理请求，从而实现碳减排。

Conclusion: 所提出的激励机制框架能够有效平衡AI推理的碳排放与用户体验质量之间的权衡，为AI服务提供商提供了一个实用的可持续发展解决方案，既满足了用户的环保意识需求，又降低了AI服务的碳足迹。

Abstract: The widespread use of AI services has raised concerns for its environmental sustainability, towards which recent studies have identified carbon emissions of AI inference as the major contributor. This paper introduces a framework for designing AI inference incentives based on the users' valuation for inference quality and latency, together with their environmental consciousness, while accounting for the tradeoff between carbon emissions and the two QoE parameters. Our approach can accommodate different tradeoffs, that depend on the size and complexity of the AI models and the allocation of resources to serve inference requests. The incentives can be offered through a practical two-tier service subscription that offers users a discount in exchange for reduced carbon emissions. The discounted service option gives the AI provider the flexibility to serve some percentage of inference requests at a lower quality and higher latency during periods of high carbon intensity.

</details>


### [137] [Probabilistic Smoothing with Ratio-Monotone Transforms for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27316)
*Kukyoung Jang,Taehyun Cho,Junrui Zhang,Ping Xu,Kyungjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合灵活对称单峰核与单调比率变换的通用平滑框架，用于全局优化，在保持全局最优性的同时提升了鲁棒性并降低了超参数敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率平滑方法依赖高斯核和特定变换，存在超参数敏感性强、鲁棒性有限的问题，且通常需要衰减的平滑调度。

Method: 提出了一个通用平滑框架：1）使用灵活的对称单峰核替代高斯核；2）采用单调比率变换；3）证明了在温和条件下平滑目标保持全局极大值点且平稳点会聚集在真实最优附近；4）为随机梯度上升提供了显式复杂度边界；5）证明了留一法基准能有效降低方差。

Result: 理论上证明了无需衰减调度即可保持全局最优性和平稳点聚集性，提供了随机梯度上升的显式复杂度边界。实验上，在高维基准测试和黑盒对抗攻击任务中展现出改进的鲁棒性和有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了现有方法的超参数敏感性问题，提供了更强的鲁棒性和理论保证，且无需衰减的平滑调度即可实现有效的全局优化。

Abstract: Probabilistic smoothing is a standard tool for global optimization, but existing methods rely on Gaussian kernels and specific transforms, often resulting in strong hyperparameter sensitivity and limited robustness. We propose a general smoothing framework that combines flexible symmetric unimodal kernels with monotonic ratio-based transformations. Under mild conditions, we show that the smoothed objective preserves the global maximizer and that all stationary points concentrate near the true optimum for sufficiently large amplification, without requiring a decreasing smoothing schedule. We further provide explicit complexity bounds for stochastic gradient ascent and show that a leave-one-out baseline provably reduces variance. Experiments on high-dimensional benchmarks and black-box adversarial attacks demonstrate improved robustness and competitive performance.

</details>


### [138] [From Scores to Gibbs Correctors: Accelerating Uniform-Rate Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27352)
*Yuchen Liang,Ness Shroff,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GADD方法，通过基于Gibbs后验的校正器加速离散扩散模型采样，首次在均匀速率模型中实现O(polylog(ε⁻¹))复杂度，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在文本等符号域表现优异，但均匀速率模型需要大量采样步骤。现有加速方法要么需要额外训练，要么存在慢混合问题。

Method: 提出Gibbs加速离散扩散（GADD）校正器，利用具体得分函数结构直接构建Gibbs后验似然，仅需标准得分估计无需额外训练。引入基于归纳论证的新型理论框架分析预测器-校正器方法，跟踪预测器迭代的误差传播。

Result: GADD实现O(polylog(ε⁻¹))的采样复杂度，这是均匀速率离散扩散模型扩散采样器首次达到该速率。实验表明GADD在合成数据、零样本文本采样和零样本条件音乐生成中持续改善样本质量和计算效率，优于Euler方法和CTMC校正器等基线。

Conclusion: GADD为离散扩散模型提供了高效、无需额外训练的加速方案，理论分析框架独立价值显著，为预测器-校正器方法分析提供了新方法。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have achieved strong empirical performance in text and other symbolic domains, but, especially for uniform-rate models, they often require many steps to generate a single sample. Existing acceleration methods either rely on training additional quantities or suffer from slow mixing. In this work, we propose a novel Gibbs-based corrector for discrete diffusion models, termed Gibbs-Accelerated Discrete Diffusion (GADD). GADD leverages the structure of the concrete score function to construct Gibbs posterior likelihoods directly, without requiring any additional training beyond standard score estimation. We show that GADD achieves an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$, yielding the first such rate for diffusion-based samplers for uniform-rate discrete diffusion models. We also conduct numerical experiments demonstrating the practical advantages of GADD across synthetic data, zero-shot text sampling, and zero-shot conditional music generation. These results corroborate the theory and show that GADD consistently improves sample quality and wall-clock efficiency over standard baselines, including vanilla Euler methods and CTMC correctors. Beyond this, our theoretical analysis introduces a novel framework for analyzing predictor-corrector methods in discrete diffusion models, which may be of independent interest. Unlike existing approaches that rely on the Girsanov change-of-measure technique, our method is based on an induction argument that tracks error propagation across predictor iterations while accounting for inaccuracies in the corrector updates.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Xe-Forge: Multi-Stage LLM-Powered Kernel Optimization for Intel GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26118)
*Marcin Spoczynski,Daniel Fleischer,Moshe Berchansky,Gabriela Ben-Melech Stan,Shira Guskin,Weilin Xu,Adam Siemieniuk,Alexander Heinecke*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Xe-Forge，一个面向Intel GPU的多阶段LLM驱动的Triton内核自动优化流水线，在97个内核上实现1.17x平均加速，部分达82x，消除手动移植瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将深度学习算法移植到新硬件加速器时，开发者需要对每个Triton内核重复执行量化、内存访问合并、分块大小调优等低级优化，这种手动重复工作成为主要瓶颈。虽然底层优化模式基本一致，但每个内核都需要针对不同设备的硬件约束进行反复试验和错误分析的循环。

Method: 提出Xe-Forge多阶段LLM驱动的流水线，包含多达九个优化阶段（算法重构、算子融合、块指针现代化、GPU特定调优、开放探索等）。采用Chain-of-Verification-and-Refinement (CoVeR)代理生成候选方案，在真实硬件上验证，并针对失败进行迭代。通过精心策划的知识库编码Intel GPU约束（二次幂线程束计数、GRF模式、SLM大小），这些约束不在LLM训练数据中，确保模型保持在架构有效边界内。

Result: 在Intel Arc Pro B70上对97个Level-2 KernelBench内核和Flash Attention进行评估：相对PyTorch eager实现1.17x几何平均加速，67%内核得到改进，9个内核超过5x加速（最高达82x），Flash Attention在所有测试配置下实现2-13.3x加速且无性能退化。

Conclusion: 证明了结合结构化领域知识和硬件在环验证可以系统性地消除当前阻碍算法在新加速器上部署的重复移植工作，为自动化硬件适配提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Porting deep learning algorithms to new hardware accelerators requires developers to repeatedly apply the same low-level optimizations -- quantization, memory access coalescing, tile size tuning, and architecture-specific workarounds -- to every Triton kernel in their code-base. This manual, repetitive effort is a major bottleneck: each kernel demands the same cycle of trial-and-error profiling against hardware constraints that vary across devices, yet the underlying optimization patterns remain largely consistent. We present Xe-Forge, a multi-stage LLM-powered pipeline that automates this process for Intel GPU. Given a functionally correct Triton kernel, the system applies up to nine optimization stages -- from algorithmic restructuring and operator fusion through block pointer modernization, GPU-specific tuning, and open-ended discovery -- each driven by a Chain-of-Verification-and-Refinement (CoVeR) agent that generates candidates, validates them on real hardware, and iterates on failures. A curated knowledge base encodes Intel GPU constraints (power-of-two warp counts, GRF modes, SLM sizing) that are absent from LLM training data, keeping the model within architecturally valid bounds. We evaluate Xe-Forge on 97 Level-2 KernelBench kernels and Flash Attention on the Intel Arc Pro B70, achieving a 1.17x geometric mean speedup over PyTorch eager with 67% of kernels improving, nine kernels exceeding 5x (up to 82x), and 2--13.3x speedups on Flash Attention across all tested configurations without regression -- demonstrating that structured domain knowledge with hardware-in-the-loop verification can systematically eliminate the repetitive porting effort that currently gates algorithm deployment on new accelerators.

</details>


### [140] [Edge AI Deployment Beyond Models: A BSP-Aware Systems Framework for Industrial Embedded Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26119)
*Pitchai Muthu M*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对工业边缘AI部署提出BSP感知框架，将其视为系统问题而非后期打包，通过五层架构实现可复现、可诊断和可靠的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业边缘AI项目常先开发模型后考虑平台，但在嵌入式系统中面临长产品生命周期、厂商特定内核、异构加速器、安全约束和复杂I/O路径等挑战。模型仅是更大执行链的一部分（从传感器经BSP到生产服务循环），因此需要将部署视为系统问题而非后期应用打包。

Method: 提出面向工业嵌入式平台的BSP感知框架，包含五层架构：硬件层、BSP/操作系统适配层、运行时与加速层、应用/推理层、运维/验证层。基于Android、NXP i.MX、NVIDIA Jetson、ONNX Runtime和TensorRT的厂商架构文档，以及嵌入式AI基准测试、设备不稳定性和异构边缘机群的系统文献。

Result: 构建了一个实用框架，将底层平台工作与可测量的部署结果连接起来，包括可复现性、可诊断性、持续吞吐量和现场可靠性等指标。

Conclusion: 稳健的边缘AI部署必须作为系统问题处理，通过五层BSP感知框架可以有效连接底层平台工作与实际部署成果，提升部署的可复现性、可诊断性和现场可靠性。

Abstract: Industrial Edge AI programs often begin with the model and only later confront the platform. That sequencing is attractive because it allows early demonstrations, but it breaks down when the deployment target is an embedded system with long product lifecycles, vendor-specific kernels, heterogeneous accelerators, safety constraints, and nontrivial I/O paths. In that environment, a model is only one component of a larger execution chain that begins at the sensor, traverses the board support package (BSP), and ends in a production service loop. This paper argues that robust Edge AI deployment must be treated as a systems problem rather than a late-stage application packaging exercise. The paper presents a BSP-aware framework for industrial embedded platforms organized around five layers: hardware, BSP/operating-system adaptation, runtime and acceleration, application/inference, and operations/validation. The discussion is grounded in vendor architecture documentation for Android, NXP i.MX, NVIDIA Jetson, ONNX Runtime, and TensorRT, and in systems literature on embedded AI benchmarking, device instability, and heterogeneous edge fleets. The result is a practical framework that connects low-level platform work to measurable deployment outcomes such as reproducibility, diagnosability, sustained throughput, and field reliability.

</details>


### [141] [Semantic-aware Token Selection and Resource Optimization for Communication-efficient Split Federated Fine-tuning in Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26120)
*Xianke Qiang,Zheng Chang,Geyong Min*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ST-SFLora框架，通过语义Token选择和资源联合优化解决边缘端大模型分割联邦学习的通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署大型Transformer视觉模型面临硬件限制和无线环境动态变化的挑战。虽然分割联邦学习（SFL）通过将深层卸载到边缘服务器缓解了计算负担，但在传输高维激活Token时存在严重的通信开销瓶颈。

Method: 提出语义Token分割联邦LoRA微调框架（ST-SFLora）：引入语义传输效率（STE）指标平衡语义保留与传输成本；基于STE构建联合资源优化问题，动态确定Token选择、上行带宽分配和发射功率；采用交替优化算法求解混合整数非凸问题。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，ST-SFLora在所有基线方法中实现了最低的客户端资源消耗，同时在通信效率和模型性能之间取得了良好的权衡。

Conclusion: ST-SFLora框架成功解决了边缘端大模型分割联邦学习的通信瓶颈问题，为资源受限移动设备上的大模型部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying large Transformer-based vision models on resource-limited mobile devices at network edge is severely constrained by hardware limitations and dynamic wireless environments. While federated learning (FL) enables collaborative training without sharing raw data, strictly local fine-tuning of such massive models remains computationally prohibitive for edge devices. Split federated learning (SFL) alleviates this burden by offloading deep layers to the edge server, yet it suffers from heavy communication overhead when transmitting high-dimensional activation tokens. To address this bottleneck, we propose ST-SFLora, a semantic token-based split federated LoRA fine-tuning framework. We introduce a new metric, \emph{Semantic Transmission Efficiency} (STE), to balance semantic retention and transmission cost. Based on STE, we formulate a joint resource optimization problem that dynamically determines token selection, uplink bandwidth allocation, and transmit power under latency and energy constraints. The resulting mixed-integer nonconvex problem is efficiently solved via an alternating algorithm. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that ST-SFLora achieves the lowest client-side resource consumption among baselines while delivering a favorable trade-off between communication efficiency and model performance.

</details>


### [142] [Revisiting Bruck: Phase-Efficient All-to-All Communication in Reconfigurable Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26930)
*Anton Juerss,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTri，一种针对光可重构网络的双向All-to-All调度算法，通过平衡三进制块传播在⌈log₃n⌉个阶段完成通信，相比静态All-to-All最高提升10倍性能，相比可重构Bruck提升2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: All-to-All通信是分布式机器学习和高性能计算工作负载的关键性能瓶颈，密集流量日益给纵向扩展互连带来压力。虽然光可重构网络(ORNs)通过适应物理拓扑到活动工作负载来改善通信成本和带宽利用率，但其优势关键取决于集合操作是否包含可由稀疏且可重用的拓扑状态服务的结构化阶段。

Method: 本文重新审视Bruck的All-to-All实现，演示了通信模式和重配置策略协同设计的拓扑优化优势。提出ReTri，一种用于ORN的双向All-to-All调度，采用平衡三进制块传播在⌈log₃n⌉个阶段完成All-to-All。ReTri的成对双向交换产生的重配置策略使重配置延迟能够在多个阶段间分摊。

Result: 初步仿真显示，即使在毫秒级的重配置延迟下，ReTri将完成时间相比静态All-to-All提升最高10倍，相比可重构Bruck提升最高2.1倍。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计通信模式和重配置策略，ReTri证明了拓扑优化在光可重构网络上的显著优势，为缓解All-to-All通信瓶颈提供了有效解决方案，即使在较大的重配置延迟下仍能保持显著的性能提升。

Abstract: All-to-All communication is a key performance bottleneck for distributed machine learning (ML) and high-performance computing (HPC) workloads, where dense traffic increasingly stresses scale-up interconnects. While these ML and HPC workloads have driven unprecedented infrastructure demand, optical reconfigurable networks (ORNs) offer a promising path forward. By adapting the physical topology to the active workload, they improve communication cost and bandwidth utilization. However, their benefit is critically contingent on whether the collective consists of structured phases that can be served by sparse and reusable topology states.
  In this paper, we revisit Bruck's All-to-All implementation and demonstrate the benefits of topology optimization in which both communication pattern and reconfiguration strategy are co-designed. We present ReTri, a bidirectional All-to-All schedule for ORNs. ReTri uses balanced ternary block propagation to complete All-to-All in $\lceil \log_3 n\rceil$ phases. The induced reconfiguration strategy from ReTri's pairwise bidirectional exchanges allow reconfiguration delays to be amortized across multiple phases. Preliminary simulations show that ReTri improves completion time by up to $10\times$ over static All-to-All, even for millisecond-scale reconfiguration delays, and improving reconfigurable Bruck by up to $2.1\times$.

</details>


### [143] [Agentic AI Workload Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26297)
*Yichao Yuan,Ankita Nayak,Souvik Kundu,Nishil Talati*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次对智能体（Agentic AI）工作负载进行了端到端特征分析，揭示了其并非简单的长提示任务，而是具有独特的解码主导特性、上下文重用模式以及工具使用的时序结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI的兴起，大语言模型服务正从孤立的提示-生成请求转向有状态的、多轮执行模式。这种转变涉及重复调用模型、执行工具以及上下文随时间增长，因此迫切需要从LLM服务和工具执行两个角度深入理解此类工作负载的特性，以指导高效系统设计。

Method: 研究人员建立了一套端到端追踪基础设施，对ReAct风格的智能体进行了系统性分析。实验覆盖了推理型和非推理型的Gemma与Qwen模型配置，并在五个智能体基准测试上收集数据，从模型服务层面和工具执行层面进行了细粒度剖析。

Result: 研究发现：1）智能体工作负载并非单纯的长提示任务，在有效上下文缓存支持下，多数输入token在轮次间被复用，使执行过程变为解码主导型，并增强了对持久化KV缓存状态的依赖；2）工具使用呈现清晰的时序结构，智能体在执行早期倾向于读取/探索行为，后期则转向执行/写入行为。

Conclusion: 高效的智能体服务系统必须协同管理三大要素：重复的模型重入、持久的上下文状态以及随工作负载变化的工具行为，这为未来智能体服务系统的优化与设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Agentic AI shifts LLM serving from isolated prompt-generation requests to stateful, multi-turn executions that repeatedly invoke the model, call tools, and grow context over time. This paper characterizes ReAct-style agents from both the LLM-serving and tool-execution perspectives using an end-to-end tracing infrastructure across reasoning and non-reasoning Gemma and Qwen configurations on five agentic benchmarks. Our study shows that agentic workloads are not simply long-prompt workloads: with effective context caching, most input tokens are reused across turns, making execution decode-dominated while increasing dependence on long-lived KV-cache state. We also find that tool use has a clear temporal structure, with agents shifting from read/explore behavior early in execution to execute/write behavior later. These results show that efficient agentic serving must jointly manage repeated model re-entry, persistent context state, and workload-dependent tool behavior.

</details>


### [144] [Autonomic Federated-Market Orchestration for the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27106)
*Lauri Lovén,Roberto Morabito,Abhishek Kumar,Susanna Pirttikangas,Jukka Riekki,Sasu Tarkoma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Neural Pub/Sub，一种基于市场定价信号的去中心化联合代理自治系统，在边缘-云计算连续体中实现跨自治域的自主管理，在性能上优于或匹配集中式方案，并能承受代理故障和网络分区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘-云计算连续体需要跨自治管理域扩展的自管理机制，同时需遵守租户和运营方指定的数据主权要求，传统集中式控制难以满足这些需求。

Method: 采用MAPE-K控制闭环，包括：每代理健康与负载监测、边际成本清算价格分析、多拟阵可行域上的放置规划、联合跨域调度以及有界陈旧价格信号的对等订阅摘要；基于Walrasian收敛命题，在树形和串并联服务依赖DAG上实现去中心化价格分配。

Result: 在4虚拟机、4域、48工作节点的测试床上，联合市场方案在相同进程数下优于单进程预言机2-4%（45/45获胜，p~2.8e-14），与四分片集中式编排器差距在±1.5%内；轮询完成率在高负载下急剧下降（98.8%→3.3%），市场方案保持稳定；系统在代理死亡和网络分区下完成率≥98.7%，主权强制无运行时开销。

Conclusion: Neural Pub/Sub成功证明了基于市场定价信号的去中心化方法能够有效管理跨域边缘-云系统，在性能、容错性和数据主权方面均表现优异，但异构域压力测试和跨站点WAN部署仍需未来工作。

Abstract: The edge-cloud computing continuum demands self-management mechanisms that scale across autonomous administrative domains while honouring tenant- and operator-specified data sovereignty. We present Neural Pub/Sub, a federated-broker autonomic substrate whose self-organising behaviour emerges from market-based price signals rather than centralised control. Its MAPE-K control loop closes over per-broker health and load monitoring, marginal-cost clearing-price analysis, placement planning over a polymatroidal feasibility region, federated cross-domain dispatch, and shared peer subscription summaries with bounded-staleness price signals. The Plan step is anchored in a Walrasian convergence proposition: under gross-substitutes valuations on tree and series-parallel service-dependency DAGs, decentralised price-based allocation matches the welfare of a centralised oracle. We evaluate the substrate on a 4-VM, 4-domain, 48-worker federated edge-cloud testbed (single data centre, 50 ms emulated WAN) in a 1005-run campaign augmented by a fair-process-count sharded-oracle comparator. The federated market dominates a single-process oracle by 2-4% with 45 of 45 per-seed wins (sign-test p ~ 2.8e-14, Hodges-Lehmann median -39.6 ms); against a four-shard centralised orchestrator at equal process count the gap stays within +/-1.5% across all nine (pipeline, load) cells. Round-robin completion rate collapses 98.8% -> 22.4% -> 3.3% across arrival rates 5/10/15 pps while the market preserves completion; the advantage decomposes into three Walrasian properties (information completeness, admission control, price discovery). Federation withstands broker death and network partition (completion rate >= 98.7% across 75 cells), and sovereignty enforcement adds no measurable runtime overhead across 60 governance-grid runs. Heterogeneous-domain stressors and cross-site WAN deployment remain future work.

</details>


### [145] [Totoro$^+$: An Adaptive and Scalable Edge Federated Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26323)
*Cheng-Wei Ching,Xin Chen,Taehwan Kim,Jian-Jhih Kuo,Dilma Da Silva,Liting Hu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Totoro$^+$，一个基于DHT的对等网络联邦学习系统，通过去中心化架构将参数服务器分配给每个应用，实现了大规模联邦学习应用的同时运行，在500个EC2服务器上的实验显示训练速度提升1.2-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习系统采用集中式架构，多个FL应用共享一个中央参数服务器，导致系统可扩展性受限，无法支持大规模联邦学习应用同时运行。为解决这一问题，需要设计一个完全去中心化的联邦学习系统架构。

Method: 提出Totoro$^+$系统，采用基于DHT的P2P模型将集中式FL系统重构为完全去中心化架构。核心技术包括：(1)局部感知的P2P多环结构；(2)基于发布/订阅的森林抽象；(3)具有ε-近似纳什均衡保证的博弈论路径规划模型。每个FL应用都有专属参数服务器，边缘节点可灵活担任协调者、聚合器、客户端选择器或工作者等角色。

Result: 在500个Amazon EC2服务器上的真实环境实验表明：Totoro$^+$随FL应用数量和节点数量N扩展性良好，训练总时间加速1.2×-14.0×，在百万级节点规模下实现O(log N)跳数的模型分发和梯度聚合，并能有效适应实际边缘网络环境和节点变动。

Conclusion: Totoro$^+$成功实现了大规模联邦学习系统的去中心化架构，通过创新的P2P多环结构、发布/订阅抽象和博弈论路径规划，显著提升了系统的可扩展性、适应性和训练效率，为边缘网络上的大规模联邦学习应用提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is an emerging distributed machine learning (ML) technique that enables in-situ model training and inference on decentralized edge devices. We propose Totoro$^+$, a novel scalable FL system that enables massive FL applications to run simultaneously on edge networks. The key insight is to explore a distributed hash table (DHT)-based peer-to-peer (P2P) model to re-architect the centralized FL system design into a fully decentralized one. In contrast to previous studies where many FL applications shared one centralized parameter server, Totoro$^+$ assigns a dedicated parameter server to each application. Any edge node can act as any application's coordinator, aggregator, client selector, worker (participant device), or any combination of the above, thereby radically improving scalability and adaptivity.
