Totoro$^+$ introduces three innovations to realize its design: a locality-aware P2P multi-ring structure, a publish/subscribe-based forest abstraction, and a game-theoretic path planning model with a guarantee of an $ε$-approximate Nash equilibrium. Real-world experiments on 500 Amazon EC2 servers show that Totoro$^+$ scales gracefully with the number of FL applications and $N$ edge nodes speeds up the total training time by $1.2\times-14.0\times$, achieves $\mathcal{O}(\log N)$ hops for model dissemination and gradient aggregation with millions of nodes, and efficiently adapts to the practical edge networks and churns.

</details>


### [146] [GridPilot: Real-Time Grid-Responsive Control for AI Supercomputers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26384)
*Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究开发了GridPilot三层预测控制器，在真实GPU硬件上实现97.2毫秒的端到端响应时间，比北欧快速频率储备要求快6.9倍，证明了MW级AI/HPC设施可作为可控的电网响应灵活性资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数据中心电力需求增长速度超过电网供应能力，而系统运营商越来越需要能够在数秒内调整功率的大型灵活负载来消纳波动性风电和光伏发电。对于多兆瓦级AI/HPC设施，关键的实际问题是：软件堆栈能以多快速度将电网请求转化为设施电表处GPU功率的实际变化。

Method: 开发了GridPilot三层预测控制器，在毫秒、秒和小时时间尺度上运行，并辅以确定性安全岛旁路实现快速响应。在三块NVIDIA V100 GPU测试平台上进行实测，并引入瞬时PUE校正机制，使调度承诺在电表层面而非仅在IT负载层面保持稳健。

Result: 在真实硬件上实现了97.2毫秒的端到端触发到目标响应时间，比北欧快速频率储备700毫秒要求快6.9倍。在六个代表性欧洲电网（从瑞典到波兰）的重放实验中，PUE感知控制器弥补了2.5-5.8个百分点的冷却开销拖累。

Conclusion: GridPilot作为开源发布，证明了MW级AI/HPC需求可以通过设计被工程化为可控的电网响应灵活性资源，为数据中心参与电网调节提供了技术可行性验证。

Abstract: At global scale, data-center electricity demand is growing faster than the grids that supply it, while system operators increasingly require large flexible loads that can adjust power within seconds to absorb variable wind and solar generation. For multi-megawatt AI/HPC facilities, the key unresolved question is practical and measurable: how quickly can the software stack translate a grid request into a real change in GPU power at the facility meter, where commitments are settled? We answer this on real hardware with GridPilot, a three-tier predictive controller operating across milliseconds, seconds, and hours, augmented by a deterministic safety-island bypass for fast response. On a three-GPU NVIDIA V100 testbed, GridPilot achieves a measured end-to-end trigger-to-target response of 97.2 ms, which is 6.9x faster than the 700 ms requirement of Nordic Fast Frequency Reserve. We further incorporate an instantaneous Power Usage Effectiveness (PUE) correction so dispatched commitments remain robust at meter level rather than only at IT load level. In replay experiments across six representative European grids (from Sweden to Poland), the PUE-aware controller closes 2.5-5.8 percentage points of cooling-overhead drag. GridPilot is released as open source and serves as a proof of concept that MW-scale AI/HPC demand can be engineered as controllable, grid-responsive flexibility by design.

</details>


### [147] [Configuration-Driven Dynamic API Routing for Resilient Service Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26404)
*Nataraj Agaram Sundar,Tejas Morabia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配置驱动的动态API路由架构，用于增强第三方服务集成的韧性，通过因子列表、实时遥测和闭环决策引擎实现运行时供应商选择，无需应用重新部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代在线服务依赖第三方API进行认证、支付、通信等关键功能，这些依赖超出应用所有者的直接运营控制，可能出现区域中断、限流、延迟峰值、配额耗尽或行为变更等问题，导致用户可见的故障，因此需要一种机制来管理这些外部依赖的韧性和可靠性。

Method: 提出配置驱动的动态API路由架构，核心组件包括：可插拔的因子列表（定义操作特定的硬门控和加权评分函数）、实时遥测、熔断器、舱壁隔离和闭环决策引擎。路由算法基于实时指标、区域策略约束、配额状态、延迟、成本和事件信号评估候选提供商。路由策略与应用代码分离，支持运行时调整供应商选择。

Result: 形式化了因子列表模型，描述了请求时路由算法和计算滑动窗口提供商健康指标的事件管道，分析了降级提供商场景下的故障转移行为。通过匿名化SMS验证案例研究，验证了将手动供应商切换替换为基于完成率遥测的自动路由的可行性和效果。

Conclusion: 配置驱动的动态API路由架构成功实现了第三方服务集成的韧性管理，允许运维人员在运行时调整供应商选择策略而无需重新部署应用，通过自动化路由决策有效降低了外部依赖故障对用户的影响。

Abstract: Modern online services rely on third-party APIs for authentication, payments, communication, identity verification, fraud detection, observability, and fulfillment. These dependencies are outside the direct operational control of the application owner and may experience regional outages, throttling, latency spikes, quota exhaustion, or behavior changes that surface as user-visible failures. This paper presents configuration-driven dynamic API routing, an architecture for resilient third-party service integration based on pluggable factor lists, real-time telemetry, circuit breakers, bulkhead isolation, and a closed-loop decision engine. A factor list defines operation-specific hard gates and weighted scoring functions that evaluate candidate providers using live metrics, regional policy constraints, quota state, latency, cost, and incident signals. The router separates routing policy from application code, allowing operators to adapt vendor selection at runtime without redeploying applications. We formalize the factor-list model, describe a request-time routing algorithm, present the event pipeline that computes sliding-window provider health metrics, and analyze failover behavior under degraded-provider scenarios. We also describe an anonymized SMS verification case study in which manual vendor switching was replaced by automated routing driven by completion-rate telemetry.

</details>


### [148] [Characterization-Guided GPU Fault Resilience in NVIDIA MPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26461)
*Rixin Liu,Xingqi Cui,Kaijian Wang,Xinheng Ding,Zirui Liu,Yuke Wang,Jiarong Xing*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了容错MPS设计，通过故障隔离和快速恢复两种机制解决了NVIDIA MPS在多进程GPU共享中因单进程故障导致所有进程终止的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA MPS虽然通过允许多进程并发执行实现细粒度GPU共享，但其容错能力薄弱：单个进程的故障会终止所有并行运行的进程，严重限制了其在多租户GPU集群等对容错性要求较高的场景中的应用。

Method: 基于对GPU故障的系统化特征分析及其端到端处理流水线的深入研究，设计了两种互补机制：(1) 针对占主导地位的内存相关故障，通过开源GPU驱动内核模块中的软件干预实现完全隔离；(2) 针对处理流程位于专有软件中的其他故障，设计了基于虚拟内存的GPU驻留状态共享快速恢复机制。

Result: 在不同GPU和工作负载上的评估表明，所提出的两种机制能够有效处理相应类型的故障，且开销极小。

Conclusion: 该工作成功设计并实现了容错MPS，有效解决了多进程GPU共享中的故障传播问题，为MPS在容错敏感场景中的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: NVIDIA Multi-Process Service (MPS) enables fine-grained GPU sharing by allowing multiple processes to execute concurrently on the same GPU, making it an important mechanism for improving GPU utilization. However, MPS has weak fault resilience: a fault in one process can terminate all co-running processes, limiting its adoption in resilience-critical settings such as multi-tenant GPU clusters. In this work, we design fault-resilient MPS to solve this problem. Our design is guided by insights from a systematic characterization of GPU faults and a deep analysis of their end-to-end processing pipeline. Based on these insights, we design two complementary mechanisms. A fault isolation mechanism for the dominant memory-related faults that can be fully isolated by software intervention in the open GPU driver kernel module. For other faults whose process is within proprietary software, we design a practical mechanism -- fast recovery using virtual memory based GPU-resident state sharing. Our evaluation on different GPUs and workloads shows that these mechanisms can handle corresponding faults effectively with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [149] [StreamSplit: Continuous Audio Representation Learning via Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26523)
*Minh K. Quan,Pubudu N. Pathirana*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出StreamSplit框架，通过分布流式处理和不确定性引导的自适应分割策略，解决了边缘设备上大批次对比学习的资源约束问题，在保持精度的同时显著降低了延迟、带宽和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表示学习依赖的大批次对比学习(CL)与边缘设备的资源约束存在根本冲突：小批次降低模型保真度，而卸载到云端会带来不可接受的延迟和带宽成本。现有静态模型压缩方案无法适应边缘环境的动态资源波动，亟需一种能够适应运行时资源波动的新型解决方案。

Method: 提出StreamSplit框架，包含两个核心创新：(1)基于分布的流式框架，使用可处理的混合损失函数将表示质量与本地批次大小解耦，在稀疏更新的情况下保持保真度；(2)不确定性引导的自适应分割器，采用轻量级强化学习策略动态划分计算，集成实时资源监控与嵌入模糊性来即时优化精度-延迟权衡。

Result: 在从Raspberry Pi 4到Apple M2的多种硬件平台上进行评估，StreamSplit相比以服务器为中心的基线方法：每样本延迟降低高达4.7倍，带宽减少77.1%，能耗降低52.3%，同时保持精度在服务器中心模型的2.2%以内。

Conclusion: 证明了自适应分布式学习是现代边缘生态系统的可行路径，StreamSplit成功弥合了连续环境音频与CLAP、COLA等模型的离散批次需求之间的鸿沟，使流式对比学习在异构ARM客户端平台上变得实用可行。

Abstract: Large-batch Contrastive Learning (CL), the foundation of modern representation learning, is fundamentally incompatible with the volatile resource constraints of edge devices. This conflict creates a dilemma: small on-device batches degrade model fidelity, while offloading to the cloud incurs unacceptable latency and bandwidth costs. Existing solutions often resort to static model compression, which fails to adapt to the runtime volatility of edge environments. To bridge this gap, we present StreamSplit, a novel framework that makes streaming CL practical across heterogeneous ARM client platforms. StreamSplit resolves the conflict between the continuous nature of ambient audio and the discrete batch requirements of models like CLAP and COLA. We introduce: (1) A distribution-based streaming framework that decouples representation quality from local batch size, using a tractable Hybrid Loss to maintain fidelity despite sparse updates; and (2) An Uncertainty-Guided Adaptive Splitter that uses a lightweight Reinforcement Learning (RL) policy to dynamically partition computation. Uniquely, this policy integrates real-time resource monitoring with embedding ambiguity to optimize the accuracy-latency trade-off on the fly. We evaluate StreamSplit on diverse hardware, from the resource-constrained Raspberry Pi 4 to the high-performance Apple M2. Results demonstrate that StreamSplit reduces per-sample latency by up to 4.7x and cuts bandwidth by 77.1% and energy by 52.3% compared to server-centric baselines. Crucially, it maintains accuracy within 2.2% of server-centric models, proving that adaptive, distributed learning is a viable path for the modern edge ecosystem.

</details>


### [150] [A Formal Semantics of C with OpenMP Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26527)
*Ke Du,Anshu Sharma,Liyi Li,William Mansky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为带有OpenMP指令的C代码建立了形式化语义，基于CompCert验证编译器，能够捕获OpenMP指令与变量状态之间的微妙交互，并确保成功执行的程序无数据竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenMP是一种流行的并行化框架，用户可以通过简单的注释将顺序代码转换为并行代码。然而，在原本正确的程序中添加OpenMP pragma很容易无意中引入错误，包括逻辑错误和竞争条件。

Method: 基于CompCert验证编译器的C语义及其并发扩展，构建了一个带有OpenMP指令的C代码的形式化语义。

Result: 该语义捕获了先前OpenMP语义所忽略的OpenMP指令与变量状态之间的微妙交互，并为检测由错误注释引起的不良行为提供了基础：特别是，任何成功的执行都保证没有数据竞争。

Conclusion: 该形式化语义为检测和防止OpenMP并行化过程中引入的数据竞争等问题提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: OpenMP is a popular parallelization framework that lets users transform sequential code into parallel code with a few simple annotations. Unfortunately, it is also easy to inadvertently introduce errors by adding OpenMP pragmas into otherwise correct programs, including both logic errors and race conditions. We present a formal semantics for C code with OpenMP directives, building on the C semantics of the CompCert verified compiler and its extension to concurrency. Our semantics captures subtle interactions between OpenMP directives and variable state that have been obscured by previous OpenMP semantics, and provides a basis for detecting undesired behaviors introduced by incorrect annotations: in particular, any successful execution is guaranteed to be free of data races.

</details>


### [151] [Reducing Internal State in Eigenvalue-Only Divide-and-Conquer Tridiagonal Eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26599)
*Ruiyi Zhan,Shaoshuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于对称特征值计算的边界行分治算法，通过仅传递边界行/列信息，将内存需求从二次降低到线性，避免了传统分治算法中大量矩阵变换的存储和重放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分治算法在计算对称特征值时，在合并（conquer）阶段需要存储或重放大型变换矩阵，导致内存需求高且存在不必要的矩阵-向量计算，使得QR方法在实际中被更多采用。作者希望利用分治算法的天然并行性优势，同时解决其内存效率问题。

Method: 提出边界行分治算法：关键发现是合并阶段只需要选定的边界行/列，而非完整的累积特征向量矩阵。通过递归过程中直接传播这些边界行，避免材料化完整的变换矩阵，从而减少内存占用和计算开销。论文提供了算法描述、时空复杂度分析、正确性和稳定性论证，以及优化的CPU和GPU实现。

Result: 算法将内存需求从二次空间降低到线性空间；消除了传统惰性重放公式中不必要的矩阵-向量计算；提供了优化的CPU和GPU实现；与标准数值库中的QR和分治例程进行了性能对比评估。

Conclusion: 边界行分治算法成功解决了传统分治算法在特征值计算中的内存瓶颈问题，在保持算法并行优势的同时实现了线性空间复杂度，为对称特征值计算提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Divide and Conquer (D&C) is a widely used algorithmic strategy for symmetric eigenvalue decomposition. Its natural parallelism makes D&C attractive on modern multicore CPUs and GPUs, but existing eigenvalue-only routines often default to QR-based methods because conventional D&C still materializes or replays large transformation matrices during the conquer phase. This paper proposes a boundary-row D&C algorithm for eigenvalue-only computation. The key observation is that the conquer phase only needs selected boundary rows/columns rather than the full accumulated eigenvector matrix. By propagating these boundary rows directly through the recursion, the proposed algorithm reduces the memory requirement from quadratic to linear space while also eliminating unnecessary matrix-vector work in the conventional lazy-replay formulation. We provide the algorithm, its time and space complexity analysis, correctness and stability arguments, optimized CPU and GPU implementations, and an evaluation against QR and D&C routines in standard numerical libraries.

</details>


### [152] [Nonlinear spectral clustering with C++ GraphBLAS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26975)
*Dimosthenis Pasadakis,Olaf Schenk,Verner Vlacic,Albert-Jan Yzelman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于$p$-范数（$p\in(1,2]$）的多路谱聚类算法的高效C++ GraphBLAS实现，在保证聚类质量的同时显著提升了计算性能和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性谱聚类方法虽然具有更好的数值特性和坚实的数学基础，但估计多个非线性特征向量会带来更高的计算成本，因此需要一种高效的实现方法来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 使用新型C++ GraphBLAS API实现直接多路$p$-范数谱聚类算法；将关键操作以线性代数形式表达，在相关代数参数化下对稀疏矩阵和稠密向量进行操作。

Result: 算法在共享内存环境下展现出良好的强扩展性，能够处理高达800万节点和4800万边的大规模数据集；在平衡图割指标上获得了高质量的聚类结果，与其他竞争方法相比表现出色。

Conclusion: 所提出的实现方法成功地在保持聚类质量的同时降低了非线性谱聚类的计算开销，证明了该方法在处理大规模图数据方面的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Nonlinear reformulations of the spectral clustering method have gained a lot of recent attention due to their increased numerical benefits and their solid mathematical background. However, the estimation of the multiple nonlinear eigenvectors is associated with an increased computational cost. We present an implementation of a direct multiway spectral clustering algorithm in the $p$-norm, for $p\in(1,2]$, using a novel C++ GraphBLAS API. The key operations are expressed in linear algebraic terms and are executed over the resulting sparse matrices and dense vectors, parameterized in the algebra pertinent to the computation. We demonstrate the effectiveness and accuracy of our shared-memory algorithm on several artificial test cases. Our numerical examples and comparative results against competitive methods indicate that the proposed implementation attains high quality clusters in terms of the balanced graph cut metric. The strong scaling capabilities of our algorithm are showcased on a range of datasets with up to $8$ million nodes and $48$ million edges.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [ATOM: Instantiating Budget-Controllable Multi-Agent Collaboration via Nucleus-Electron Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26178)
*Xinkui Zhao,Sai Liu,Yifan Zhang,Qingyu Ma,Zewen Lin,Naibo Wang,Guanjie Cheng,Chang Liu,Yueshen Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了ATOM框架，通过原子结构启发的核-电子层级设计，实现了预算可控的LLM多智能体协作图生成，在六个基准上达到SOTA性能的同时提升了30%的token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的多智能体系统在协作拓扑优化上面临稳定性与可扩展性的内在权衡问题，且常常无法将计算预算与查询难度相匹配，导致资源分配不合理。

Method: 提出ATOM自适应框架，采用受原子结构启发的核-电子层级设计：(1)维护一个稳定的、离线学习的协作骨干（原子核）；(2)在推理时动态激活查询条件化的智能体（电子）；(3)引入复杂度感知的预算策略，通过估计查询难度来严格控制电子实例化，实现资源消耗与任务需求的匹配。

Result: 在六个多样化基准上的广泛实验表明，ATOM达到了最先进的性能，同时与强基线相比，token效率提升了高达30%。

Conclusion: ATOM成功解决了多智能体协作中稳定性与可扩展性的权衡问题，通过复杂度感知的预算策略实现了计算资源与任务难度的动态匹配，为构建高效的LLM多智能体系统提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems rely on optimized collaboration topologies to balance performance and communication costs. However, current methods struggle with the inherent stability-extensibility trade-off and often misalign computational budgets with query difficulty. We propose \textsc{ATOM}, an adaptive framework that generates budget-controllable collaboration graphs via a novel task-driven reinforcement learning paradigm. Inspired by atomic structures, \textsc{ATOM} employs a nucleus-electron hierarchy: it maintains a stable, offline-learned collaboration backbone (the nucleus) while dynamically activating query-conditioned agents (electrons) during inference. Crucially, a complexity-aware budgeting strategy aligns resource consumption with task demands by estimating query difficulty to strictly regulate electron instantiation. Extensive experiments across six diverse benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{ATOM} achieves state-of-the-art performance while improving token efficiency by up to $30\%$ compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [154] [AgentSociety: Incentivizing Agentic Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26203)
*Aditya Vema Reddy Kesari,Krishna Reddy Kesari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了AgentSociety，一个基于流动民主和社会选择理论信息扩散的去中心化智能体协作机制，实现了自利智能体在激励相容下的自主决策与协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的智能体需要处理开放式用户请求，不仅需直接解决问题，还需有效利用智能体间通信渠道和反馈信号，这需要构建一个能让智能体自主运行、战略性通信、协作行为并受经济激励驱动的多智能体环境，类似人类社会的运作方式。

Method: 提出基于流动民主和社会选择理论信息扩散的去中心化智能体协作机制AgentSociety。智能体利用局部上下文自主决策以最大化效用，通过激励协作实现集体成果。机制证明向更有能力的邻居智能体委托是激励相容的，并通过共识自然生成多智能体路由路径。

Result: 证明了委托机制的激励相容性和多智能体路由路径的共识生成；论证了智能体选择性披露信息的激励；刻画了纳什均衡，表明智能体收益反映其边际贡献；在真实数据集上评估了自利异构智能体基于共识路由的协作性能。

Conclusion: AgentSociety成功构建了多智能体协作环境，在激励相容框架下实现了自利智能体的有效协作，为部署智能体处理开放性请求提供了可行的去中心化解决方案。

Abstract: The success of deployed agents relies on their ability to handle open-ended user requests using their inherent capabilities, not only in solving requests directly but also in effectively leveraging inter-agent communication channels and feedback signals over time. This requires a multi-agent environment where agents can operate autonomously, strategically communicate, behave collaboratively and be driven by economic incentives, much like humans in society. Towards this vision, we propose $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$, a mechanism that enables decentralized agentic collaboration grounded in liquid democracy and information diffusion from social choice theory. We show that $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ provides an environment for agents to make autonomous decisions utilizing their local context to maximize their utility while achieving collective outcomes through incentivized collaboration. Specifically, we prove that delegation to more competent neighbor agents is incentive compatible and naturally generates multi-agent routing path by consensus. Additionally, our mechanism incentivizes agents to selectively disclose information to their neighbor agents when doing so aligns with their self-interest, so as to garner influence. We characterize the Nash equilibrium showing that agent payoffs are reflective of their marginal contributions. We compare and benchmark strategy profiles adopted by open and proprietary state-of-the-art language models deployed in $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ against best response. Finally, we evaluate collaborative performance from consensus-based routing among self-interested heterogeneous agents in $\mathtt{AgentSociety}$ on real-world datasets.

</details>


### [155] [Decoupled Delay Compensation: Enhancing Pre-trained MARL Policies via Learned Dynamics Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26286)
*Maxim Mednikov,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化的执行阶段状态估计层，通过结合学习的门控转移模型和卡尔曼滤波，用当前信念状态估计替换延迟的通信观测，从而提升多智能体强化学习系统在陈旧观测、通信延迟和丢包等非理想条件下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多智能体强化学习系统常面临陈旧观测、随机通信延迟和间歇性丢包问题，而在理想同步条件下训练的策略在这些情况下因基于过时反馈行动而表现显著下降。

Method: 提出一个模块化的执行阶段状态估计层，将学习的门控转移模型与递归卡尔曼滤波层集成，从异步测量中估计瞬时状态，用当前信念状态估计替换延迟的通信观测。该估计器作为预训练策略的插件，无需修改原始MARL训练算法、架构或奖励结构。

Result: 在多样化的多智能体和连续控制基准测试中，该估计层一致提升了对通信延迟和消息丢失的鲁棒性，在协调密集型和动态不稳定任务中观察到最显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该模块化状态估计层能有效缓解通信延迟和丢包对MARL系统的影响，特别适用于对时间一致性要求高的协调密集型和动态不稳定任务。

Abstract: Real-world multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems must often operate under stale observations, stochastic communication delays, and intermittent packet loss. Policies trained under idealized synchronous conditions frequently exhibit significant performance degradation in these regimes because they act on outdated feedback. We propose a modular execution-stage state-estimation layer that replaces delayed communicated observations with current belief-state estimates. The framework integrates a learned Gated transition model with a recursive Kalman filtering layer to estimate instantaneous states from asynchronous measurements. A primary advantage of this approach is its modularity, The estimator serves as a plug-in for pre-trained policies, requiring no modifications to the original MARL training algorithm, architecture, or reward structure. Evaluation across diverse multi-agent and continuous-control benchmarks demonstrates that the proposed layer consistently enhances robustness to communication latency and message loss. The most significant performance gains are observed in coordination-intensive and dynamically unstable tasks where temporal consistency is critical for control.

</details>


### [156] [Constitutional Arms Races in the Public Goods Game: Co-Evolving LLM Constitutions Under Cooperation-Defection Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26448)
*Ujwal Kumar,Arth Singh,Hershraj Niranjani,Machiko Hirota,Takehiro Takayanagi,Alice Saito,Eiji Kamioka,Phan Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在对抗性环境下的宪法协同进化，发现在公共物品博弈中合作方与搭便车方能达到稳定均衡，但只有在耦合适应度和充足评估预算的条件下，对抗性协同进化才可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM智能体在目标冲突下会出现勒索、破坏和文档泄露等行为，暴露了基于单智能体或合作假设的对齐方法存在局限性。虽然已有研究表明LLM引导的进化搜索能发现有效的合作宪法，但对抗性环境下的两个关键性质尚未明确：适应度函数是否真正产生对抗压力，以及LLM变异算子在对抗性专家目标下是否可靠。

Method: 采用对抗性宪法协同进化方法，设置蓝色合作方与红色搭便车方进行30代进化博弈。实验环境包括公共物品博弈（PGG）和空间网格世界。研究团队测试了不同乘数m（1.2、1.5、2.0、3.0）下的表现，比较了独立评分环境与分数优势适应度目标（S_own - S_opp）的效果，并调整评估种子数量K（K=2和K=5）来控制模式回归。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）在PGG中，双方收敛到近均势均衡，得分S约为0.78，在测试的所有乘数m下均保持稳定；（2）在独立评分环境中，各派得分结果统计上不相关（corr(S_B, S_R) = +0.088），未产生对抗压力，而分数优势适应度目标恢复了对抗压力；（3）在纯对抗适应度下，评估种子数K控制模式回归：K=2时发生回归，K=5时在全部30代中维持强专家模式。

Conclusion: 自然语言宪法的对抗性协同进化在耦合适应度和充足评估预算的条件下是可行的。进化产生的红色宪法可作为可解释的红队测试工具，用于测试未来的合作设计。这为构建更鲁棒的LLM对齐方法提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Frontier LLM agents engage in blackmail, sabotage, and document leaks under goal conflicts in agentic settings, exposing limitations of alignment methods built around single-agent or cooperative assumptions. Recent work shows LLM-guided evolutionary search can discover effective cooperative constitutions, but two properties of the adversarial setting remain uncharacterized: whether the fitness function actually induces adversarial pressure, and whether the LLM mutation operator behaves reliably under adversarial-specialist objectives. We study adversarial constitutional co-evolution (Blue cooperators vs. Red free-riders, 30 generations) across a Public Goods Game (PGG) and a spatial grid-world. Three findings: (1) in the PGG, both factions converge to a near-parity equilibrium at S approximately 0.78, robust across tested multipliers m in {1.2, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0}; (2) in independently scored environments, per-faction scoring leaves outcomes statistically uncoupled, with corr(S_B, S_R) = +0.088, and produces no adversarial pressure; a score-advantage fitness target S_own - S_opp restores it; (3) under pure-adversary fitness, evaluation seed count K controls mode regression: K = 2 regresses, while K = 5 sustains a strong specialist for all 30 generations. Adversarial co-evolution of natural-language constitutions is feasible, but only under coupled fitness and adequate evaluation budget; the evolved Red constitutions serve as interpretable red-team artifacts for testing future cooperative designs.

</details>


### [157] [Persistent AI Agents in Academic Research: A Single-Investigator Implementation Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26870)
*Anas H. Alzahrani*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过为期4个月的自我观察案例研究，首次系统性评估了大型语言模型在持久化科研环境中的运行表现，发现缓存主导的工作流可能将经济计量单位从"每token成本"转变为"每完成产出物成本"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要将大型语言模型作为模型、基准测试或短对话会话进行评估，缺乏对智能体长期嵌入真实学术研究环境（具备持久记忆、本地文件、外部工具、调度任务、委托角色和安全协议）的系统性认知。研究旨在填补这一空白，探索持久化智能体环境的实际运行特征。

Method: 采用结构化自我观察案例研究方法，研究周期为2026年1月31日至5月25日。分析单元为持久化人-智能体环境系统，包括研究者、智能体运行时、记忆层、工具、仓库、调度任务、专业化智能体角色和治理规则。使用PARE-M（持久化智能体研究环境测量）框架组织结果，涵盖架构、利用率、产出物生产、资源使用、可复现性和治理六个维度。

Result: 可恢复的主智能体遥测数据包含96个活跃日内的75,671条去重记录，其中用户角色消息8,059条、助手角色消息23,710条。工作空间包含502个记忆相关文件、17个配置智能体目录和57个技能文件。系统活跃时间579.7小时。记忆衍生记录识别出482个输出代理事件和889个失败/验证/修正/协议代理事件。2026年5月的严格轨迹子集捕获627个模型完成事件和7,395万记录token，其中82.9%为缓存读取。

Conclusion: 工作流呈现缓存主导特征，表明持久化智能体环境可能将经济计量单位从"每token成本"转变为"每完成产出物成本"。未来评估应采用产出物级别分母、可复现解析规则、修正分类体系以及独立的治理事件编码方法。

Abstract: Background: Large language models are typically evaluated as models, benchmarks, or short conversational episodes. Less is known about what happens when an agent is embedded persistently in a real academic research environment with durable memory, local files, external tools, scheduled routines, delegated roles, and explicit safety protocols. Methods: A structured self-observed implementation case study was conducted from January 31 to May 25, 2026. The unit of analysis was the persistent human-agent environment: researcher, agent runtime, memory layer, tools, repositories, scheduled jobs, specialized agent roles, and governance rules. Outcomes were organized using PARE-M (Persistent Agentic Research Environment Measurement), a measurement framework covering architecture, utilization, artifact production, resource use, reproducibility, and governance. Results: Recoverable main-agent telemetry contained 75,671 de-duplicated records across 96 active days, with 8,059 user-role and 23,710 assistant-role messages. The workspace included 502 memory-related files, 17 configured agent directories, and 57 skill files. Active system time was 579.7 hours (30-minute capped-gap estimate). Memory-derived records identified 482 output-proxy events and 889 failure, verification, correction, or protocol-proxy events. A strict May 2026 trajectory subset captured 627 model-completed events and 73.95 million recorded tokens, of which 82.9% were cache reads. Conclusions: The workflow was cache-dominant, suggesting that persistent agentic environments may shift the economic unit from cost per token to cost per completed artifact. Future evaluations should use artifact-level denominators, reproducible parsing rules, correction taxonomies, and independent coding of governance events.

</details>


### [158] [Cost of Structural Learning Under Censored Feedback: A Threshold-Bandit Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27076)
*Michael Ledford,William Regli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体在未知阈值触发奖励机制下的协作学习问题，提出了TAC-MAB模型，并设计了集中式(C-TAC)和分布式(D-TAC)算法，其中D-TAC在保持性能的同时将通信开销降低了23倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体应用中，许多任务只有在足够数量的智能体组成联盟时才能获得奖励，否则反馈会被完全审查。这种审查导致了可识别性问题：智能体无法区分随机失败和协调不足，因此需要研究如何在被审查的反馈下进行有效学习。

Method: 形式化阈值激活协作多臂老虎机(TAC-MAB)问题；设计集中式算法C-TAC，实现O(log T)的累积遗憾；提出分布式事件触发协议D-TAC，智能体仅在结构信念变化时同步，采用保守信念融合策略。

Result: 理论分析表明C-TAC的累积遗憾可分解为结构搜索项和统计监控项；实验结果显示D-TAC相比集中式基准实现了23倍的通信减少，同时在保守信念融合下保持了可行性对齐。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了在被审查反馈下学习的协调成本，证明了无需持续同步即可实现接近集中式的通信效率，为分布式多智能体协作学习提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: In many multi-agent applications, tasks yield rewards only when executed by a coalition meeting an unknown size threshold; otherwise, feedback is fully censored. This censorship creates an identifiability problem: agents cannot distinguish stochastic failure from insufficient coordination. We formalize this setting as the Threshold-Activated Cooperative Multi-Armed Bandit (TAC-MAB) and analyze it under both centralized and decentralized coordination. We show that a centralized algorithm (C-TAC) achieves cumulative regret O(log T), decomposed into a structural-search term that captures the cost of resolving feasibility under censored feedback and a statistical-monitoring term for value estimation. We then introduce D-TAC, a decentralized event-triggered protocol in which agents synchronize only when their structural beliefs change. Empirically, D-TAC achieves a 23x reduction in communication relative to the centralized baseline while preserving feasibility alignment under conservative belief fusion. These results characterize the coordination cost of learning under censored feedback and show that near-centralized communication efficiency is achievable without continuous synchronization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [BrickAnything: Geometry-Conditioned Buildable Brick Generation with Structure-Aware Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26182)
*Zhengyang Ni,Feng Yan,Yu Guo,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BrickAnything，一种基于几何条件的自回归框架，能够从点云等多样化3D表示生成几何保真且物理可建造的积木结构，通过结构感知树形标记化、偏好对齐后训练和约束解码等方法有效提升了结构稳定性与生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有积木生成方法存在明显局限性：基于启发式优化的方法在目标3D形状不满足预定义约束时可能无法找到可行解；而序列生成方法则缺乏对底层3D几何和装配关系的显式建模，难以保证结构的物理可建造性（如稳定性和离散零件约束）。

Method: 1) 以点云作为统一几何接口，采用几何条件化的自回归框架生成积木序列；2) 提出结构感知树形标记化方法，通过局部附着关系表示积木结构，使序列生成与物理建造过程一致，减少无效中间状态；3) 引入偏好对齐后训练、有效性约束解码和自适应回退机制，提升可建造性指标（稳定性与几何保真度）。

Result: 实验表明BrickAnything能够生成几何保真且物理可实现的积木结构；所提出的树形标记化方法相比传统排序策略显著降低了回退和重新生成的频率，提升了生成效率与成功率。

Conclusion: BrickAnything框架成功实现了从多样化3D表示到可建造积木结构的有效转换，为积木结构生成问题提供了新思路。其核心贡献在于将几何信息与装配约束紧密结合，并通过结构感知标记化和约束解码机制保证了生成结果的物理可实现性。

Abstract: Generating physically buildable brick structures from 3D shapes requires more than geometric reconstruction: the output must also satisfy discrete part constraints and structural stability. Existing brick generation methods either rely on heuristic optimization, which can break down when the target 3D shape does not admit a feasible structure under predefined constraints, or generate brick sequences without explicitly modeling the underlying 3D geometry and assembly relations. In this work, we present BrickAnything, a geometry-conditioned autoregressive framework for generating buildable brick structures from diverse 3D representations. BrickAnything uses point clouds as a unified geometric interface and predicts brick sequences that reconstruct the target shape under assembly constraints. To model structural dependencies among bricks, we introduce a structure-aware tree tokenization, which represents brick structures through local attachment relations. This formulation makes sequence generation more consistent with the physical construction process, and reduces invalid intermediate states. We further introduce preference-based alignment post-training, validity-constrained decoding and adaptive rollback to improve buildability objectives such as stability and geometric fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BrickAnything produces geometrically faithful and physically realizable brick structures, and that the proposed tokenization effectively reduces rollback and regeneration compared with conventional ordering strategies.

</details>


### [160] [Can LLMs Introspect? A Reality Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26242)
*Shashwat Singh,Tal Linzen,Shauli Ravfogel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过重新检验两种评估范式，发现现有证据不足以证明大语言模型具备内省能力，模型的表现更可能源于模式匹配而非真正的元认知监控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称大语言模型能够检测并报告自身内部状态，但作者基于人类元认知研究的经验指出，现有结论可能为时过早，需要区分真正的内省与基于表面线索的模式匹配，且仅依靠行为证据不足以建立强有力的内省主张。

Method: 重新检验两种评估范式：(1)检测内部状态是否被篡改的任务，通过对比模型对内部状态干预和输入操作的区分能力；(2)基于隐藏状态预测标签的任务，通过比较仅有输入访问权限的分类器与模型自身上下文预测的性能，并引入重新标记的控制设置。

Result: 在第一种范式中，模型无法可靠区分内部状态干预与输入操作，表明其成功源于一般的异常检测能力；在第二种范式中，仅有输入访问权限的分类器与模型自身表现相当，且在重新标记的控制设置中模型表现接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 当前证据不足以证明大语言模型展现出元认知监控能力，模型的成功更可能反映基于表面线索的模式匹配，而非对内部表征的特权访问。

Abstract: Can large language models detect and report their own internal states? A number of studies have argued that the answer to this question is yes. We argue, based on lessons from human metacognition research, that this conclusion may be premature: to be convinced of this conclusion we need to distinguish genuine introspection from pattern matching based on surface-level cues. Furthermore, we argue that behavioral evidence alone is inherently insufficient to establish strong introspective claims.
  We re-examine two recently introduced evaluation paradigms in light of this consideration. In the first paradigm, models are expected to detect whether their internal states have been tampered with. We find that models cannot reliably distinguish such interventions on their internal states from manipulations of the input, suggesting that their success in the original studies reflects their ability to detect anomalies more generally, as opposed to interventions on their internal states in particular. In the second paradigm we examine, models are tasked with predicting labels derived from their own hidden states. Here, we find that classifiers that only have access to the input achieve equivalent performance to the model's own in-context predictions, indicating that the original results do not conclusively demonstrate that the model has privileged access to its internal representations. We further introduce a relabeled control setting, where models cannot rely on the semantics of the task to solve it, and instead must rely on the internal representation; models perform closer to chance on this better-controlled version of the task. Taken together, these results indicate that current evidence is insufficient to establish that LLMs display metacognitive monitoring.

</details>


### [161] [Is Agent Memory a Database? Rethinking Data Foundations for Long-Term AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26252)
*Abdelghny Orogat,Essam Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GEM框架，将长期智能体记忆视为新型数据管理工作负载，通过四个状态级操作算子和六个正确性条件解决现有系统的四种失效模式，证明记录级系统无法满足要求，并实现MemState原型验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的AI智能体需要持久化记忆以支持跨会话学习、减少重复上下文注入并支持决策审计。现有智能体记忆系统和数据库范式将记忆视为存储，关注记录、嵌入或边的正确性，导致四种失效模式：无限制增长、语义修订缺失、容量驱动遗忘和只读检索。每个系统仅提供长期记忆所需的部分能力。

Method: 提出Governed Evolving Memory (GEM)抽象，将长期智能体记忆定义为新的数据管理工作负载，其正确性是状态轨迹的属性而非单个记录的属性。GEM用四个状态级操作算子替代记录级数据库操作：摄入(ingestion)、修订(revision)、遗忘(forgetting)和检索(retrieval)。建立六个正确性条件来管控状态演化。通过三个结构性观察证明无论采用何种存储模型，记录级系统都无法满足这些条件。在属性图后端上实现MemState原型系统。

Result: 证明了没有任何记录级系统能够满足GEM的六个正确性条件，无论存储模型如何。MemState原型验证了该抽象的可行性，同时暴露了与原生引擎之间的差距。识别出定义以记忆为中心的数据管理工作负载的三个研究方向。

Conclusion: 长期智能体记忆应该被视为一个全新的数据管理工作负载，其核心是将正确性从记录级别提升到状态轨迹级别。GEM框架为构建真正满足长期记忆需求的系统提供了理论基础和实践路径，需要原生引擎的支持而非简单复用现有数据库范式。

Abstract: Long-running AI agents need persistent memory. Memory supports learning across sessions, reduces repeated context injection, and enables auditing of past decisions. Current agent memory systems and database paradigms treat memory as storage. They localize correctness at records, embeddings, or edges. Each supplies only some of the capabilities that long-term memory requires. The result is four recurring failure modes: unregulated growth, missing semantic revision, capacity-driven forgetting, and read-only retrieval. In our vision, long-term agent memory is a new data-management workload. Its correctness is a property of the state trajectory, not of individual records. We formalize this as Governed Evolving Memory (GEM). GEM replaces record-level database operations with four state-level operators: ingestion, revision, forgetting, and retrieval. Six correctness conditions govern how the state evolves. Three structural observations establish that no record-level system can satisfy these conditions, regardless of the storage model. We realize the abstraction in MemState, a prototype on a property-graph backend. MemState validates feasibility and exposes the gap to a native engine. We outline three research directions that define memory-centric data management as a workload.

</details>


### [162] [Personalizing Embodied Multimodal Large Language Model Agents over Long-term User Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26256)
*Jeongeun Lee,Chanyoung Park,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出POLAR框架，通过多模态记忆增强机制使具身智能体能够利用长期交互中积累的个性化上下文，显著提升了在多跳推理和用户上下文追踪等复杂场景下的任务执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的具身智能体在个性化辅助方面存在不足。在真实场景中，用户意图往往通过历史交互隐式表达，而非通过明确的指令或物体类别指定，这要求智能体能够利用随时间积累的个性化上下文来理解用户需求。

Method: 提出POLAR框架，将历史交互组织为多模态知识图谱，包含两类记忆：语义记忆（捕获个性化上下文和视觉概念）和情景记忆（捕获具身体验如智能体轨迹）。在执行任务时，通过检索相关记忆来解释当前请求并指导任务执行。

Result: 在多个MLLM骨干网络和多样化评估场景上的实验表明，所提出的记忆机制能够持续提升性能。在需要跨多次交互推理、执行多跳推理或追踪用户特定上下文变化的场景中，性能提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 记忆机制对于长期个性化具身智能体至关重要。通过有效利用历史交互中积累的信息，POLAR框架成功解决了个性化辅助中的隐式意图理解问题，为构建更智能的个性化具身助手提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based embodied agents have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks in physical environments. However, personalized assistance requires more than following generic instruction or recognizing object categories. In real-world scenarios, the intended target is often specified only implicitly through prior interactions, requiring agents to leverage personalized context accumulated over time. In this work, we propose POLAR, a multiomodal memory-augmented framework for personalized embodied agents over long-term user interactions. POLAR organizes prior interactions into a multimodal knowledge graph that captures semantic memory for personalized context and visual concepts, and episodic memory for embodied experiences such as agent trajectories. To execute embodied tasks, POLAR retrieves relevant memories to interpret the current request and guide task execution. We evaluate POLAR across multiple MLLM backbones and diverse evaluation scenarios to study the role of memory in long-term personalization. Results show that the proposed memory mechanism consistently improves performance by enabling more effective use of information accumulated over prior interactions. The gains are especially pronounced when the agents are required to reason across multiple interactions, perform multi-hop inference, or tracking updates in user-specific context over time.

</details>


### [163] [Constraint acquisition needs better benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26279)
*Rafał Stachowiak,Tomasz P. Pawlak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MPMMine基准测试套件，用于评估从领域知识中发现、验证和增强数学规划模型的算法，解决了现有基准测试不适合约束获取研究的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的约束获取(CA)及相关数学规划模型验证与增强研究缺乏适当的基准测试。现有基准测试存在以下问题：主要为求解器评估而设计而非CA算法；组织松散；对单个问题处理不一致；缺少CA方法所需的领域知识工件。这些缺陷阻碍了研究的可重复性和跨研究比较，减缓了CA方法的成熟。

Method: 提出了MPMMine基准测试套件，遵循一致性、标准化、完整性、可扩展性、开放性和版本控制原则。采用统一结构，依赖开放格式：MiniZinc、CommonMark和JSON。每个问题提供多个模型，每个模型提供数十个实例，以及在整数和连续域中的数千个解和非解，同时提供自然语言描述以支持文本到模型的方法。

Result: MPMMine提供了一个标准化的基准测试套件，包含多样化的数学规划模型、实例和解，能够支持约束获取算法的评估。该套件具有统一的结构和开放格式，便于研究者使用和扩展。

Conclusion: MPMMine填补了约束获取和数学规划模型增强领域基准测试的空白，通过提供标准化、完整的基准测试资源，有望促进该领域研究的可重复性和方法成熟度。

Abstract: Constraint Acquisition (CA) and related research on the validation and enhancement of Mathematical Programming (MP) models from domain knowledge artifacts are currently limited by inadequate benchmarks. This deficiency impedes reproducibility and cross-study comparability, slowing the maturation of CA methods. Existing benchmarks were designed for solver evaluation rather than for assessing CA algorithms. They are loosely organized, treat individual problems inconsistently, and omit the domain knowledge artifacts required by CA methods. This work presents MPMMine, a benchmark suite designed to assess algorithms that discover, validate, and enhance MP models using diverse domain knowledge artifacts. MPMMine is guided by consistency, standardization, completeness, extensibility, openness, and version control. It adopts a uniform structure and relies on open formats: MiniZinc, CommonMark, and JSON. It provides multiple models per problem, tens of instances per model, and thousands of solutions and non-solutions in both integer and continuous domains, alongside natural-language descriptions to support text-to-model methods.

</details>


### [164] [Your Agents Are Aging Too: Agent Lifespan Engineering for Deployed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26302)
*Jianing Zhu,Yeonju Ro,John Robertson,Kevin Wang,Junbo Li,Haris Vikalo,Aditya Akella,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AgingBench，首个针对AI智能体全生命周期可靠性的纵向评测基准，发现智能体在部署后存在多种老化机制导致的性能衰退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI智能体评测方法仅关注初始状态，忽视了智能体在长期运行过程中因交互历史压缩、记忆检索、事实更新和维护操作而产生的状态演变和可靠性衰退问题。

Method: 提出AgingBench基准，将智能体老化分为四种机制（压缩老化、干扰老化、修订老化、维护老化），使用时间依赖图和成对反事实探针生成记忆流水线写入、检索和利用阶段的诊断配置文件。

Result: 在7个场景、14个模型、多种记忆策略下进行约400次运行（8-200个会话），发现智能体老化是多维度的：行为测试可能正常但事实精度下降；派生状态追踪可能在单个模型内急剧崩溃；相同错误答案可能需要不同的修复策略。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体部署需要全生命周期评测、机制级诊断和阶段针对性修复，仅依靠更强的初始模型是不够的。

Abstract: Long-lived AI agents are increasingly deployed as persistent operational systems, yet they are still evaluated like freshly initialized models. Day-one benchmarks miss a basic systems question: how long does an agent remain reliable after deployment? Even when model weights are frozen, an agent's effective state keeps changing as it compresses interaction history, retrieves from a growing memory store, revises facts after updates, and undergoes routine maintenance. Reliability therefore becomes a lifespan property of the full agent harness, not only a snapshot property of the base model. We introduce AgingBench, a longitudinal reliability benchmark for agent lifespan engineering: measuring not only whether deployed agents degrade, but what form the degradation takes and where repair should target. AgingBench organizes agent aging into four mechanisms: compression aging, interference aging, revision aging, and maintenance aging. To diagnose these failures, AgingBench uses temporal dependency graphs and paired counterfactual probes that produce diagnostic profiles for the write, retrieval, and utilization stages of the memory pipeline. Across 7 scenarios, 14 models, multiple memory policies, and both runner-controlled and autonomous agents, over ~400 runs spanning 8 - 200 sessions show that agent aging is not one-dimensional: behavioral tests can remain clean while factual precision decays; derived-state tracking can collapse sharply within a single model; and the same wrong answer can require different repairs depending on what the diagnostic profile points to. These results suggest that reliable agent deployment requires lifespan evaluation, mechanism-level diagnosis, and stage-targeted repair, not only stronger day-one models.

</details>


### [165] [Experiments in Agentic AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26305)
*Judy Fox,Geoffrey Fox*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于混合"本地主体-远程大脑"架构的自主代理AI框架，分别用于时间序列数据集的大规模自动管理以及复杂物理讲座的结构化报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进AI系统在上下文理解和推理能力方面存在局限，难以严格支持科学工作流。作者旨在开发自主代理AI系统来克服这些限制，实现科学工作流的自动化支持。

Method: 采用混合"本地主体-远程大脑"架构（通过Google Colab），使用基于Python的本地编排器调用大型语言模型（LLM）云端后端。关键技术包括：细粒度属性提取（Cellular RAG）、远程数据检查和分布式并发控制等系统工程方法。

Result: 成功开发并验证了两个代理系统：(1) DeepTS/DeepCollector：自动化时间序列数据集的大规模筛选、提取和去重；(2) DeepScribe：将视觉密集、数学复杂的物理讲座自主转换为结构化科学报告。

Conclusion: 证明了代理AI能够有效克服当前系统的上下文和推理限制，成功支持科学工作流。同时提出了DeepTS向深度知识图谱的泛化方案，并探讨了该概念方法在高能物理领域（DeepQCD）的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper details two novel frameworks for developing autonomous, agentic AI in scientific workflows. Both systems leverage a hybrid Local Body, Remote Brain architecture via Google Colab, utilizing Python-based local orchestrators to invoke large language model (LLM) cloud backends. The first agent, DeepTS/DeepCollector, automates the large-scale curation, extraction, and deduplication of time-series datasets. The second, DeepScribe, is an autonomous presentation analyzer that converts visually dense, mathematically complex physics lectures into structured scientific reports. Through practical systems engineering-such as granular attribute extraction (Cellular RAG), remote data inspection, and distributed concurrency controls-we demonstrate how agentic AI can overcome the context and reasoning limitations of current state-of-the-art systems to rigorously support scientific workflows. Finally, we outline a generalization of DeepTS to support deep knowledge graphs and discuss the application of this conceptual approach to high-energy physics (DeepQCD).

</details>


### [166] [ScientistOne: Towards Human-Level Autonomous Research via Chain-of-Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26340)
*Rui Meng,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Jiefeng Chen,Chun-Liang Li,Palash Goyal,Mihir Parmar,Yiwen Song,Yale Song,Rajarishi Sinha,Parthasarathy Ranganathan,Burak Gokturk,Jinsung Yoon,Tomas Pfister*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Chain-of-Evidence (CoE)可验证性框架、ScientistOne端到端自主研究系统以及CoE Audit审计方法，解决了自主研究代理产生的幻觉引用、不可复现分数和方法-代码不一致等问题，在五个前沿研究任务中实现了零幻觉引用、完美分数验证和最高方法-代码一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主研究代理虽然能产生竞争性解决方案和专业手稿，但其输出包含表面评估无法检测的可验证性失败：虚构引用、不可复现分数以及与实现不一致的方法描述，亟需建立系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出三大核心贡献：1) Chain-of-Evidence (CoE)可验证性框架，要求每个主张都可追溯到证据源；2) ScientistOne端到端自主研究系统，在文献综述、方案发现和论文撰写全过程中通过构造维护证据链；3) CoE Audit事后审计工具，包含分数验证、规范违规、引用验证和方法-代码对齐四个统一适用的完整性检查。

Result: 在75篇论文、五个系统、五个前沿研究任务的评估中，所有基线系统都存在系统性失败模式：幻觉引用率达21%，分数验证通过率低至42%，方法-代码对齐率20%-80%。ScientistOne实现零幻觉引用(0/337)、完美分数验证(12/12)和最高方法-代码对齐率(14/15)，在所有五个任务上匹配或超越人类专家表现，并在六个额外任务中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过Chain-of-Evidence框架和ScientistOne系统成功解决了自主研究代理的可验证性问题，证明了在保持高性能的同时可以确保研究输出的可信度和可复现性，为自主研究系统的可靠性建立了新标准。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents produce competitive solutions and professional-looking manuscripts, yet their outputs contain verifiability failures undetectable by surface-level evaluation: fabricated citations, unreproducible scores, and method descriptions that diverge from the implementation. We address this through three contributions. First, Chain-of-Evidence (CoE), a verifiability framework requiring every claim to be traceable to its evidence source. Second, ScientistOne, an end-to-end autonomous research system that maintains evidence chains by construction throughout literature review, solution discovery, and paper writing. Third, CoE Audit, a post-hoc audit whose four integrity checks -- score verification, specification violation, reference verification, and method-code alignment -- apply uniformly to all systems. Across 75 papers spanning five systems and five frontier research tasks, every baseline exhibits at least one systematic failure mode: hallucinated reference rates reach 21%, score verification passes in as few as 42% of papers, and method-code alignment ranges from 20% to 80%. ScientistOne achieves zero hallucinated references (0/337), perfect score verification (12/12), and the highest method-code alignment (14/15), while matching or exceeding human expert performance on all five tasks. ScientistOne further generalizes to six additional tasks spanning medical imaging, fine-grained recognition, 3D perception, and language modeling, achieving state-of-the-art on Parameter Golf and gold medals on MLE-Bench tasks where baselines fail entirely.

</details>


### [167] [Anchor: Mitigating Artifact Drift in Agent Benchmark Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26321)
*Maksim Ivanov,Abhijay Rana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Anchor任务生成流水线，通过形式化业务工作流规范来解决企业环境中指令、环境、求解器和验证器不一致的问题（artifacts drift），并基于此创建了包含300个长期任务的ERP-Bench基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体的企业工作训练和评估环境在真实性、可验证性和规模之间难以平衡。由于指令、环境、预言机和验证器由松耦合过程创建，经常出现对任务要求的不一致，导致环境无法求解、易受奖励黑客攻击或不一致。

Method: 提出Anchor流水线，将领域专家的业务工作流规范形式化为约束优化程序。从单一参数化规范出发，联合生成自然语言指令、环境配置、经求解器认证的基本真值解和基于状态的验证器。修改参数可生成可控难度且已知最优解的新任务。

Result: 应用Anchor创建了ERP-Bench基准，包含跨采购和制造工作流的300个长期任务，运行在生产级ERP系统上。生成参数能有效预测实际难度。前沿模型在26.1%的试验中满足显式任务约束，但仅17.4%达到完全最优解。

Conclusion: Anchor和ERP-Bench为构建可审计的、具有经济价值的智能体工作评估环境提供了具体方案，并公开发布了任务生成器和数据集。

Abstract: AI agents are beginning to complete valuable, long-horizon business operations tasks, but training and evaluation environments for enterprise work still struggle to balance realism, verifiability, and scale. Environment and task creation frequently suffers from a failure mode we call artifact drift: when instructions, environments, oracles, and verifiers are created by loosely coupled processes, they frequently disagree on what a task requires, producing environments that are unsolvable, reward-hackable, or inconsistent. We introduce Anchor, a task-generation pipeline that formalizes domain experts' specifications of business workflows into constraint optimization programs. From a single parametric specification, the pipeline jointly produces a natural-language instruction, environment configuration, solver-certified ground-truth solution, and state-based verifier. With Anchor, altering parameters yields new tasks with controlled difficulty and known optimal solutions, producing harness-agnostic environments whose rewards depend solely on end-state business correctness. We apply Anchor to produce ERP-Bench: a benchmark of 300 long-horizon tasks spanning procurement and manufacturing workflows in a production-grade ERP system. We find that generation parameters predict realized difficulty, and that frontier models satisfy explicit task constraints in 26.1% of trials but reach a fully optimal solution in only 17.4% of trials. Overall, we show that Anchor and ERP-Bench offer a concrete recipe for building auditable evaluation environments for economically valuable agent work. We release the task generator and ERP-Bench dataset at erpbench.ai

</details>


### [168] [UnityMAS-O: A General RL Optimization Framework for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26646)
*Yiqun Chen,Wei Yang,Erhan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Qi Liu,Zechun Niu,Bin Zhang,Haitao Li,Rui Li,Lingyong Yan,Jinyuan Feng,Biqing Qi,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了UnityMAS-O，一个针对LLM多智能体系统的通用强化学习优化框架，将完整工作流作为优化单元，通过四类核心对象实现逻辑智能体与物理模型参数的解耦，在检索增强QA、迭代智能搜索和反思式代码生成任务上显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统主要依赖人工编排提示、工具和控制规则，缺乏统一的强化学习接口进行优化。现有RL后训练框架主要面向单策略优化，缺乏用户自定义多智能体工作流、结构化交互、角色特定信用分配和可配置参数共享的抽象机制。

Method: UnityMAS-O将完整工作流作为优化单元，通过四类一等对象表示工作流：逻辑智能体角色、图轨迹、用户定义奖励和智能体-模型映射。框架采用基于Ray的星型拓扑运行时，中央控制器执行工作流、调用工具、记录结构化轨迹并组装奖励，模型本地工作组处理rollout、缓冲、优势计算和分布式PPO风格更新。支持全共享、全分离和部分共享的参数配置，并在角色、轮次和轨迹三个层面分配奖励。

Result: 在检索增强QA、迭代智能搜索和反思式代码生成任务上进行验证，在Natural Questions、HotpotQA和保留代码任务上，多智能体RL优化后的工作流优于人工指定的工作流，尤其是对于较小模型和严格的代码全通过指标，性能提升更为显著。

Conclusion: UnityMAS-O可以作为可复用的基础架构，将多样化的基于LLM的多智能体工作流转换为可训练的多智能体RL系统，填补了当前LLM多智能体系统缺乏统一强化学习优化接口的空白。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into interacting roles, but most remain manually orchestrated by prompts, tools, and control rules, while agents are rarely optimized through a unified reinforcement learning interface. Existing RL post-training frameworks mainly target single-policy optimization and lack abstractions for user-defined multi-agent workflows, structured interaction, role-specific credit assignment, and configurable parameter sharing.
  We present UnityMAS-O, a general RL optimization framework for LLM-based multi-agent systems. UnityMAS-O treats the complete workflow as the optimization unit, rather than a single response or policy trajectory. It represents workflows through four first-class objects: logical agent roles, graph trajectories, user-defined rewards, and agent--model mappings. This decouples logical agents from physical model parameters, supporting full sharing, full separation, and partial sharing, with rewards assigned at role, turn, and trajectory levels.
  UnityMAS-O extends verl with a Ray-based star-topology runtime. A central controller executes workflows, invokes tools, records structured trajectories, and assembles rewards; model-local worker groups handle rollout, buffering, advantage computation, and distributed PPO-style updates. Users can define agents, workflows, model mappings, and rewards without rewriting the optimization infrastructure.
  We instantiate UnityMAS-O on retrieval-augmented QA, iterative agentic search, and reflective code generation. Across Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and held-out code tasks, multi-agent RL improves manually specified workflows after optimization, with especially large gains for smaller models and strict code all-passed metrics. These results show that UnityMAS-O can serve as a reusable substrate for converting diverse LLM-based multi-agent workflows into trainable multi-agent RL systems.

</details>


### [169] [OmniToM: Benchmarking Theory of Mind in LLMs via Explicit Belief Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26322)
*Adam Bawatneh,Sagar Sapkota,Amrit Singh Bedi,Santu Karmaker,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了OmniToM基准，通过显式建模信念结构来评估大语言模型的心理理论能力，发现现有模型在行为者特定的信念追踪方面存在瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的心理理论评估范式（端点问答）仅根据最终答案判断性能，掩盖了模型是否真正构建了鲁棒推理所需的心理状态表示，特别是在涉及分歧、演变或错误信念的场景中。

Method: 提出OmniToM基准，采用两阶段评估框架：阶段1（信念提取）从故事中提取与社会动态相关的信念；阶段2（信念标记）为每个信念分配七维模式标签（递归顺序、真实状态、知识获取、明确性、内容类型、心理来源和语境）。基准基于895个故事和22,343个标注信念命题构建，使用人工校准的LLM辅助标注流程。

Result: 在零样本评估中，OmniToM揭示了行为者特定的信念追踪瓶颈：当前LLMs在将叙事事实转化为行为者信念和共享心理状态所需的知识获取和表征决策方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在心理理论任务中的核心弱点在于知识获取和表征决策能力，无法有效将叙事事实转化为行为者的信念和共享心理状态，这为未来模型改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to infer others' knowledge, intentions, and emotions, is commonly evaluated in large language models (LLMs) using end-point question answering, where performance is judged solely by the final answer to a social reasoning query. This paradigm obscures whether the model actually constructs the underlying mental-state representations required for robust reasoning, particularly in scenarios involving divergent, evolving, or mistaken beliefs. In order to address this research gap, we introduce OmniToM, a benchmark that directly evaluates these representations by requiring explicit modeling of belief structures for all relevant actors within a narrative. These structures are composed of belief propositions: minimal statements of what an actor takes to be true about the world or another actor's mental state, allowing knowledge, intentions, emotions, and false beliefs to be analyzed in a common format. Models are evaluated in two stages: Stage 1: Belief Extraction, which extracts from the story the beliefs relevant to its social dynamics, and Stage 2: Belief Labeling, which assigns each belief a seven-dimensional schema label covering recursive order, truth status, knowledge access, explicitness, content type, mental source, and context. Built from 895 stories from the existing ToMBench story corpus and augmented with 22,343 labeled belief propositions, OmniToM uses a human-calibrated LLM-assisted annotation pipeline. Across diverse models in zero-shot evaluation, OmniToM reveals an actor-specific belief-tracking bottleneck: current LLMs struggle with the knowledge-access and representational decisions required to transform narrative facts into actors' beliefs and shared mental states.

</details>


### [170] [JobBench: Aligning Agent Work With Human Will](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26329)
*Yuetai Li,Yichen Feng,Zhangchen Xu,Zixian Ma,Kaiyuan Zheng,Fengqing Jiang,Xinghua Sun,Rulin Shao,Zichen Chen,Yue Huang,Xinyang Han,Brian Lee,Kayla Xu,Shenglai Zeng,Hang Hua,Xiangliang Zhang,Basel Alomair,Ranjay Krishna,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Luyao Niu,Xiang Yue,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JobBench基准，从替代人类转向增强人类：基于专家认为高优先级的委托任务评估AI代理，覆盖35种职业130个任务，最佳模型准确率仅45.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业AI代理基准主要以经济价值（GDP价值）为评估范围，强调替代人类的故事。本文希望转变社区的目标劳动市场效应：从替代转向增强，构建真正做人类想要委托的任务的代理，而非仅关注最具经济价值的任务。

Method: 引入JobBench基准，包含35种职业的130个代理任务。每个任务被封装为包含异构参考文件的工作空间，要求代理在真实专业工作的杂乱信息流中进行推理。输出通过事实锚定的评分标准链进行评分，每个任务平均包含35.6个二元标准。评估了36个模型。

Result: 在评估的36个模型中，表现最强的是Claude Opus 4.7（在Claude Code环境下），但准确率仅为45.9%，表明当前AI代理在真实工作任务上仍有很大改进空间。

Conclusion: JobBench旨在推动AI社区将目标从替代人类转向增强人类：构建能够完成人类真正想要委托任务的代理，而非仅关注最具经济价值的任务，从而更好地满足人类需求。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for occupational AI agents are scoped primarily by economic values, telling a replacement story. We introduce JobBench, which evaluates AI agents on the workflows that experts identify as high-priority for delegation, empowering humans based on their needs instead of replacing them with GDP value. JobBench covers 130 agentic tasks across 35 occupations. Each task is packaged as a workspace of heterogeneous reference files, requiring the agent to reason through the cluttered information streams of real professional work. Outputs are graded by a fact-anchored chain of rubrics, averaging 35.6 binary criteria per task. We evaluate 36 models; the strongest, Claude Opus~4.7 under Claude Code, reaches only 45.9 %. We hope JobBench shifts the community's target labour-market effect from replacement to enhancement: building agents that do what humans actually want delegated, not only what is most economically valuable.

</details>


### [171] [MUSE-Autoskill: Self-Evolving Agents via Skill Creation, Memory, Management, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27366)
*Huawei Lin,Peng Li,Jie Song,Fuxin Jiang,Tieying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MUSE-Autoskill Agent框架，通过统一的生命周期管理（创建、记忆、管理、评估、精炼），使大语言模型代理能够持续创建、复用和改进技能，从而提升任务求解能力和跨代理迁移效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能创建方法将技能视为孤立且静态的工件，这限制了技能的可复用性、可靠性和长期改进能力。技能缺乏统一的生命周期管理，难以在多任务间有效积累经验和持续优化。

Method: 提出MUSE-Autoskill Agent框架，实现技能的全生命周期管理：按需创建技能、跨任务存储与复用、高效组织与选择、通过单元测试和运行时反馈进行评估与精炼。引入技能级记忆机制，在每个技能上积累跨任务经验，支持更有效的复用和适应。

Result: 在SkillsBench上的实验表明，生命周期管理的技能能够提高任务成功率、效率、复用率和跨代理迁移能力，验证了将技能视为长期存在、具备经验感知和可测试资产的重要性。

Conclusion: 将技能纳入统一生命周期管理并赋予经验记忆能力，可显著增强LLM代理的任务求解能力与技能复用效率，为构建更可靠、可演进的智能代理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents rely on reusable skills to solve complex tasks. However, existing skill creation approaches treat skills as isolated and static artifacts, limiting their reusability, reliability, and long-term improvement. We propose MUSE-Autoskill Agent (Memory-Utilizing Skill Evolution), a skill-centric agent framework that lets agents continuously improve their task-solving capability by creating, reusing, and refining skills under a unified lifecycle (creation, memory, management, evaluation, and refinement). Our framework enables agents to create skills on demand, store and reuse them across tasks, organize and select them efficiently, and evaluate them through unit tests and runtime feedback for continuous refinement. We further introduce skill-level memory that accumulates experience for each skill across tasks, enabling more effective reuse and adaptation over time. Experiments on SkillsBench provide initial evidence that lifecycle-managed skills can improve task success, efficiency, reuse, and cross-agent transfer, highlighting the importance of treating skills as long-lived, experience-aware, and testable assets.

</details>


### [172] [Managing Uncertainty in LLM-Generated Procedural Knowledge for Virtual Laboratory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26333)
*Polychronis Karpodinis,Dimitris Kalles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个框架，通过结构化领域表示和不确定的LLM生成样本提取程序规则，用于修复和管理虚拟实验室规划中的不确定性程序知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育虚拟实验室可以提供可扩展、自适应的实验训练，但创建新的模拟实验程序成本高昂。虽然大语言模型可以生成详细的实验程序，但其输出可能存在遗漏操作、步骤顺序错误或逻辑不兼容等问题，不能直接作为可执行计划。

Method: 提出了一个原型框架，利用结构化领域表示和不确定的LLM生成的状态转换样本来提取候选程序规则，将其转化为显式可检查的约束，并使用这些约束来修复不确定的程序步骤。

Result: 在涉及实验仪器、容器、工具和材料转移操作的虚拟实验室领域中展示了该方法的有效性，能够减少LLM生成的程序知识中的不确定性。

Conclusion: 虽然动机来自教育虚拟实验室，但该框架解决了更广泛的问题：在结构化交互环境中管理用于动作规划的不确定程序知识。

Abstract: Educational virtual laboratories can make experimental training more scala-ble, adaptive, and accessible, especially when students have limited access to physical laboratory facilities. However, authoring new simulated laboratory procedures remains costly: educators must describe new equipment, define how instruments and materials interact, and specify valid procedural flows that can be executed or assessed inside the virtual environment. Large lan-guage models can assist in this authoring process by generating detailed ex-perimental procedures, but their output should not be treated as directly exe-cutable plans. They may omit necessary actions, arrange steps in the wrong order, or produce instructions that are logically incorrect or incompatible with the laboratory equipment. This paper presents a prototype framework for managing uncertainty in LLM-generated procedural knowledge for virtu-al laboratory planning. The framework aims to reduce procedural uncertainty by using structured domain representations and uncertain LLM-generated state-transition samples to extract candidate procedural rules, transform them into explicit and inspectable constraints, and use them to repair uncertain procedural steps. Although the motivating domain refers to educational vir-tual laboratories, the underlying problem is more general: managing uncer-tain procedural knowledge for action planning in structured interactive envi-ronments. We illustrate the approach in a virtual laboratory domain involving laboratory instruments, containers, tools, and material-transfer actions.

</details>


### [173] [Automatic Layer Selection for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26366)
*Xinpeng Wang,William Cao,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Zhe Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FEPoID（First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension）方法，用于自动选择大语言模型中最适合幻觉检测的中间层，并配合截断策略显著提升了幻觉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明幻觉相关信号在LLMs的中间层比最终层更强，但目前缺乏自动化选择高性能层的原则性方法，如何自动选择最优层仍是一个未充分探索的问题。

Method: 1) 首先提出多个假设解释为何幻觉信号出现在中间层，并评估相应的自动层选择标准；2) 提出新的选择标准FEPoID（内在维度的首个有效峰值），无需训练且计算开销极小；3) 引入简单有效的截断策略，进一步放大幻觉相关信号。

Result: FEPoID在多种LLM架构、规模和任务（问答和摘要幻觉检测基准）上均能一致地识别最优或接近最优的层，性能优于其他选择标准和现有幻觉检测基线方法；截断策略进一步显著提升了整体检测性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的FEPoID方法成功解决了幻觉检测中自动层选择的问题，证明了内在维度特征可用于有效识别最优层，同时截断策略的引入为幻觉检测提供了新的改进方向，该方法具有实用性和通用性。

Abstract: Recent studies on hallucination detection have shown that hallucination-related signals are more strongly encoded in intermediate layers than in the final layer of large language models (LLMs). Although a growing body of work has sought to exploit this property for hallucination detection, how to automate the selection of high-performing layers remains underexplored, and principled methods for this purpose are still lacking. To address this gap, we first propose several hypotheses for why such signals emerge in intermediate layers and evaluate corresponding criteria for automatic layer selection across diverse LLM architectures, scales, and tasks, covering both question answering and summarization hallucination detection benchmarks. However, we find that none of these criteria consistently delivers satisfactory performance. We therefore propose a new selection criterion, First Effective Peak of Intrinsic Dimension (FEPoID), which consistently identify optimal or near-optimal layers and outperforms both the aforementioned criteria and existing hallucination detection baselines. FEPoID is training-free and incurs negligible computational overhead. In addition, we study the generation behaviors of LLMs and introduce a simple yet effective truncation strategy, which further amplifies hallucination-related signals and substantially improves overall detection performance. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DesoloYw/Automatic-Layer-Selection-for-Hallucination-Detection.git

</details>


### [174] [Exploiting Local Dynamics Regularity for Reusable Skills in Offline Hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26371)
*Sarthak Dayal,Abhinav Peri,Carl Qi,Claas Voelcker,Alexander Levine,Caleb Chuck,Amy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARL算法，通过利用局部动力学直觉学习可重用的技能，在复杂环境中实现了有意义技能的聚类，并提升了下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次强化学习（HRL）承诺通过发现和重用时序扩展技能来高效解决长视界任务，但如何获得真正可重用的技能仍然是一个开放挑战。

Method: 提出CARL（Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control）算法，利用局部动力学直觉：不同全局上下文中的局部转换需要相似的动作序列。通过对齐这些上下文与其所需的动作序列，学习哪些技能可重用以及在何处重用它们。

Result: 在复杂人形环境中展示了有意义技能的定性聚类；与HIQL集成时，在OGBench基准测试上提升了下游性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能够使高级策略更好地推理所使用的低级技能，为多种HRL算法提供了通用的技能抽象方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) promises to solve long-horizon Reinforcement Learning (RL) tasks more efficiently than non-hierarchical counterparts by discovering and reusing temporally-extended skills. However, obtaining skills that are actually reusable remains an open challenge. Towards this end, we focus on abstractions that exploit the intuition of local dynamics: local transitions in different global contexts require similar kinds of action sequences. By aligning these contexts with the action sequences they require, we are able to learn which skills to reuse and where to reuse them. In principle, this information should benefit many HRL algorithms, where high-level policies have to reason about the low-level skills they use. The resulting algorithm CARL (Contrastive Action-based Representations for Reusable Local Control) shows both qualitative clustering of meaningful skills in complex humanoid environments and improved downstream performance on the OGBench benchmark when integrated with HIQL.

</details>


### [175] [Advancing Creative Physical Intelligence in Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26396)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Jeonghwan Kim,Emre Can Acikgoz,Bingxuan Li,Kunlun Zhu,Jiateng Liu,Aditi Tiwari,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MM-CreativityBench基准，用于评估大型多模态模型在视觉丰富、物理约束环境中的创造性工具使用能力。研究发现现有模型存在探索不足和幻觉问题，通过提出的可供性对齐方法可以有效提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型多模态模型在感知和推理方面进展迅速，但尚不清楚这些能力是否能泛化到开放环境中的视觉 grounded 创造性问题解决。这种需要识别场景中元素如何以非显而易见但物理可行的方式进行创造性重新组合的能力是人类智能的核心，但在现有基准测试中 largely untested。

Method: 1) 提出MM-CreativityBench基准，包含场景图像和候选实体及其部件的结构化视图，支持细粒度交互式评估；2) 提出可供性对齐方法，将创造性工具使用建模为偏好学习问题；3) 使用直接偏好优化(DPO)鼓励模型偏好基于视觉证据的属性-可供性推理；4) 整合来自可供性知识库的监督信号以指导更广泛的实体探索和多轮规划。

Result: 实验表明当前LMM存在关键缺陷：经常忽略相关实体、对关键部件检查不足、或产生非grounded的幻觉属性，主要原因是缺乏持续的grounded探索而非生成能力不足。所提方法在以下方面取得持续改进：选择正确实体和部件的能力提升，幻觉和grounding相关错误显著减少。

Conclusion: 当前大型多模态模型在创造性工具使用方面存在系统性不足，主要表现为grounded探索能力的欠缺。通过可供性对齐的偏好学习可以有效缓解这一问题，为提升LMM在开放环境中的创造性问题解决能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large multimodal models (LMMs) have rapidly advanced in perception and reasoning; however, it remains unclear whether these capabilities generalize to discovering visually grounded solutions in open-ended environments, beyond pattern recognition. In such settings, intelligence requires more than answering well-posed questions: it involves identifying how elements in a scene can be repurposed in non-obvious yet physically feasible ways. This form of creative problem-solving is central to human intelligence, but remains largely untested in current benchmarks. To evaluate this ability, we introduce MM-CreativityBench, a benchmark for affordance-grounded creative tool use in visually rich, physically constrained environments. Each instance presents a scenario image with structured views of candidate entities and their parts, enabling fine-grained, interactive evaluation of how models iteratively inspect the scene, identify relevant affordances, and compose visually and physically grounded solutions. Our experiments show that current LMMs often fall short, not due to lack of generative capability, but because they do not sustain grounded exploration. Models often overlook relevant entities, under-examine critical parts, or hallucinate attributes not grounded in the image. Motivated by this failure mode, we propose affordance-grounded alignment, which casts creative tool use as a preference learning problem. Using Direct Preference Optimization, we encourage models to prefer attribute-affordance reasoning grounded in visual evidence over hallucinated alternatives. In addition, we incorporate supervision derived from an affordance knowledge base to guide broader entity exploration and multi-turn planning. Our results show consistent gains in selecting the correct entities and parts, while substantially reducing hallucination and grounding-related errors.

</details>


### [176] [From Static Context to Calibrated Interactive RL: Mitigating Distribution Shift in Multi-turn Dialogue with Aligned Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26403)
*Xiaohua Wang,Jiakang Yuan,Zisu Huang,Muzhao Tian,Changze Lv,Kaitao Song,Tao Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Calibrated Interactive RL框架，通过结合交互式强化学习和模拟器对齐来解决LLM对话代理中的上下文分布偏移问题，实现了最先进的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对话代理训练范式存在根本局限性：Static Context RL和Interactive RL都受限于上下文分布偏移，即训练时观察到的对话历史与真实对话中遇到的历史不匹配。这种偏移随对话轮次呈二次方累积，严重降低对话质量。偏移来源于两个因素：(i)策略诱导偏移，源于在静态历史上训练而非自生成轨迹；(ii)模拟器诱导偏移，源于模拟行为与真实人类行为之间的差异。

Method: 提出Calibrated Interactive RL统一框架，将交互式强化学习与模拟器对齐相结合。通过对齐模拟器与人类交互模式，减少模拟到真实的差距(sim-to-real gap)，缓解累积分布偏移问题。

Result: 在多个对话任务上的实验验证了理论分析：(i)Interactive RL通过缓解策略分布偏移显著优于Static Context基线；(ii)用对齐方法校准模拟器进一步弥合模拟到真实的差距，实现了最先进的下游性能。

Conclusion: 理论上揭示了现有训练范式的根本局限性，并通过Calibrated Interactive RL框架成功解决了上下文分布偏移问题，为开发高度交互式LLM对话代理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A long-standing goal of the research community is to develop highly interactive LLM-based dialogue agents. Recent research focuses on optimizing policies based on fixed offline logs (Static Context RL) or using a prompt-based simulator (Interactive RL). In this work, we theoretically show that both paradigms are fundamentally limited by context distribution shift--a mismatch between dialogue histories observed during training and those encountered in real conversations. This shift compounds quadratically over turns and severely degrades dialogue quality. Specifically, we attribute this shift to two distinct sources: (i) policy-induced shift, arising from training on static histories rather than self-generated trajectories; and (ii) simulator-induced shift, stemming from discrepancies between simulated and real human behaviors. To address these challenges, we propose Calibrated Interactive RL, a unified framework that couples interactive RL with simulator alignment. By aligning the simulator with human interaction patterns, our approach reduces the sim-to-real gap and mitigates compounding distribution shifts. Experiments across multiple dialogue tasks confirm our theoretical analysis: (i) Interactive RL significantly outperforms the Static Context baseline by mitigating policy distribution shift; and (ii) calibrating simulators with our alignment method further bridges the sim-to-real gap, yielding state-of-the-art downstream performance.

</details>


### [177] [Reasoning, Code, or Both? How Large Language Models Handle Variations in Math Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26414)
*Matthew Kutakh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了代码执行方法对大语言模型数学推理鲁棒性的影响。实验发现，思维链(CoT)方法在问题变体下表现最为稳健(准确率下降1.3个百分点)，优于单次代码执行PAL(下降1.7个百分点)和迭代代码执行SBSC方法。尽管差异未达统计显著性(p=0.096)，但结果表明代码执行并未提升模型在小学数学问题变体上的推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLMs)在数学推理基准测试中表现优异，但当问题被简单修改（如更换名字或数字）时性能会下降。代码执行方法（让模型生成并运行Python代码而非自然语言推理）已被提出作为解决方案，但其对推理鲁棒性（在问题变体下保持准确性的能力）的影响尚未得到系统性测试。研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究在GSM-Symbolic数据集的1,000个问题上评估了三种方法：(1)使用思维链(CoT)提示的纯推理方法；(2)使用程序辅助语言模型(PAL)的单次代码执行方法；(3)使用逐步编码(SBSC)的迭代代码执行方法。所有三种方法均在Claude Haiku 4.5上运行，并使用配对的原始问题和修改后问题进行测试。

Result: CoT是鲁棒性最强的方法，准确率下降1.3个百分点，1.8%的问题在扰动下失效。PAL鲁棒性最差，准确率下降1.7个百分点，3.1%的问题失效。SBSC介于两者之间。虽然这些差异未达到统计显著性(p=0.096)，但方向性趋势在所有测量指标上保持一致。

Conclusion: 代码执行方法，无论是单次执行还是迭代执行，都不能提高模型在小学数学问题变体上的推理鲁棒性。自然语言推理方法(CoT)在该场景下表现更优。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve impressive accuracy on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet their performance drops when problems are modified with simple changes like different names or numbers. Code execution methods, which let models generate and run Python code instead of reasoning in natural language, have been proposed as a solution, but their effect on reasoning robustness (the ability to maintain accuracy across problem variations) has not been systematically tested. This study evaluates three approaches on 1,000 problems from the GSM-Symbolic dataset: pure reasoning using chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting, single-shot code execution using Program-Aided Language models (PAL), and iterative code execution using Step-by-Step Coding (SBSC). All three were run on paired original and modified problems using Claude Haiku 4.5. CoT was the most robust method, with an accuracy drop of 1.3 percentage points and 1.8% of problems breaking under perturbation. PAL was the least robust at 1.7 percentage points and 3.1% broke, with SBSC falling in between. Although these differences were not statistically significant ($p = .096$), the directional trend was consistent across all measures, suggesting that code execution, whether single-shot or iterative, does not improve reasoning robustness on grade-school-level problem variations.

</details>


### [178] [The MiniMax-M2 Series: Mini Activations Unleashing Max Real-World Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26494)
*MiniMax,:,Aili Chen,Aonian Li,Baichuan Zhou,Bangwei Gong,Binyang Jiang,Boji Dan,Changqing Yu,Chao Wang,Cheng Ma,Cheng Zhong,Cheng Zhu,Chengjun Xiao,Chengyi Yang,Chengyu Du,Chenyang Zhang,Chi Zhang,Chuangyi Huang,Chunhao Zhang,Chunhui Du,Chunyu Zhao,Congchao Guo,Da Chen,Deming Ding,Dianjun Sun,Dongyu Zhang,Enhui Yang,Fei Yu,Guang Zheng,Guodong Zheng,Guohong Li,Haichao Zhu,Haigang Zhou,Haimo Zhang,Han Ding,Hao Zhang,Haohai Sun,Haolin Lyu,Haonan Lu,Haoyu Wang,Huajie Shi,Huiyang Li,Jiacheng Chen,Jian Zhang,Jiaqi Zhuang,Jiaren Cai,Jiaxin Pan,Jiayao Li,Jiayuan Song,Jichuan Zhang,Jie Wang,Jihao Gu,Jin Zhu,Jingwei Dong,Jingyang Li,Jingyu Zhang,Jingze Zhuang,Jinhao Tian,Jinli Liu,Jinyi Hu,Jun Tao,Jun Zhang,Junbin Ruan,Junhao Xu,Junjie Yan,Junteng Liu,Junxian He,Kang Xu,Ke Ji,Ke Yang,Kecheng Xiao,Keyu Duan,Keyu Li,Le Han,Letian Ruan,Li Yuan,Lianfei Yu,Liheng Feng,Lijie Mo,Lin Li,Lingye Bao,Lingyu Yang,Lingyuan Zhou,Loki,Lu Chen,Lunbin Ceng,Ming Li,Ming Zhong,Mingliang Tao,Mingyuan Chi,Mujie Lin,Nan Hu,Ningxin Chen,Peiyin Zhu,Peng Gao,Pengcheng Gao,Pengfei Li,Penglin Li,Pengyu Zhao,Qibin Ren,Qidi Xu,Qihan Ren,Qile Li,Qin Wang,Quanliang Chen,Qunhong Ceng,Rong Tian,Rui Dong,Ruitao Leng,Ruize Zhang,Shanqi Liu,Shaoyu Chen,Sheng Jia,Shun Yao,Shuoran Zhao,Shuqi Yu,Sichen Li,Sicheng Pan,Songquan Zhu,Tengfei Li,Tian Xie,Tiancheng Qin,Tianrun Liang,Wei Liu,Weiqi Xu,Weitao Li,Weixiang Chen,Weiyu Cheng,Weiyu Zhang,Wenhu Chen,Wenqian Zhao,Xiancai Chen,Xiangjun Song,Xiangyuan Wang,Xiao Luo,Xiao Su,Xiaobo Li,Xiaodong Han,Xiaojie Wu,Xihao Song,Xingyi Han,Xinyu Guan,Xuan Lu,Xun Zou,Xunhao Lai,Xutong Li,Yan Gong,Yang Wang,Yang Xu,Yangsen Wang,Ye Tang,Yicheng Chen,Yinran Qiu,Yiqi Shi,Yiting Guo,Yiwen Huang,Yixuan Wang,Yongyi Hu,Yu Gao,Yu Zhang,Yuanxiang Ying,Yuanzhen Zhang,Yubo Wang,Yuchen Song,Yufeng Yang,Yuhang Meng,Yuhang Miao,Yuhao Li,Yujie Liu,Yulin Hu,Yunan Huang,Yunji Li,Yunyi Huang,Yusen Zhang,Yusu Hong,Yutao Xie,Yutong Zhang,Yuwen Liao,Yuxuan Shi,Yuze Wenren,Zebin Li,Zehan Li,Zejian Luo,Zeyu Jin,Zeyuan Sun,Zhanpeng Zhou,Zhaochen Su,Zhendong Li,Zhengmao Zhu,Zhengyuan Peng,Zhenhua Fan,Zhi Zhang,Zhichao Xu,Zhiheng Lv,Zhikang Xu,Zhitao He,Zhiwei He,Zhongyuan Li,Zibo Gao,Zijia Wu,Zijian Song,Zijian Zhou,Zijun Sun,Zishan Huang,Ziying Chen,Ziyue Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MiniMax-M2系列混合专家语言模型，通过极小的激活参数（9.8B/229.9B总参数）实现了前沿智能体性能，并引入了首个能自主调试训练过程并修改自身脚本的自我进化检查点M2.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型在部署时面临激活参数量大、计算成本高的问题，且缺乏针对智能体任务的端到端优化设计。本文旨在通过"小激活、大智能"原则，设计专为智能体部署优化的高效MoE架构。

Method: 构建了三个核心组件：(i)智能体驱动数据管道，生成大规模可验证轨迹（覆盖智能体编程和协作任务）；(ii)Forge——可扩展的智能体原生强化学习系统，支持长期轨迹适应、窗口化FIFO调度、前缀树合并和推理优化；(iii)M2.7检查点实现自我进化能力，可自主调试训练运行并修改自身脚手架。

Result: M2系列模型在智能体编程、深度搜索、办公任务和推理基准测试中达到前沿性能水平，同时保持极低的激活参数量（仅9.8B激活/229.9B总参数）。

Conclusion: MiniMax-M2系列成功证明了"小激活"架构可以释放"大智能"，为智能体应用提供了高效且强大的解决方案，M2.7的自我进化能力标志着向自主优化模型迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We introduce the MiniMax-M2 series, a family of Mixture-of-Experts language models built around the principle that mini activations can unleash maximum real-world intelligence. The flagship M2 contains 229.9B total parameters with only 9.8B activated per token. Designed end-to-end for agentic deployment, the M2 series rests on three components: (i) agent-driven data pipelines producing large-scale, verifiable trajectories across agentic coding and agentic cowork, each grounded in an executable workspace and an artifact-aligned reward; (ii) Forge, a scalable agent-native RL system that adapts to long-horizon agent trajectories, paired with windowed-FIFO scheduling, prefix-tree merging, inference optimization, and a clean training-inference-agent decoupling that supports both white-box and black-box agents; (iii) the latest M2.7 checkpoint takes an early step toward self-evolution -- autonomously debugging training runs and modifying its own scaffold. Across M2 through M2.7, this combination translates a mini-activation footprint into frontier-tier performance on agentic coding, deep search, office-task, and reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [179] [Which Changes Matter? Towards Trustworthy Legal AI via Relevance-Sensitive Evaluation and Solver-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26530)
*Chen Linze,Cai Yufan,Hou Zhe,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了法律AI应对法律相关变化敏感、对法律无关变化保持稳定的要求，构建了统一评估套件揭示现有法律大模型的不足，并提出了基于形式推理的对抗性多智能体框架LexGuard来提升法律推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律推理要求区分实质性变化与非实质性变化，现有法律大语言模型在处理法律无关扰动时缺乏稳定性，同时难以准确识别法律相关的实质性变化，这影响了法律AI的可信度和可靠性。

Method: 提出LexGuard框架：1)将成文法形式化为可执行约束；2)使用对抗性智能体提取竞争性事实-法律论点；3)调用SMT求解器验证法律满足性和逻辑一致性。同时构建了涵盖司法公平性、鲁棒性和法规混淆场景的统一评估套件。

Result: 评估显示现有法律大模型系统性地对法律无关变化敏感，且经常无法区分相关法律要素和法定规则。LexGuard实验表明能有效降低对操纵性框架的脆弱性，改善相似法规间的消歧，限制法律无关属性的影响，并在良性重表述下提高一致性。

Conclusion: 法律可信度不仅需要准确性，还需要对法律实质性变化具有校准的敏感性，法律AI应当在法律无关变化下保持稳定，同时对法律相关变化保持敏感。

Abstract: Legal reasoning requires distinguishing changes that matter from those that do not. Legal AI should remain stable under legally irrelevant perturbations, but should change when perturbations alter legally material points. We formulate this requirement as a legal-relevance-sensitive evaluation problem: LLMs should only be sensitive to the legally relevant change. We introduce a unified evaluation suite covering should-change and should-not-change evaluation across judicial fairness, robustness, and statute-confusion scenarios. Our evaluation shows that existing legal LLMs are systematically sensitive to legally irrelevant variations and often fail to distinguish related legal elements and statutory rules. To mitigate these failures, we present LexGuard, an adversarial multi-agent framework grounded in formal reasoning. LexGuard formalizes statutes into executable constraints, uses adversarial agents to extract competing fact-statute arguments, and invokes SMT solvers to verify legal satisfaction and logical consistency. Experiments show that LexGuard improves legal reasoning reliability by reducing vulnerability to manipulative framing, improving disambiguation among similar statutes, limiting the influence of legally irrelevant attributes, and increasing consistency under benign reformulations. We show that legal trustworthiness requires not only accuracy, but calibrated sensitivity to legally material changes.

</details>


### [180] [PolyFusionAgent: A Multimodal Foundation Model and Autonomous AI Assistant for Polymer Property Prediction and Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26543)
*Manpreet Kaur,Xingying Zhang,Qian Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PolyFusionAgent框架，结合多模态聚合物基础模型（PolyFusion）和工具增强的文献驱动设计代理（PolyAgent），实现了交互式、证据链接的聚合物发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合物发现面临化学设计空间巨大、结构-性质-先验知识表示碎片化的挑战，导致AI模型与物理实验现实脱节，限制了其支持直接可操作设计决策的能力。

Method: 开发了PolyFusionAgent交互式框架：1）PolyFusion多模态聚合物基础模型，对齐序列、拓扑、3D几何和指纹等互补视图，学习可迁移的共享潜在空间；2）PolyAgent设计代理，将预测和逆向设计与文献证据检索链接，在统一工作流中提出、评估和验证假设。

Result: PolyFusion在热物理性质预测方面表现提升，能够生成化学有效且结构新颖的聚合物（超出参考设计空间）；PolyAgent通过文献证据检索闭合设计循环，实现了可验证的科学推理。

Conclusion: PolyFusionAgent成功实现了结合大规模表示学习、多模态化学知识和可验证科学推理的交互式聚合物发现，为聚合物设计提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Polymer discovery is central to fields ranging from energy storage to biomedicine, but it is hindered by an astronomically large chemical design space and fragmented representations of structure, properties, and prior knowledge. This fragmentation leaves many AI models disconnected from physical and experimental reality, restricting their ability to support directly actionable design decisions.
