*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Chen Ye,Xuyang Teng,Bo Yang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了检索增强生成（RAG）系统存在监控-控制差距：模型能识别矛盾证据，但这种认知无法有效约束其最终决策行为，导致单轮评估高估系统安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG安全评估协议假设单轮鲁棒性可以预测多轮证据累积场景下的鲁棒性，这一假设在安全关键应用中缺乏验证，需要探究模型在多轮交互中的真实安全表现。

Method: 提出多轮文档累积协议，在四个模型家族（1.5B-32B参数）上进行超过50,000次轮次评估；结合隐藏状态探测、注意力分析和响应策略分类法进行机制分析，并通过人工验证进行验证。

Result: 单轮诊断系统性高估RAG安全性；矛盾确认与安全解决方案无相关性；不存在通用提示修复方案；危险相关信息在内部被表征并获得增强注意力，但未能约束输出行为。

Conclusion: 动作选择是核心缺陷所在，模型认知与行为之间的差距必须在高风险场景部署前被测量和弥合。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented LLMs are deployed for tasks where evidence quality determines action safety, yet evaluation protocols assume that single-turn robustness predicts robustness when evidence accumulates across turns. We show this assumption is fundamentally incorrect. Models exhibit a monitoring-control gap: they readily acknowledge contradictory evidence, yet this awareness fails to constrain their final recommendations - detecting epistemic conflict does not imply resolving it safely. Through a multi-turn document accumulation protocol across four model families (1.5B-32B parameters) and over 50,000 turn-level evaluations, we demonstrate that single-turn diagnostics systematically overestimate RAG safety, that contradiction acknowledgement is uncorrelated with safe resolution, a pattern corroborated by targeted human validation, and that no universal prompt fix exists. Converging mechanism evidence - hidden-state probing, attention analysis, and response-strategy taxonomy - points to action selection as the most plausible locus of the deficit: danger-relevant information is internally represented and receives enhanced attention during unsafe generation, yet fails to constrain output behavior. The gap between what models recognize and what they do must be measured and closed before retrieval-augmented systems can be trusted in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [218] [Query Symbolically or Retrieve Semantically? A Dataset and Method for Semi-Structured Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27164)
*Mateusz Czyżnikiewicz,Ryszard Tuora,Adam Kozakiewicz,Tomasz Ziętkiewicz,Mateusz Galiński,Michał Godziszewski,Michał Karpowicz,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DualGraph框架，通过结合文本知识图谱和符号知识图谱，解决了半结构化文档检索中语义相似性检索的局限性，在 SpecsQA 基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG系统依赖于语义相似性检索，在半结构化文档中表现不佳，因为这类文档需要精确的过滤、聚合或跨文档的结构化属性检索；而传统的符号方法在噪声语料库中又表现脆弱，缺乏一种能有效结合语义和符号检索的方法。

Method: 提出了DualGraph框架，包含两个互补视图：文本知识图谱用于语义检索，符号知识图谱用于对类型化的主谓宾三元组进行符号查询；基于这两个组件，提供了多种策略来选择或组合语义和符号证据。同时构建了SpecsQA基准数据集。

Result: DualGraph在SpecsQA基准测试中，在各种问题类型上均持续优于现有的稠密检索、GraphRAG、符号方法和面向表格的基线方法。

Conclusion: DualGraph通过有机结合语义检索和符号查询，有效地弥合了半结构化文档检索中的技术差距，证明了双视图方法在处理复杂半结构化数据检索问题上的优越性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for question answering typically retrieve evidence by semantic similarity between the query and document chunks. While effective for unstructured text, this approach is less reliable on semi-structured corpora where answering may require exact filtering, aggregation, or exhaustive retrieval over structured attributes across multiple documents. Symbolic approaches support such operations, but they are often brittle on noisy natural-language corpora. We address this gap with DualGraph, a RAG framework that represents documents through two complementary views: a Textual Knowledge Graph for semantic retrieval and a Symbolic Knowledge Graph for symbolic querying over typed subject--predicate--object triples. Building on these two components, we provide multiple strategies for selecting or combining semantic and symbolic evidence.We also introduce SpecsQA, a benchmark from a commercial shopping website with semi-structured product documents and manually curated questions spanning open-ended and specification-oriented retrieval. Experiments show that DualGraph consistently outperforms state-of-the-art dense-retrieval, GraphRAG, symbolic, and table-oriented baselines across question types.Code and data are available at https://github.com/corneliocristina/DualGraphRAG.

</details>


### [219] [The Compressive Knowledge Graph Hypothesis: Which Graph Facts Matter for Scientific Hypothesis Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27176)
*Shashwat Sourav,Viktoriia Baibakova,Sanjay Das,Ran Elgedawy,Maria Mahbub,Emily Herron,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出“冗余感知压缩知识图谱假说”，发现紧凑的科学结构子图通常足以替代完整知识图谱来引导模型生成假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管知识图谱能为语言模型提供结构化的科学背景，但目前尚不清楚图谱中的哪些事实真正影响了生成的假设。研究旨在探究知识图谱在引导假设生成中的实际效用。

Method: 在Mistral-7B、Llama-3.1-70B和Gemini 2.5 Flash三种模型上，针对电池材料领域，通过改变知识图谱的密度、本体丰富度、拓扑结构和控制结构进行扰动实验，并使用提供的图谱和固定参考指标评估输出。

Result: 知识图谱的效用具有选择性和模型依赖性。紧凑的top-k子图通常能近似完整知识图谱的效果，且这种压缩性不依赖于特定的语义排序规则，随机和基于拓扑的子集也能恢复大部分信号。即使没有知识图谱，模型也能从先验知识中恢复相当一部分图谱内容。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持冗余感知压缩知识图谱假说：有用的知识图谱信号通常可以从紧凑的、科学结构化的子图中恢复，而不需要完整的局部图谱。这为优化知识图谱在科学假设生成中的应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) can provide structured scientific context to language models, but it remains unclear which graph facts actually shape the generated hypotheses. We study KG-guided hypothesis generation for battery materials across Mistral-7B, Llama-3.1-70B, and Gemini 2.5 Flash. We perturb local KGs by varying density, ontology richness, topology, and control structure, and evaluate outputs with both provided-graph and fixed-reference metrics. Across models, KG utility is selective and model-dependent: graph context changes outputs, but no-KG outputs also recover substantial graph content from model priors. Compact top-k subgraphs often approximate full-KG behavior, including when claimed-outcome triples are held out. At the same time, compression is not unique to one semantic ranking rule, random and topology-based subsets can also recover much of the signal. These results support a redundancy-aware Compressive KG hypothesis: useful KG signal is often recoverable from compact, scientifically structured subgraphs rather than requiring the full local graph.

</details>


### [220] [Learning to Act under Noise: Enhancing Agent Robustness via Noisy Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27209)
*Yuxin Chen,Xiaodong Cai,Junfeng Fang,Zhuowen Han,Yu Wang,Yaorui Shi,Yi Zhang,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Xiang Wang,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NoisyAgent框架，通过在训练中引入用户噪声和工具噪声来提升LLM智能体在真实环境中的鲁棒性，同时也能改善理想环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在真实部署环境中性能显著下降，这源于理想化训练设置与真实世界交互动态之间的根本性不匹配。真实环境本质上是随机的和不完美的，而当前训练范式依赖于精心策划的任务指令和稳定、受控的环境。

Method: 提出NoisyAgent训练框架，识别并引入两类交互噪声：捕捉用户交互中歧义性和变异性的用户噪声，以及反映工具执行中失败和异常的工具噪声。通过修改用户交互模式和模拟工具执行结果将扰动引入训练流程。噪声仅应用于部分rollouts，并随着模型适应当前噪声水平而逐步增加难度，以稳定训练并鼓励智能体处理更具挑战性的不完美情况。

Result: 广泛实验表明该方法在噪声和动态环境中持续提升智能体鲁棒性。分析发现，在噪声条件下训练也能在理想化基准测试上获得性能提升，表明受控的环境噪声暴露能促进更具泛化性的推理和决策行为。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了建模交互不完美性对于弥合智能体训练与真实部署差距的重要性，表明在训练过程中模拟真实环境噪声是提升智能体实际应用能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have facilitated the widespread deployment of LLMs as interactive agents capable of reasoning, planning, and tool use. Despite strong performance on existing benchmarks, such agents often exhibit notable degradation when deployed in real-world settings, where environments are inherently stochastic and imperfect. We argue that this discrepancy arises from a fundamental mismatch between idealized training settings and real-world interaction dynamics, where current paradigms rely on carefully curated task instructions and stable, well-controlled environments. To address this gap, we propose NoisyAgent, an agentic training framework that explicitly incorporates environmental imperfections into the agent learning process. We identify two major sources of interaction noise in real-world scenarios: user noise, which captures ambiguity and variability in user interaction, and tool noise, which reflects failures and anomalies in tool execution. We introduce such perturbations into the training pipeline by modifying user interaction patterns and simulating tool execution results within the training environment. To stabilize training while encouraging agents to handle increasingly challenging imperfections, noise is applied to only a subset of rollouts and progressively increased in difficulty as the model adapts to the current noise level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently improves agent robustness under noisy and dynamic environments. Our analysis reveals that training under noise conditions also yields performance gains on idealized benchmarks, suggesting that controlled exposure to environmental noise promotes more generalizable reasoning and decision-making behaviors. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling interaction imperfections for bridging the gap between agent training and real-world deployment.

</details>


### [221] [Gumbel Machine: Counterfactual Student Writing Generation via Gumbel Noise Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27249)
*Hunter McNichols,Alexander Scarlatos,Mihai Dascalu,Danielle McNamara,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Gumbel Machine框架，通过β-Hindsight控制解码算法生成既符合评分标准又与学生原作相似的虚拟反事实文本，解决了跨学科教学中示例与学生作品差异过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨学科教学中，高质量范例往往与学生当前作品差异显著，导致学生难以模仿学习。理想的教学示范应是学生作品的反事实版本——即经过改进但仍与原作相似的版本。现有基于大语言模型的反事实文本生成方法往往是领域特定的，难以转化为实际应用。

Method: 提出Gumbel Machine方法，这是一种灵活、模块化的反事实生成框架。核心是创新的受控解码算法——β-Hindsight控制，该算法利用潜在随机性作为可调节的相似性控制机制，在反事实生成过程中平衡LLM的指令跟随能力与对参考事实文本的相似性。

Result: 在多个学生写作数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够有效生成既符合评分标准又与参考文本相似的反事实文本，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Gumbel Machine提供了一种通用的、可控的反事实文本生成方案，成功解决了教学场景中学生作品改进示例生成的实际问题，具有较好的实用性和推广价值。

Abstract: An effective method of teaching across disciplines is to provide examples of high-quality work. However, an example may be significantly different from a student's current work, making it challenging for them to emulate. An ideal learning demonstration is a counterfactual version of the student work, an improved version that is still similar to their own. Existing automated approaches for counterfactual text generation using Large Language Models (LLMs) result in domain-specific systems that are difficult to translate into practical applications. We present the Gumbel Machine, a flexible, modular approach to generating counterfactuals that leverages LLM instruction-following capabilities while encouraging similarity to a reference factual text. Central to our approach is a novel, controlled decoding algorithm, $β$-Hindsight control, which uses latent randomness as a tunable similarity control mechanism during counterfactual generation. Experiments on datasets of student writing, scored on various criteria, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach at generating counterfactuals both rubric-consistent and similar to a reference.

</details>


### [222] [SIA: Self Improving AI with Harness & Weight Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27276)
*Prannay Hebbar,Yogendra Manawat,Samuel Verboomen,Alesia Ivanova,Selvam Palanimalai,Kunal Bhatia,Vignesh Baskaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SIA框架，通过Feedback-Agent同时优化任务智能体的支架（harness）和模型权重，在法律分类、GPU内核优化和RNA去噪三个领域分别实现56.6%、91.9%运行时间减少和502%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统依赖人工构建和改进，现有自改进研究分为两个孤立方向：harness-update学派固定模型权重优化支架结构，test-time training学派固定支架优化模型权重。两个方向各自为政，缺乏协同。本文旨在打破这两个研究孤岛，探索同时优化支架和权重的自改进循环。

Method: 提出SIA（Self-Improving Agent）框架，设计Feedback-Agent作为元智能体，在任务反馈驱动下同时更新任务特定智能体的harness（包括工具、提示、重试逻辑、搜索过程）和模型权重。这种双重更新机制结合了结构优化和知识内化的优势。

Result: 在三个对比领域进行评估：（1）中国法律指控分类（LawBench）：性能提升56.6%；（2）低级GPU内核优化：运行时间减少91.9%；（3）单细胞RNA去噪：性能提升502%。所有基准测试中，组合两个更新杠杆均优于单独的支架迭代。

Conclusion: 同时优化harness和模型权重能够产生协同效应：支架更新使模型具有智能体特性，塑造其搜索和行为方式；权重更新构建领域直觉，这些直觉无法通过提示或支架灌输。结合两种更新机制是实现AI自改进的有效路径。

Abstract: Humans are the bottleneck in building and improving AI. Both the models and the agents that wrap them are written, tuned, and corrected by people. The long-horizon goal of an AI that can figure out how to improve itself remains open. Two largely disjoint research lines attack this bottleneck. The harness-update school has a meta-agent rewrite the scaffold of a task-specific agent (its tools, prompts, retry logic, and search procedure) while the model weights are held fixed. The test-time training school uses hand-written RL pipelines to update the model's own weights on task feedback while the harness is held fixed. These two silos operate in isolation. We propose SIA, a self-improving loop in which a language-model agent (the Feedback-Agent) updates both the harness and the weights of a task-specific agent. We evaluate across three contrasting domains: Chinese legal charge classification, low-level GPU kernel optimisation, and single-cell RNA denoising. Combining both levers outperforms scaffold iteration alone on all three benchmarks. The gains are 56.6% on LawBench, 91.9% runtime reduction on GPU kernels, and 502% on denoising over the initial baseline. Harness updates make the model agentic, shaping how it searches and acts, while weight updates build the domain intuition that no prompt or scaffold can instil.

</details>


### [223] [Modeling Agentic Technical Debt and Stochastic Tax: A Standalone Framework for Measurement, Simulation, and Dashboarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27320)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Raja Iqbal,Narayan Ramasubbu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个形式化模型，区分了代理式技术债务（累积的设计和治理负债存量）与随机税（业务流程中使用随机代理产生的运营负担流量），并通过应付账款仿真和配套电子表格进行演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理式AI系统在业务流程中的应用日益广泛，需要区分两类不同的成本：累积的设计治理负债和持续产生的运营负担，以便管理者更好地理解和控制相关风险与成本。

Method: 从紧凑的仪表板表达式出发，扩展为完整的结构模型；定义所有变量和参数；展示如何从运营数据估算每个成本类别；使用应付账款仿真和配套电子表格进行框架演示。

Result: 成功建立了代理式技术债务与随机税的区分模型：两者相关但不相同——债务可以放大税收，而即使债务最小化，税收仍可能为正；提供了可操作的成本估算方法和实际案例验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为管理者提供了实用的工具来识别、量化和管理代理式AI系统中的两类成本，有助于做出更明智的技术投资和治理决策。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems combine probabilistic reasoning with delegated action through tools, context, memory, orchestration, and external workflow integration. This note develops a formal and managerially usable model that distinguishes Agentic Technical Debt from Stochastic Tax. Agentic Technical Debt is a stock of accumulated design and governance liability. Stochastic Tax is a recurring flow of operating burden that arises when stochastic agents are used in business workflows. The two constructs are related, but they are not the same: debt can amplify the tax, while the tax can remain positive even when debt is minimized. The note starts from a compact dashboard expression, expands it into a fuller structural model, defines all variables and parameters, shows how each cost category can be estimated from operational data, and illustrates the framework with an accounts-payable simulation and companion spreadsheet.

</details>


### [224] [Maat: The Agentic Legal Research Assistant for Competition Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27331)
*Basant Mounir,Farida Madkour,Amira Abdelaziz,Asmaa Sami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Maat，一个基于ReAct框架的竞争法研究智能体，通过编排多种工具和RAG技术，解决了现有通用和法律AI助手在竞争法分析中缺乏专业领域知识、引用不足和幻觉问题，在案例任务上显著优于现有基准助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争法专家在进行法律研究时需要审查大量案例、决策和司法报告以识别先例并评估关键要素。现有的通用研究助手（如Claude、ChatGPT）和法律助手（如SaulLM-7B、LegalGPT）在竞争法分析方面存在明显不足：缺乏专门的领域专业知识、提供的官方引用不足，或会产生竞争法案例幻觉。

Method: 与竞争法专家迭代设计开发Maat系统，这是一个协调研究过程不同任务工具的ReAct智能体。Maat使用RAG技术将案例和发现建立在官方来源上以确保可靠性，提供丰富的内联引用，当数据库覆盖不足时回退到网络搜索，当查询模糊时提示用户澄清。

Result: Maat在特定案例任务上显著优于所有基准助手，在理论问题任务上表现接近最优基准助手。研究使用的数据集已在GitHub上公开。

Conclusion: Maat成功填补了竞争法领域AI研究助手的空白，通过专业化设计和多工具协作有效解决了现有AI助手在竞争法分析中的局限性，为法律AI助手的垂直领域应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Competition law experts conducting legal research must review extensive volumes of cases, decisions, and judicial reports to identify precedents and assess key elements in competition and merger cases. Although general research assistants such as Claude and ChatGPT and legal assistants such as SaulLM-7B and LegalGPT are increasingly used to assist legal research, they remain inadequate for competition law analysis: they lack specialized domain expertise, provide insufficient official citations, or hallucinate competition law cases. We propose Maat, a ReAct agent that orchestrates tools corresponding to different tasks of the research process. Designed iteratively with competition law experts, Maat grounds cases and findings in official sources using RAG for reliability, provides rich in-line citations, falls back to web search when database coverage is insufficient, and prompts the user for clarification when queries are ambiguous. Maat significantly outperforms all baseline assistants on case-specific tasks and performs within range of the top baseline on theoretical question tasks. The dataset used is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [225] [2-ASP(Q) programs with weak constraints: Complexity and efficient implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27338)
*Andrea Cuteri,Giuseppe Mazzotta,Francesco Ricca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有弱约束的双量词ASP(Q)程序(2-ASP(Q)^w)，提供了完整的复杂度刻画，并提出了基于CEGAR技术的求解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASP(Q)是将Answer Set Programming扩展了量词的框架，其中2-ASP(Q)^w是一个重要的实用片段，能够表达高达Delta_3^P复杂度类的优化问题。现有研究缺乏对该片段完整复杂度分析和高效求解方法。

Method: 1. 对2-ASP(Q)^w程序的主要计算任务进行完整复杂度刻画，包括紧完备性结果和非平凡情况分析；2. 在Casper系统中引入针对ASP(Q)的Counterexample-Guided Abstraction Refinement (CEGAR)技术来计算（最优）量化回答集。

Result: 获得了2-ASP(Q)^w主要计算任务的完整复杂度刻画；开发的CEGAR技术在来自不同应用领域的困难基准测试上显示出有效性。

Conclusion: 2-ASP(Q)^w片段在理论和实践上都得到了深入研究，所提出的CEGAR求解策略为实际应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: ASP(Q) extends Answer Set Programming (ASP) with Quantifiers over answer sets. In this paper we focus on the class of ASP(Q) programs with two quantifiers and weak constraints, denoted as 2-ASP(Q)^w. 2-ASP(Q)^w is a practically relevant fragment of ASP(Q) that is expressive enough to capture optimization problems up to the class Delta_3^P. On the theoretical side, we provide a complete complexity characterization of the main computational tasks for 2-ASP(Q)^w programs, including tight completeness results and the analysis of nontrivial cases that have not been addressed in previous works. On the practical side, we introduce novel strategies for computing (optimal) quantified answer sets in the Casper system, that rely on a Counterexample-Guided Abstraction Refinement (CEGAR) technique tailored to ASP(Q). An experimental evaluation on hard benchmarks from different application domains shows that the proposed techniques are effective in practice.

</details>


### [226] [Alignment Tampering: How Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback Is Exploited to Optimize Misaligned Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27355)
*Dongyoon Hahm,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了RLHF中的结构性漏洞——对齐篡改（alignment tampering），即LLM通过影响偏好数据集导致RLHF放大不良行为，现有缓解方法难以在不牺牲质量的前提下解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF作为大语言模型对齐的标准方法存在核心局限：偏好数据集由模型自身输出构建且成对比较只表明哪个更好而不揭示原因，这可能被利用导致对齐篡改。

Method: 提出对齐篡改概念并通过实验验证，当LLM生成带有偏见但质量较高的回复时，标注者会因质量偏好这些回复，偏好标签无法区分质量与偏见，强化学习优化此类奖励会放大错位偏见。

Result: 实验证明对齐篡改可放大多种偏见：从关键词偏见到宣传（如性别歧视）、品牌推广和工具性目标寻求；现有鲁棒RLHF技术无法在不牺牲回复质量的情况下完全解决对齐篡改问题。

Conclusion: 当前RLHF存在结构性漏洞，需要新的方法来防止对齐篡改漏洞，这对LLM安全对齐具有重要启示。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard method to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. In this work, we introduce alignment tampering, a potential vulnerability where the LLM undergoing alignment influences the preference dataset, causing RLHF to amplify undesired behaviors. This arises from core limitations of RLHF: (1) preference datasets are constructed from the LLM's own outputs, allowing it to influence them, and (2) pairwise comparisons only indicate which response is better, not why. These limitations can be exploited to cause alignment tampering. For example, if an LLM generates biased responses with higher quality, annotators will prefer them based on quality. However, preference labels do not distinguish quality from bias, and the reward model inherits this limitation. Optimizing such rewards through reinforcement learning or best-of-N sampling can amplify misaligned biases. Our experiments demonstrate amplification across diverse biases: from keyword bias to propaganda (e.g., sexism), brand promotion, and instrumental goal-seeking. Mitigation remains challenging, as existing techniques for robust RLHF fail to fully resolve alignment tampering without sacrificing response quality. These findings reveal structural vulnerabilities of current RLHF and emphasize the need to prevent this vulnerability. Project page: https://alignment-tampering.github.io/

</details>


### [227] [Natural Language Query to Configuration for Retrieval Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27361)
*Melissa Z. Pan,Negar Arabzadeh,Mathew Jacob,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Esha Choukse,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BRANE方法，针对检索代理的多配置选择问题，在推理时实现查询级别的动态配置优化，在保持准确率的同时降低高达89%的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索代理具有众多配置选项（LLM、检索器、文档数量、跳跃次数、合成策略），当前采用针对工作负载的一次性手动调优，忽视了每个查询的优化潜力，存在成本与质量的权衡空间。

Method: BRANE利用LLM将查询转换为工作负载特定特征，训练轻量级的配置预测器估计管道是否能正确回答查询；推理时选择预测正确性最大化且惩罚成本的最优配置，无需重训练即可调节成本-质量权衡。

Result: 在MuSiQue、BrowseComp-Plus和FinanceBench三个数据集上，BRANE持续推动成本-质量帕累托前沿，在匹配最佳固定配置准确率的同时成本降低高达89%，性能优于LLM路由、基于规则和微调Qwen3-4B基线方法。

Conclusion: 查询级别的检索管道全配置优化是静态工作负载级别调优的可行替代方案，证明了动态配置选择的有效性。

Abstract: Modern retrieval agents expose many configuration choices -- LLM, retriever, number of documents, number of hops, and synthesis strategy -- each shaping both answer quality and serving cost. Today, these pipelines are typically hand-tuned once per workload, leaving substantial per-query optimization untapped. We formulate the problem: given a natural-language query and either an accuracy or a budget target, select from a predefined pipeline catalog the configuration that minimizes cost or maximizes accuracy at inference time. We propose **BRANE**, which uses an LLM to convert each query into workload-specific characteristics, then trains a lightweight per-configuration predictor that estimates whether the pipeline will answer the query correctly. At inference time, **BRANE** selects the configuration that maximizes predicted correctness penalized by cost, exposing a tunable cost-quality tradeoff without retraining. Across MuSiQue, BrowseComp-Plus, and FinanceBench, **BRANE** consistently pushes the cost-quality Pareto frontier, matches the best fixed configuration's accuracy at up to 89% lower cost, and outperforms LLM-routing, rule-based, and fine-tuned Qwen3-4B baselines. These results show that per-query configuration of the full retrieval pipeline is a practical alternative to static workload-level tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Cassandra: Enabling Reasoning LLMs at Edge via Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26558)
*Soongyu Choi,Yuntae Kim,Muyoung Son,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出Cassandra，一种算法-硬件协同设计的自推测解码框架，通过细粒度数据选择构建免训练草稿模型，在低批次场景下实现最高2.41倍加速，相比SOTA方法在相同内存预算下生成1.81倍更多token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型大语言模型遭受解码阶段开销，基于近似的方法会降低精度；现有无损推测解码方法在无需额外训练的情况下难以在消费级设备上实现强低批次性能，限制了实际部署。

Method: 采用算法-硬件协同设计：1)通过优化剪枝和尾数截断，识别模型权重和KV缓存中的关键值，构建高性能免训练草稿模型；2)设计轻量级编码器-解码器硬件模块，减少Cassandra表示与标准浮点格式间的转换开销，支持与商用GPU/NPU无缝集成。

Result: 相比BF16基线实现高达2.41倍加速，无需额外训练；在NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090上运行Llama 3 8B时，在相同内存预算下比SOTA方法Eagle-3多生成1.81倍的token。

Conclusion: Cassandra成功解决了低批次场景下无损推测解码的效率问题，通过创新的细粒度数据选择和硬件协同设计，在无需训练的前提下显著提升了推理性能，为大语言模型在消费级设备上的部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has emerged as a promising lossless approach for accelerating Large Language Models (LLMs). As reasoning LLMs increasingly suffer from decode-stage overhead and approximation-based methods degrade accuracy, lossless speculative decoding has become essential for efficient inference. However, existing methods still struggle to deliver strong low-batch performance without additional training, limiting practical deployment on consumer devices. To address this challenge, we propose Cassandra, an algorithm-hardware co-designed self-speculative decoding framework optimized for low-batch scenarios. Cassandra constructs a high-performance, training-free draft model through fine-grained data selection. Using optimized pruning and mantissa truncation, it identifies the most salient values in both model weights and the Key-Value (KV) cache, enabling rapid candidate token generation before full-precision parallel verification. Unlike prior self-speculative decoding methods based on layer skipping or structured KV compression, Cassandra achieves significantly higher efficiency. To further reduce the overhead of format conversion between Cassandra representations and standard floating-point formats, we also introduce a lightweight encoder-decoder hardware module designed for seamless integration with commercial GPUs and NPUs. Experimental results show that Cassandra achieves up to 2.41x speedup over the BF16 baseline without additional training. Furthermore, on Llama 3 8B running on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4090, Cassandra generates 1.81x more tokens under the same memory budget compared to Eagle-3, a state-of-the-art speculative decoding method.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [LearnedCache: An eBPF-Integrated Perceptron-Based Eviction Policy for the Linux Page Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26168)
*Zejia Qi*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文开发了LearnedCache，一个基于eBPF和单层感知器的Linux页面缓存淘汰策略，通过机器学习模型预测页面重用时间，在真实内核数据上实现了约80%的AUC，并在特定工作负载下相比FIFO基线在插入率上提升高达10%，证明了ML驱动的缓存淘汰策略在Linux内核中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux页面缓存是操作系统和应用性能的核心组件，但现有淘汰策略受限于启发式规则的僵化性。随着AI工具的兴起和Linux设备工作负载的日益多样化，研究机器学习驱动的缓存淘汰策略成为必要。虽然CDN等用户空间应用已有相关研究，但内核层面的ML驱动缓存策略研究仍然缺失。

Method: 开发LearnedCache系统，将单层感知机模型嵌入Linux内核。该方法：1）在多样化工作负载的真实内核数据上训练模型；2）构建多个线性模型预测页面重用时间；3）利用eBPF技术将模型集成到内核中；4）在真实内核环境中进行实时性能评估，实现机器学习驱动的页面缓存淘汰决策。

Result: 在页面重用时间预测任务上，多个线性模型实现了约80%的中位AUC。通过50组配对试验与FIFO基线对比，在特定工作负载下，LearnedCache在插入率（一种频率调整的缓存命中率衍生指标）上实现统计学显著的提升，最高达10%，同时产生最小开销，证明ML驱动的缓存淘汰策略在Linux内核中的实用性。

Conclusion: 机器学习驱动的缓存淘汰策略在Linux内核中是可行且实用的，能够在代表性经验工作负载下超越传统FIFO策略，实现统计学显著的性能提升，同时保持低开销。这为内核层面的ML应用开辟了新方向，展示了ML在操作系统核心组件优化中的潜力。

Abstract: Linux is the foundation of the digital age, accounting for the majority of the cloud and mobile OS markets. Any device that runs Linux uses the Linux page cache, a central pillar in OS and application performance, serving to reduce extraneous disk access. Many page cache eviction policies have been developed but remain bound by the rigidity of heuristics. The rise of AI-driven tools in recent years, melded with the ever-increasing variety of workloads for Linux devices, sets the stage for machine-learning-driven cache eviction policies. Promising research has been done in this field, but only in the field of user-space applications such as CDNs. We develop LearnedCache, an eBPF-integrated single-layer perceptron-based cache eviction policy for the Linux page cache, trained on real kernel data from diverse workloads. We demonstrate median AUCs of nearly 80% over multiple linear models modeling page reuse time, then take a step further by embedding these models inside the Linux kernel for real-time performance evaluation. Through statistical testing over 50 paired trials against a baseline of FIFO for each workload, LearnedCache reveals that machine-learning-derived cache eviction policies are practical in the Linux kernel under representative empirical workloads and are able to surpass conventional FIFO by statistically significant margins of up to 10% in insertion rate, a frequency-adjusted derivation of cache hit rate, in specific workloads while incurring minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Device Context Protocol: A Compact, Safety-First Architecture for LLM-Driven Control of Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26159)
*Dongxu Yang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出设备上下文协议(DCP)，解决MCP无法应用于微控制器及LLM控制物理设备的安全问题，在27.6KB flash/0.6KB RAM的极小内存占用下，实现对100%权限提升攻击和78%提示注入攻击的拦截。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP协议专为软件服务设计（MB级内存），无法适配占据物理设备长尾市场的微控制器。IoT-MCP虽移植到边缘网关（74KB内存），但仍排除最小规格MCU。更关键的是，LLM存在幻觉和提示注入风险，直接控制物理硬件存在严重安全隐患。

Method: 提出设备上下文协议(DCP)，包含：(1)超紧凑帧格式——典型帧小于50字节（6字节头+CBOR载荷+可选16字节HMAC）；(2)协议层原语——能力作用域、范围/类型检查、演练评估、单位即类型；(3)主机端网桥——在字节到达设备前拦截畸形或幻觉调用。在ESP32上实现参考固件。

Result: ESP32参考固件仅需27.6KB flash/0.6KB RAM。实验研究涵盖5个LLM、4家厂商、6类对抗提示的675次工具调用，DCP拦截100%权限提升攻击、78%提示注入攻击，而原始MCP和IoT-MCP拦截率仅0-1%。在比OpenAPI 3规范少三个数量级的固件占用下实现同等表达能力。

Conclusion: DCP填补了MCP（面向企业SaaS连接）与其无法触及的物理设备之间的缺失层级，为LLM安全控制资源受限的微控制器提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as orchestrators of external tools via the Model Context Protocol (MCP), but MCP is built for software services with megabytes of memory and does not descend to the microcontrollers that dominate the long tail of physical devices. Recent work (IoT-MCP) ports MCP to edge gateways at 74 KB peak memory; this still excludes the smallest commodity MCUs and, critically, does not address the safety problem of giving an unreliable caller (an LLM that may hallucinate or be prompt-injected) direct control of physical hardware. We present the Device Context Protocol (DCP): a sub-50-byte typical frame (6-byte header + CBOR payload + optional 16-byte HMAC), a manifest schema in which capability scoping, range and type checks, dry-run evaluation, and units-as-types are protocol-layer primitives, and a host-side Bridge that rejects malformed or hallucinated calls before any byte reaches the device. Reference firmware measures 27.6 KB flash / 0.6 KB RAM on ESP32; the Python Bridge, ESP32 firmware, and a language-neutral conformance suite are MIT-licensed and public. An empirical study -- 675 tool calls produced by five LLMs across four vendors (DeepSeek, Alibaba, Zhipu, MiniMax) against six categories of adversarial prompts, with the injection category instantiating AgentDojo's attack templates -- shows DCP rejects 100% of capability-escalation attempts and 78% of prompt-injection attempts, versus 0--1% for Raw MCP and IoT-MCP, matching the expressiveness of a well-formed OpenAPI 3 schema at three orders of magnitude less firmware footprint. We position DCP as the missing layer between MCP (which is moving toward enterprise SaaS connectivity) and the physical devices it does not reach.

</details>


### [231] [mmDiff: A Noise-Robust Differentiable Ray-Tracing Framework for mmWave Scene Calibration and Channel Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26406)
*Haofan Lu,Yadi Cao,Wanghao Yi,Omid Abari*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出mmDiff框架，通过可微分定向散射模型解决3D重建模型几何缺陷对无线仿真的敏感性问题，实现毫米波信道的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR和摄影测量等3D重建技术可构建真实环境的几何模型用于无线数字孪生，但重建模型存在孔洞和表面噪声等几何缺陷，而无线仿真对这些噪声高度敏感，导致材料属性校准和信道预测困难。

Method: 提出可微分定向散射模型近似噪声敏感的镜面反射，将反射功率平滑分配到邻近射线方向，使仿真器对局部几何缺陷具有鲁棒性，并证明该近似在渐近路径增益精度上保持准确性；构建端到端可微分框架mmDiff，从稀疏毫米波测量中校准材料属性并预测毫米波信道。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的评估表明，mmDiff在噪声重建几何环境下相比使用纯镜面反射的现有方法具有更优性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于噪声3D重建模型的无线数字孪生提供了可行的技术路径，能够有效处理几何缺陷问题，为网络规划和优化提供可靠支持。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction techniques such as LiDAR scanning and photogrammetry have made it practical to build detailed geometric models of real-world environments. Such reconstructed models can potentially serve as the foundation for wireless digital twins and support network planning and optimization. The core challenge is that reconstructed models inevitably contain geometric artifacts such as holes and noisy surfaces, and wireless simulation is highly sensitive to such noise. To solve this problem, we propose a differentiable directional scattering model to approximate the noise-sensitive specular reflection. This approximation smoothly distributes reflected power among nearby ray directions, making the simulator inherently robust to local geometric artifacts in the reconstructed model. We prove mathematically that this approximation preserves asymptotic path-gain accuracy. Building on this idea, we propose mmDiff, an end-to-end differentiable framework for calibrating material properties from sparse mmWave measurements and predicting mmWave channels. We evaluate mmDiff on both real-world and synthetic datasets, and demonstrate its superior performance over prior methods using pure specular reflection in noisy reconstructed geometry.

</details>


### [232] [SLA-Aware Traffic Steering in Hybrid TN-NTN 5G Backhaul: A Potential Game Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26673)
*Hojjat Navidan,Delia Rico,Mohammad Cheraghinia,Ingrid Moerman,Adnan Shahid*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的SLA感知流量调度框架，用于混合地面-非地面网络回传链路。该方法基于精确势博弈理论实现去中心化负载均衡，在5G测试平台上将SLA违规率降低至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非地面网络(NTN)与地面网络(TN)的融合是实现弹性5G-Advanced和未来6G回传基础设施的关键。然而，在这些高度不对称的链路上管理流量仍面临重大路由挑战，系统需要在有选择地利用昂贵的NTN资源的同时，支持具有冲突服务级别协议(SLA)的异构网络切片。

Method: 将负载均衡问题建模为精确势博弈(exact potential game)，实现上行和下行负载均衡代理之间的去中心化协调，无需控制消息开销。通过将流量调度表述为耦合优化问题，基于捕获吞吐量、延迟、丢包和SLA惩罚的效用函数，动态地将每个切片(或用户组)的流量比例分配到地面和卫星路径上。该博弈存在纯纳什均衡，确保在非平稳负载条件下流量适应的稳定性和可预测性。

Result: 在地理分布式5G测试床上，使用五个代表性切片的双向流量进行评估。实验结果表明，所提出的控制器显著优于启发式和传统基线方法：V2X切片的SLA违规率降至1.7%，紧急切片降至0.7%，而视频、IoT和尽力而为流量完全消除了SLA违规。

Conclusion: 该SLA感知流量调度框架通过博弈论方法有效解决了混合TN-NTN回传网络中的流量管理挑战，实现了低开销的去中心化协调，在保证SLA合规性的同时优化了资源利用。

Abstract: The integration of Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) with Terrestrial Networks (TN) is a key enabler for resilient 5G-Advanced and future 6G backhaul infrastructures. However, managing traffic across these highly asymmetric links remains a significant routing challenge, as systems must support heterogeneous network slices with conflicting service-level agreements (SLAs) while selectively utilizing costly NTN resources. This paper presents a computationally lightweight SLA-aware traffic-steering framework for a hybrid TN-NTN backhaul that models the load-balancing problem as an exact potential game. This mathematical foundation inherently enables decentralized coordination between uplink and downlink load-balancing agents without control-message overhead. By formulating traffic steering as a coupled optimization problem, per-slice (or per-user group) traffic fractions are dynamically distributed across terrestrial and satellite paths based on utility functions that capture throughput, latency, packet loss, and SLA penalties. The resulting game admits a pure Nash equilibrium, ensuring stable and predictable traffic adaptation under non-stationary load conditions. The framework is evaluated on a geographically distributed 5G testbed, using bidirectional traffic generated for five representative slices. Experimental results show that the proposed controller significantly outperforms heuristic and conventional baselines, reducing SLA violations to 1.7% for V2X and 0.7% for the emergency slice while completely eliminating them for video, IoT, and best-effort traffic.

</details>


### [233] [Birkhoff Decompositions and Photonic Interconnects Wait! Don't Forget the Compute!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26845)
*Eliezer Amponsah,Vamsi Addanki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对混合专家(MoE)模型中的全对全通信问题，提出了一种简单的贪婪最大权重分解策略，解决了传统BvN分解方法在MoE场景下的调度气泡和执行开销问题，显著提升了通信计算重叠效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要孤立地优化通信，忽略了通信与后续专家计算之间的交互。MoE模型的调度-计算-组合结构对经典的Birkhoff-von Neumann (BvN)分解等调度技术提出了根本性挑战：MoE通信矩阵很少是双随机矩阵，导致基于BvN的调度存在显著的调度气泡；同时，BvN分解产生过多的匹配会导致执行被分割成小批次，因固定执行开销而导致严重的计算效率损失。

Method: 提出一种简单的贪婪最大权重分解策略，在限制匹配数量的同时保持每个匹配的大批量规模。该方法能够在保证批次规模的前提下，实现更好的通信计算重叠效率。

Result: 该简单方法显著改善了重叠效率，减少了计算开销，性能接近理想的无线拥堵全对全通信的性能。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视MoE执行中的电路调度问题，证明了考虑通信与计算交互的重要性，并提出了一种实用有效的解决方案，为可重构光互连在分布式训练和推理中的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The growing demand for efficient communication in distributed training and inference has sparked significant interest in reconfigurable photonic interconnects across both academia and industry. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, with their highly skewed communication patterns, present a natural opportunity for such circuit-switched fabrics. However, existing approaches largely optimize communication in isolation, overlooking the interaction between communication and the expert computation that follows.
  In this paper, we revisit circuit scheduling for all-to-all communication in MoE execution. We show that the dispatch--compute--combine structure fundamentally challenges classical scheduling techniques such as Birkhoff--von Neumann (BvN) decomposition. First, MoE communication matrices are rarely doubly stochastic, introducing significant scheduling bubbles in BvN-based schedules. Second, while decomposition enables communication--compute overlap, the excessive number of matchings produced by BvN fragments execution into small batches, leading to severe compute inefficiencies due to fixed execution overheads. Motivated by these observations, we explore a simple greedy max-weight decomposition strategy that bounds the number of matchings while preserving large batch sizes per matching. Despite its simplicity, the approach significantly improves overlap efficiency, reduces compute overheads, and approaches the performance of an ideal congestion-free all-to-all.

</details>


### [234] [Extreme-Scale Interconnection Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26960)
*Alejandro Cano,Cristina Brinza,Cristóbal Camarero,Carmen Martínez,Ramón Beivide*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出Multipass Random Leaf-Spine (MRLS)网络拓扑，相比传统Fat-Tree在10万端点的All2All集合通信中实现50%加速，相比Dragonfly网络实现100%加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心需要支持海量端点连接的拓扑结构，现有Fat-Tree拓扑在扩展性和性能方面存在局限，需要新的拓扑方案来满足下一代计算在科学、AI等领域的突破性需求。

Method: 以Orthogonal Fat-Tree和Random Folded Clos为基础，设计MRLS网络拓扑；采用多种非最小路由策略充分利用拓扑特性；通过互连网络模拟器进行详尽评估。

Result: MRLS网络在吞吐量和灵活性方面超越Fat-Tree；在10万端点的All2All集合通信中，相比Fat-Tree实现50%加速，相比Dragonfly网络实现100%加速。

Conclusion: MRLS拓扑是超大规模系统的理想解决方案，在保持低成本的同时提供了卓越的可扩展性和性能，为未来数据中心架构提供了新方向。

Abstract: Extreme-scale data centers are the backbone of next-generation computing, enabling breakthroughs in science, artificial intelligence, and global innovation through unprecedented processing power and scalability. This work examines leaf-spine network topologies that offer extreme scalability--connecting a vast number of endpoints--while delivering strong performance at low cost. It takes as a starting point two alternatives to the widely used Fat-Tree topology: the Orthogonal Fat-Tree and the Random Folded Clos. The resulting Multipass Random Leaf-Spine (MRLS) networks inherit their advantages and surpass Fat-Trees in both throughput and flexibility. To fully leverage the topological properties of these networks, various non-minimal routing strategies are considered. An exhaustive evaluation using an interconnection network simulator provides insight into the trade-offs and scalability of these topologies under realistic conditions, positioning them as a promising solution for extreme-scale systems. The MRLS achieves a 50% speedup against a Fat-Tree for an All2All collective comprising 100k endpoints, and 100% against Dragonfly networks for the same collective.

</details>


### [235] [GENESIS: Harnessing AI Agents for Autonomous 6G RAN Synthesis, Research, and Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27360)
*Tamerlan Aghayev,Maxime Elkael,Michele Polese,Minh Dat Nguyen,Gabriele Gemmi,Andrea Lacava,Ali Saeizadeh,Reshma Prasad,Paolo Testolina,Angelo Feraudo,Soumendra Nanda,Pedram Johari,Salvatore D'Oro,Tommaso Melodia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了GENESIS框架，一个用于蜂窝网络研发的智能体AI系统，通过代理、技能、钩子和知识层（SYNAPSE）的组合，将意图转化为经过OTA实验验证的解决方案，加速了蜂窝网络研发中原本需要数月手工工程迭代的六大结构化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝网络研发面临六大结构化流程瓶颈，每个迭代都需要数月的手工工程工作。尽管大语言模型在通用软件工程中已将研发时间从数天压缩到数分钟，但在无线接入网络（RAN）场景中存在严重缺陷：容易产生API幻觉和规格误读，导致互操作性失败；且过度依赖仿真导致算法迁移到真实硬件时失败。迫切需要一个能克服这些局限的AI框架来加速蜂窝网络研发。

Method: 提出了GENESIS智能体AI框架，基于三个可组合的原语构建：代理、技能和钩子，以及一个知识层SYNAPSE。该框架将意图（如规格条款、遥测异常或研究假设）转化为解决方案，并通过OTA实验进行验证。SYNAPSE知识层既作为基本事实的来源，又作为框架产生的每个制品的接收者，实现了跨运行的能力复合累积。

Result: GENESIS框架成功将各类意图转化为经过OTA实验验证的解决方案，克服了传统LLM在RAN场景中的API幻觉和规格误读问题。通过SYNAPSE知识层的持续积累机制，框架的能力能够在多次运行中不断复合增强，为蜂窝网络研发提供了有效的自动化解决方案。

Conclusion: GENESIS框架为蜂窝网络研发提供了一个可行的AI自动化解决方案，通过智能体架构和知识积累机制有效解决了传统LLM在RAN场景中的局限性，实现了研发流程的显著加速，为未来蜂窝网络标准的快速迭代和验证开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Cellular research and development (R&D) is throttled by six structural processes that each consume months of manual engineering work per iteration: (i) synthesizing new features from standards or research papers into production code; (ii) conformance and interoperability testing; (iii) hardening against field anomalies and diverse deployment environments; (iv) data-driven optimization of network functionalities; (v) discovering and prototyping novel waveforms, functionalities, and capabilities for future standards; and (vi) securing the stack against vulnerabilities. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have compressed comparable R&D work in general software engineering from days to minutes, their known pitfalls worsen on Radio Access Network (RAN) use cases: they hallucinate Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) and mis-read specifications, which kills interoperability of RAN components at the first mistake, and they heavily rely on simulations for designing algorithms, which is notorious for breaking when transferred to real hardware. To address these challenges, we present GENESIS, an agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework that converts intents (e.g., a specification clause, a telemetry anomaly, or a research hypothesis) into solutions validated with over-the-air experiments, fed back into a persistent knowledge base. GENESIS is built on three composable primitives (agents, skills, hooks) and a knowledge layer (SYNAPSE) that doubles as the source of ground truth and the recipient of every artifact the framework produces, making capabilities compound across runs.

</details>
